Explanatory Remarks:

1. Editorial notes or additions are enclosed in ().

Text:

prathamaḥ sargaḥ
nipīya yasya kṣitirakṣiṇaḥ kathāḥ
tathādriyante na budhāḥ sudhāmapi |
nalaḥ sitacchatritakīrtimaṇḍalaḥ
sa rāśirāsīnmahasāṃ mahojjvalaḥ ||1||
rasaiḥ kathā yasya sudhāvadhīraṇī
nalaḥ sa bhūjānirabhūhguṇādbhutaḥ |
suvarṇadaṇḍaikasitātapatrita
jvalatpratāpāvalikīrtimaṇḍalaḥ ||2||
pavitramatrātanute jagadyuge
smṛtā rasakṣālanayeva yatkathā |
kathaṃ na sā madgiramāvilāmapi
svasevinīmeva pavitrayiṣyati ||3||
adhītibodhācaraṇapracāraṇai
rdaśāścatasraḥ praṇayannupādhibhiḥ |
caturdaśatvaṃ kṛtavānkutaḥsvayaṃ
na vedmi vidyāsu caturdaśa svayam ||4||
amuṣya vidyā rasanāgranartakī
trayīva nītāṅgaguṇena vistaram |
agāhatāṣṭādaśatāṃ jigīṣayā
navadvayadvīpapṛthagjayaśriyām ||5||
digīśavṛndāṃśavibhūtirīśitā
diśāṃ sa kāmaprasarāvarodhinīm |
babhāra śāstrāṇi dṛśaṃ dvayādhikāṃ
nijatrinetrāvataratvabodhikām ||6||
padaiścaturbhiḥ sukṛte sthirīkṛte
kṛte'munā ke na tapaḥ prapedire |
bhuvaṃ yadekāṅghrikaniṣṭhayā spṛśa
ndadhāvadharmo'pi kṛśastapasvitām ||7||
yadasya yātrāsu baloddhataṃ rajaḥ
sphuratpratāpānalaghūmamañjima |
tadeva gatvā patitaṃ sudhāmbudhau
dadhāti paṅkībhavadaṅkatāṃ vidhau ||8||
sphuraddhanarnisvanataddhanāśuga
pragalbhavṛṣṭivyayitasya saṃgare |
nijasya tejaḥ śikhinaḥ paraśśatā
vitenuriṅgālamivāyaśaḥ pare ||9||
analpadagdhāripurānalojjvalaiḥ
nijapratāpairvalayaṃ jvaladbhuvaḥ |
pradakṣiṇīkṛtya jayāya sṛṣṭyā
rarāja nīrājanayā sa rājaghaḥ ||10||
nivāritāstena mahītale'khile
nirītibhāvaṃ gamite'tivṛṣṭayaḥ |
na tatyajurnūnamananyasaṃsrayāḥ
pratīpabhūpālamṛgīdṛśāṃ dṛśaḥ ||11||
sitāṃśuvarṇairvayati sma tadguṇaiḥ
mahāsivemnaḥ sahakṛtvarī bahum |
digaṅganāṅgāvaraṇaṃ raṇāṅgaṇe
yaśaḥ paṭaṃ tadbhaṭacāturīturī ||12||
pratipabhūpairiva kiṃ tato bhiyā
viruddhadharmairapi mettṛtojjhitā |
amitrajinmitrajidojasā sa yad
vicāradṛkcāradṛgapyavartata ||13||
tadojasastadyaśasaḥ sthitāvimau
vṛtheti citte kurute yadā yadā |
tano ti bhānoḥ pariveṣakaitavāt
tadā vidhiḥ kuṇḍalanāṃ vidhorapi ||14||
ayaṃ daridro bhaviteti vaidhasīṃ
vipiṃ lalāṭe'rthijanasya jāgratīm |
mṛṣāṃ na cakre'lpitakalpapādapaḥ
praṇīya dāridradaridratāṃ nalaḥ ||15||
vibhajya merurna yadarthisātkṛto
na sindhurutsargajalavyayayairmaruḥ |
amāni tattena nijāyaśoyugaṃ
dviphālabaddhāścikurāḥ śiraḥ sthitam ||16||
ajasramabhyāsamupeyuṣā samaṃ
mudaiva devaḥ kavinā budhena ca |
dadhau paṭīyānsamayaṃ nayannayaṃ
dineśvaraśrīrudayaṃ dine dine ||17||
adhovidhānātkamalapravālayoḥ
śiraḥ su dānādakhilakṣamābhujām |
puredamūrdhva bhavatīti vedhasā
padaṃ kimasyāṅkitamūrdhvarekhayā ||18||
jagajjayaṃ tena ca kośamakṣayaṃ
praṇītavān śaiśavaśeṣavānayam |
sakhā ratīśasya ṛturyathā vanaṃ
vapustathāliṅgadathāsya yauvanam ||19||
adhāri padmeṣu tadaṅghriṇā ghṛṇā
kva tacchayacchāyalavo'pi pallave |
tadāsyadāsye'pi gato'dhikāritāṃ
na śāradaḥ pārdhikaśarvarīśvaraḥ ||20||
kimasya lomnāṃ kapaṭena koṭibhiḥ
vidhirna lekhābhirajīgaṇahguṇān |
na romakūpaughamiṣājjagatkṛtā
kṛtāśca kiṃ dūṣaṇaśūnyabindavaḥ ||21||
amuṣya dorbhyāmaridurgaluṇṭhane
dhruvaṃ gṛhītārgaladīrghapīnatā |
uraḥ śriyā tatra ca gopurasphurat
kapāṭadurdharṣatiraḥ prasāritā ||22||
svakelileśasmitaninditenduno
nijāṃśadṛktarjitapadmasaṃpadaḥ |
ataddvayījitvarasundarāntare
na tanmukhasya pratimā carācare ||23||
saroruhaṃ tasya dṛśaiva nirjitaṃ
jitāḥ smitenaiva vidhorapi śriyaḥ |
kutaḥ paraṃ bhavyamaho mahīyasī
tadānanasyopamitau daridratā ||24||
svavālabhārasya taduttamāṅgajaiḥ
samaṃ camaryeva tulābhilāṣiṇaḥ |
anāgase śaṃsati bālacāpalaṃ
punaḥ punaḥ pucchavilolanacchalāt ||25||
mahībhṛtastasya ca manmathaśriyā
nijasya cittasya ca taṃ pratīcchayā |
dvidhā nṛpe tatra jagattrayībhuvāṃ
natabhruvāṃ manmathavibhramo'bhavat ||26||
nimīlanabhraṃśajuṣā dṛśā bhṛśaṃ
nipīya taṃ yastridaśīrbirarjitaḥ |
amūstamabhyāsabharaṃ vivṛṇvate
nimeṣaniḥ svairadhunāpi locanaiḥ ||27||
adastadākarṇi phalāḍhyajīvitaṃ
dṛśordvayaṃ nastadavīkṣi cāphalam |
ti sma cakṣuḥ śravasāṃ priyā nale
stuvanti nindanti hṛdātadātmanaḥ ||28||
vilokayantībhirajasrabhāvanābalādamuṃ netranimīlaneṣvapi |
alambhi martyābhiramuṣya darśane
na vighnaleśo'pi nimeṣanirmitiḥ ||29||
na kā niśi svapnagataṃ dadarśa
taṃ jagāda gotraskhalite ca kā na tam |
tadātmatādhyātadhavā rate ca kā
cakāra vā na svamanobhavodbhavam ||30||
śriyāsya yogyāhamiti svamīkṣituṃ
kare tamālokya surūpayā dhṛtaḥ |
vihāya bhaimīmapadarpayā kayā
na darpaṇaḥ śvāsamalīmasaḥ kṛtaḥ ||31||
yathohyamānaḥ khalu bhogabhojinā
prasahya vairocanijasya pattanam |
vidarbhajāyā madanastathā manonalāvaruddhaṃ vayasaiva veśitaḥ ||32||
nṛpe'nurūpe nijarūpasaṃpadāṃ
dideśa tasminbahuśaḥ śrutiṃ gate |
viśiṣya sā bhīmanarendranandanā
manobhavājñaikavaśaṃvadaṃ manaḥ ||33||
upāsanāmetya pituḥ sma rajyate
dine dine sāvasareṣu bandinām |
paṭhatsu teṣu pratibhūpatīnalaṃ
vinidraromājani śṛṇvatī nalam ||34||
kathā prasaṅgeṣu mithaḥ sakhīmukhāt
tṛṇe'pi tanvyā nalanāmani śrute |
drutaṃ vidhūyānyadabhūyatānayā
mudā tadākarṇanasajjakarṇayā ||35||
smarātparāsoranimeṣalocanādbibhemi tadbhannamudāhareti sā |
janena yūnaḥ stuvatā tadāspade
nidarśanaṃ naiṣadhamabhyaṣecayat ||36||
nalasya pṛṣṭā niṣadhāgatā guṇān
miṣeṇa dūtadvijabandicāraṇāḥ |
nipīya tatkīrtikathāmathānayā
cirāya tasthe vimanāyamānayā ||37||
prīyaṃ priyāṃ ca trijagajjayiśriyau
likhādhilīlāgṛhabhitti kāvapi |
iti sma sā kāruvareṇa lekhitaṃ
nalasya ca svasya ca sakhyamīkṣate ||38||
manorathena svapatīkṛtaṃ nalaṃ
niśi kva sā na svapatī sma paśyati |
adṛṣṭamapyarthamadṛṣṭavaibhavāt
karoti suptirjanadarśanātithim ||39||
nimīlitādakṣiyugācca nidrayā
hṛdo'pi bāhyendriyamaunamudritāt |
adarśi saṃgopya kadāpyavīkṣito
rahasyamasyāḥ sa mahanmahīpatiḥ ||40||
aho ahobhirmahimā himāgame'–
pyabhiprapede prati tāṃ smarārditām |
tapartupūrtāvapi medasāṃ bharā
vibhāvarībhirbibharāṃbabhūvire ||41||
svakāntikīrtivrajamauktikasrajaḥ
śrayantamantarghaṭanāguṇaśriyam |
kadācidasyā yuvadhairyalopinaṃ
nalo'pi lokādaśṛṇohguṇotkaram ||42||
tameva labdhvāvasaraṃ tataḥ smaraḥ
śarīraśobhājayajātamatsaraḥ |
amoghaśaktyā nijayena mūrtayā
tayā vinirjetumiyeṣa naiṣadham ||43||
akāri tena śravaṇātithirguṇaḥ
kṣamābhujā bhīmanṛpātmajālayaḥ |
taduccadhairyavyayasaṃhiteṣuṇā
smareṇa ca svātmaśarāsanāśrayaḥ ||44||
amuṣya dhīrasya jayāya sāhasī
tadā khalu jyāṃ viśikhaiḥ sanāthayan |
nimajjayāmāsa yaśāṃsi saṃśaye
smarastrilokīvijayārjitānyayi ||45||
anena bhaimīṃ ghaṭayiṣyatastathā
vidheravandhyecchatayā vyalāsi tat |
abhedi tattādṛganaṅgamārgaṇairyadasya pauṣpairapi dhaiyakañcukam ||46||
kimanyadadyāpi yadastratāpitaḥ
pitāmaho vārijamāśrayatyaho |
smaraṃ tanucchāyatayā tamātmanaḥ
śaśāka śaṅke sa na laṅghituṃ nalaḥ ||47||
urobhuvā kumbhayugena jṛmbhitaṃ
navopahāreṇa vayaḥ kṛtena kim |
trapāsaridṛrgamapi pratīrya sā
nalasya tanvī hṛdayaṃ viveśa yat ||48||
apahnuvānasya janāya yannijā
madhīratāmasya kṛtaṃ manobhuvā |
abodhi tajjāgaraduḥkhasākṣiṇī
niśā ca śayyā ca śaśāṅkakomalā ||49||
smaropatapto'pi bhṛśaṃ na sa prabhurvidarbharājaṃ tanayāmayācata |
tyajantyasūñśarma ca māninovaraṃ
tyajanti na tvekamayācitavratam ||50||
mṛṣāviṣādābhinayādayaṃ kvacijjugopa niḥśvāsatatiṃ viyogajām |
vilepanasyādhikacandrabhāgatā
vibhāvanāccāpalalāpa pāṇḍutām ||51||
śaśāka nihnotumayena tatpriyā
mayaṃ babhāṣe yadalīkavīkṣitām |
samāja evālapitāsu vaiṇikairmumūrccha yatpañcamamūrcchanāsu ca ||52||
avāpa sāpatrapatāṃ sa bhūpatirjitendriyāṇāṃ dhuri kīrtitasthitiḥ |
asaṃvare śaṃbaravairivikrame
krameṇa tatra sphuṭatāmupeyuṣi ||53||
alaṃ nalaṃ roddumamī kilābhavanguṇā vivekapramukhā na cāpalam |
smaraḥ sa ratyāmaniruddhameva yatsṛjatyayaṃ sarganisarga īdṛśaḥ ||54||
anaṅgacihnaṃ sa vinā śaśāka no
yadāsituṃ saṃsadi yatnavānapi |
kṣaṇaṃ tadārāmavihārakaitavān
niṣevituṃ deśamiyeṣa nirjanam ||55||
atha śriyā bhartsitamatsyalācchanaḥ
samaṃ vayasyaiḥ svarahasyavedibhiḥ |
puropakaṇṭhopavanaṃ kilekṣitā
dideśa yānāya nideśakāriṇaḥ ||56||
amī tatastasya vibhūṣitaṃ sitaṃ
jave'pi māne'pi ca pauruṣādhikam |
upāharannaśvamajasracañcalaiḥ
khurāñcalaiḥ kṣoditamandurodaram ||57||
athāntareṇāvaṭugāminādhvanā
niśīthinīnāthamahaḥ sahodaraiḥ |
nigālagāddevamaṇerivotthitairvirājitaṃ kesarakeśaraśmibhiḥ ||58||
ajasrabhūmītaṭakuṭṭanotthitairupāsyamānaṃ caraṇeṣu reṇubhiḥ |
rayaprakarṣādhyayanārthamāgatairjanasya cetobhirivāṇimāṅkitaiḥ ||59||
calācalaprothatayā mahībhṛte
svavegadarpāniva vaktumutsukam |
alaṃ girā veda kilāyamāśayaṃ
svayaṃ hayasyeti ca maunamāsthitam ||60||
mahārathasyādhvani cakravartinaḥ
parānapekṣodvahanādyaśaḥ sitam |
radāvadātāṃśumiṣādanīdṛśāṃ
hasantamantarbalamarvatāṃ raveḥ ||61||
sitatviṣaścañcalatāmupeyuṣo
miṣeṇa pucchasya ca kesarasya ca |
sphuṭaṃ calaccāmarayugmacihnanairanihnuvānaṃ nijavājirājatām ||62||
api dvijihvābhyavahārapauruṣe
mukhānuṣaktāyatavalguvalgayā |
upeyivāṃsaṃ pratimallatāṃ rayasmaye jitasya prasabhaṃ garutmataḥ ||63||
sa sindhujaṃ śītamahaḥ sahodaraṃ
harantamuccaiḥ śravasaḥ śriyaṃ hayam |
jitākhilakṣmābhṛdanalpalocanastamāruroha kṣitipākaśāsanaḥ ||64||
nijā mayūkhā iva tīkṣṇadīdhitiṃ
sphuṭāravindāṅkitapāṇipaṅkajam |
tamaśvavārā javanāśvayāyinaṃ
prakāśarūpā manujeśamanvayuḥ ||65||
calannalaṃkṛtya mahārayaṃ hayaṃ
svavāhavāhocitaveṣapeśalaḥ |
pramodaniḥ spandatarākṣipakṣmabhirvyaloki lokairnagarālayairnalaḥ ||66||
kṣaṇādathaiṣa kṣaṇadāpatiprabhaḥ
prabhañjanādhyeyajavena vājinā |
sahaiva tābhirjanadṛṣṭivṛṣṭibhirbahiḥ puro'bhūtpuruhūtapauruṣaḥ ||67||
tataḥ pratīccha prahareti bhāṣiṇī
parasparollāsitaśalyapallave |
mṛṣāmṛghaṃ sādibale kutūhalānnalasya nāsīragate vitenatuḥ ||68||
prayātumasmākamiyaṃ kiyatpadaṃ
dharā tadambhodhirapi sthalāyatām |
itīva vāhairnijavegadarpitaiḥ
payodhirodhakṣamamuddhataṃ rajaḥ ||69||
hareryadakrāmi padaikakena khaṃ
padaiścaturbhiḥ kramaṇe'pi tasya naḥ |
trapā harīṇāmiti namritānanairnyavarti tairardhanabhaḥ kṛtakramaiḥ ||70||
camūcarāstasya nṛpasya sādino
jinoktiṣu śrāddhatayeva saindhavāḥ |
vihāradeśaṃ tamavāpya maṇḍalīmakārayan bhūrituraṃgamānapi ||71||
dviṣadbhirevāsya vilaṅghitā diśo
yaśobhirevābdhirakāri goṣpadam |
itīva dhārāmavadhīrya maṇḍalī
kriyāśriyā'maṇḍi turaṃgamaiḥ sthalī ||72||
acīkaraccāru hayena yā bhramīrnijātapatrasya talasthale nalaḥ |
marutkimadyāpi na tāsu śikṣate
vitatya vātyāmayacakraṅkramān ||73||
viveśa gatvā sa vilāsakānanaṃ
tataḥ kṣaṇātkṣoṇipatirdhṛtīcchayā |
pravālarāgacchuritaṃ suṣupsayā
harirdhanacchāyamivārṇasāṃ nidhim ||74||
vanāntaparyantamupetya saspṛhaṃ
krameṇa tasminnavatīrṇadṛkpathe |
nyavarti dṛṣṭiprakaraiḥ puraukasāmanuvrajadbandhusamājabandhubhiḥ ||75||
tataḥ prasūne ca phale ca mañjule
sa saṃmukhasthāṅgulinā janādhipaḥ |
nivedyamānaṃ vanapālapāṇinā
vyālokayatkānanakāmanīyakam ||76||
phalāni puṣpāṇi ca pallave kare
vayotipātodgatavātavepite |
sthitaiḥ samādāya maharṣivārdhakād
vane tadātithyamaśikṣi śākhibhiḥ ||77||
vinidrapatrāligatālikaitavānmṛgāṅkacūḍāmaṇivarjanārjitam |
dadhānamāśāsu cariṣṇu duryaśaḥ
sa kautukī tatra dadarśa ketakam ||78||
viyogabhājāṃ hṛdi kaṇṭakaiḥ kaṭurnidhīyase karṇiśaraḥ smareṇa yat |
tato durākarṣatayā tadantakṛdvigīyase manmathadehadāhinā ||79||
tvadagrasūcīsacivena kāminormanobhavaḥ sīvyati duryaśaḥ paṭau |
sphuṭaṃ sa patraiḥ karapatramūrtibhi
rviyogihṛddāruṇi dāruṇāyate ||80||
dhanurmadhusvinnakaro'pi bhīmajāparaṃ parāgaistava dhūlihastayan |
prasūnadhanvā śarasātkaroti māmiti krudhākruśyata tena kaitakam ||81||
vidarbhasubhrūstanatuṅgatāptaye
ghaṭānivāpaśyadalaṃ tapasyataḥ |
phalāni dhūmasya dhayānadhomukhānsa dāḍime dohadadhūpini drume ||82||
viyoginīmaikṣata dāḍimīmasau
priyasmṛteḥ spaṣṭamudītakaṇṭakām |
phalastanasthānavidīrṇarāgihṛdviśacchukāsyasmarakiṃśukāśugām ||83||
smarārdhacandreṣunibhe kraśīyasāṃ
sphuṭaṃ palāśe'dhvajuṣāṃ palāśanāt |
sa vṛntamālokata khaṇḍamanvitaṃ
viyogihṛtkhaṇḍini kālakhaṇḍajam ||84||
navā latā gandhavahena cumbitā
karambitāṅgī makarandaśīkaraiḥ |
dṛśā nṛpeṇa smitaśobhikuñcalā
darādarābhyāṃ darakampinī pape ||85||
vicinvatīḥ pānthapataṅgahiṃsanairapuṇyakarmāṇyalikajjalacchalāt |
vyalokayaccampakakorakāvalīḥ
sa śambarārerbalidīpikā iva ||86||
amanyatāsau kusumeṣugarbhagaṃ
parāgamandhaṃkaraṇaṃ viyoginām |
smareṇa mukteṣu purā purāraye
tadaṅgabhasmeva śareṣu saṃgatam ||87||
pikādvane śṛṇvati bhṛṅgahuṃ kṛtairdaśāmudañcatkaruṇe viyoginām |
anāsthayā sūnakaraprasāriṇīṃ
dadarśa dūnaḥ sthalapadminīṃ nalaḥ ||88||
rasālasālaḥ samadṛśyatāmunā
sphuraddvirephāravaroṣahuṃkṛtiḥ |
samīralolairmukulairviyogine
janāya ditsanniva tarjanābhiyam ||89||
dine dine tvaṃ tanuredhi re'dhikaṃ
punaḥ punarmūrccha ca tāpamṛccha ca |
itīva pānthāñśapataḥ pikāndvijānsakhedamaikṣiṣṭa sa lohitekṣaṇān ||90||
alisrajā kuṅmalamuccaśekharaṃ
nipīya cāmpeyamadhīrayā dhiyā |
sa dhūmaketuṃ viṣade viyogināmudītamātaṅkitavānaśaṅkata ||91||
galatparāgaṃ bhramibhahgibhiḥ patatprasaktabhṛṅgāvali nāgakesaram |
sa māranārācanigharṣaṇaskhalajjvalatkaṇaṃ śāṇamiva vyalokayat ||92||
tadaṅgamuddiśya sugandhi pātukāḥ
śilīmukhālīḥ kusumāhuṇaspṛśaḥ |
svacāpadurnirgatamārgaṇabhramātsmaraḥ svanantīravalokya lajjitaḥ ||93||
marullalatpallavakaṇṭakaiḥ kṣataṃ
samucchalaccandanasārasaurabham |
sa vāranārīkucasaṃcitopamaṃ
dadarśa mālūraphalaṃ pacelimam ||94||
yuvadvayīcittanimajjanocitaprasūnaśūnyetaragarbhagahvaram |
smareṣudhīkṛtya dhiyā bhayāndhasā
sa pāṭalāyāḥ stabakaṃ prakampitaḥ ||95||
munidrumaḥ korakitaḥ śitidyutirvane'manā'manyata siṃhikāsutaḥ |
tamisnapakṣatruṭikūṭabhakṣitaṃ
kalākalāpaṃ kila vaidhavaṃ vaman ||96||
purā haṭhākṣiptatuṣārapāṇḍuracchadāvṛtervīrudhi baddhavibhramāḥ |
milatrimīlaṃ sasṛjurvilokitā
nabhasvatastaṃ kusumeṣu kelayaḥ ||97||
gatā yadutsaṅgatale viśālatāṃ
drumāḥ śirobhiḥ phalagauraveṇa tām |
kathaṃ na dhātrīmati mātranāmitaiḥ
sa vandamānānabhinandati sma tān ||98||
nṛpāya tasmai himitaṃ vanānilaiḥ
sudhīkṛtaṃ puṣparasairaharmahaḥ |
vinirmitaṃ ketakareṇubhiḥ sitaṃ
viyogine'datta na kaumadīmudaḥ ||99||
ayogabhājo'pi nṛpasya paśyatā
tadeva sākṣādamṛtāṃśumānanam |
pikena roṣāruṇacakṣuṣā muhuḥ
kuhūrutāhūyata candravairiṇī ||100||
aśokamarthānvitanāmatāśayāgatān śaraṇyaṃ gṛhaśocino'dvagān |
amanyatāvantamivaiṣa pallavaiḥ
pratīṣṭakāmajvaladastrajālakam ||101||
vilāsa vāpītaṭavīcivādanātpikāligīteḥ śikhilāsyalāghavāt |
vane'pi tauryatrikamārarādha taṃ
kva bhogamāpnoti na bhāgyabhāgjanaḥ ||102||
tadarthamadhyāpya janena tadvane
śukā vimuktāḥ paṭavastamastuvan |
svarāmṛtenopajaguśca sārikāstathaiva tatpauruṣagāyanīkṛtāḥ ||103||
itīṣṭagandhāḍhyamaṭannasau vanaṃ
pikopagīto'pi śukastuto'pi ca |
avindatāmodabharaṃ bahiścaraṃ
vidarbhasubhruviraheṇa nāntaram ||104||
kareṇa mīnaṃ nijaketanaṃ dadhaddrumālavālāmbuniveśaśaṅkayā |
vyatarki sarvartughane ghane madhuṃ
sa mitramatrānusaranniva smaraḥ ||105||
latābalālāsyakalāgurustaruprasūnagandhotkarapaśyatoharaḥ |
asevatāmuṃ madhugandhavāriṇi
praṇītalīlāplavano vanānilaḥ ||106||
atha svamādāya bhayena manthanācciratnādhikamuccitaṃ cirāt |
nilīya tasminniva sannapāṃnidhirvane taḍāko dadṛśe'vanībhujā ||107||
payonilīnābhramukāmukāvalīradānanantoragapucchasucchavīn |
jalārdharuddhasya taṭāntabhūmido
mṛṇālajālasya miṣādbabhāra yaḥ ||108||
taṭāntaviśrāntaturaṃgamacchaṭāsphuṭānuvimbodayacumbanena yaḥ |
babhau caladvīcikaśāntaśātanaiḥ
sahasramuccaiḥ śravasāmivāśrayan ||109||
sitāmbujānāṃ nivahasya yaśchalādbabhāvaliśyāmalitodaraśriyām |
tamaḥ samacchāyakalaṅkasaṃkulaṃ
kulaṃ sudhāṃśorbahalaṃ vahanvahu ||110||
rathāṅgabhājā kamalānuṣaṅgiṇā
śilīmukhastomasakhena śārṅgiṇā |
sarojinīstambakadambakaitavānmṛṇālaśeṣāhibhuvānvayāyi yaḥ ||111||
taraṅgiṇīraṅkajuṣaḥ svavallabhāstaraṅgarekhā bibharāṃbabhūva yaḥ |
darodgataiḥ kokanadaughakorakairdhṛtapravālāṅkurasaṃcayaśca yaḥ ||112||
mahīyasaḥ paṅkajamaṇḍalasya yaśchalena gaurasya ca mecakasya ca |
nalena mene salile nilīnayostviṣaṃ vimuñcanvidhukālakūṭayoḥ ||113||
calīkṛtā yatra taraṅgariṅgaṇairabālaśaivālalatāparamparāḥ |
dhruvaṃ dadhurvāḍavahavyavāḍavasthitiprarohattamabhūmadhūmatām ||114||
prakāmamādityamavāpya kaṇṭakaiḥ
karambitāmodabharaṃ vivṛṇvatī |
dhṛtasphuṭaśrīgṛhavigrahā divā
sarojinī yatprabhavāpsarāyitā ||115||
yadambupūrapratibimbitāyatirmaruttaraṅgaistaralastaṭadrumaḥ |
nimajjya mainākamahībhṛtaḥ satastatāna pakṣāndhuvataḥ sapakṣatām ||116||
payodhilakṣmīmuṣi kelipalvale
riraṃsuhaṃsīkalanādasādaram |
sa tatra citraṃ vicarantamantike
hiraṇmayaṃ haṃsamabodhi naiṣadhaḥ ||117||
priyāsu bālāsu ratakṣamāsu ca
dvipatritaṃ pallavitaṃ ca bibhratam |
smarārjitaṃ rāgamahīruhāṅkuraṃ
miṣeṇa cañcvoścaraṇadvayasya ca ||118||
mahīmahendrastamavekṣya sa kṣaṇaṃ
śakuntamekāntamanovinonadim |
priyāviyogādvidhuro'pi nirbharaṃ
kutūhalākrāntamanā manāgabhūt ||119||
avaśyabhavyeṣvanavagrahagrahā
yayā diśā dhāvati vedhasaḥ spṛhā |
tṛṇena vātyeva tayānugamyate
janasya cittena bhṛśāvaśātmanā ||120||
athāvalambya kṣaṇamekapādikāṃ
tadā nidadrāvupapalvalaṃ khagaḥ |
sa tiryagāvarjitakaṃdharaḥ śiraḥ
pidhāya pakṣeṇa rati klamālasaḥ ||121||
sanālamātmānananirjitaprabhaṃ
hriyā nataṃ kāñcanamambujanma kim |
abuddha taṃ vidrumadaṇḍamaṇḍitaṃ
sa pīta mambhaḥ prabhucāmaraṃ nu kim ||122||
kṛtāvarohasya hayādupānahau
tataḥ pade rejaturasya bibhratī |
tayoḥ pravālairvanayostathāmbujairniyoddhukāme kimu baddhavarmaṇī ||123||
vidhāya mūrtiṃ kapaṭena vāmanīṃ
svayaṃ balidhvaṃsiviḍambinīmayam |
upetapārśvaścaraṇena mauninā
nṛpaḥ pataṅgaṃ samadhatta pāṇinā ||124||
tadāttamātmānamavetya saṃbhramātpunaḥ punaḥ prāyasadutplavāya saḥ |
gato virutyoḍḍayane nirāśatāṃ
karau niroddhurdaśati sma kevalam ||125||
sasaṃbhramotpātipatatkulākulaṃ
saraḥ prapadyotkatayā'nukampratām |
tamūrmilolaiḥ patagagrahānnṛpaṃ
nyavārayadvāriruhaiḥ karairiva ||126||
patatriṇā tadrucireṇa vañcitaṃ
śriyaḥ prayāntyāḥ pravihāya palvalam |
calatpadāmbhoruhanūpuropamā
cukūja kūle kalahaṃsamaṇḍalī ||127||
na vāsayogyā vasudheyamīdṛśastvamaṅga ! yasyāḥ patirujjhitasthitiḥ |
iti prahāya kṣitimāśritā nabhaḥ
khagāstamācukuśurāravaiḥ khalu ||128||
na jātarūpacchadajātarūpatā
dvijasya dṛṣṭeyamiti stuvanmuhuḥ |
avādi tenātha sa mānasaukasā
janādhināthaḥ karapañjaraspṛśā ||129||
dhigastu tṛṣṇātaralaṃ bhavanmanaḥ
samīkṣya pakṣānmama hemajanmanaḥ |
tavārṇavasyeva tuṣāraśīkarairbhavedamībhiḥ kamalodayaḥ kiyān ||130||
na kevalaṃ prāṇivadho vadho mama
tvadīkṣaṇādviśvasitāntarātmanaḥ |
vigarhitaṃ dharmadhanairnibarhaṇaṃ
viśiṣya viśvāsajuṣāṃ dviṣāmapi ||131||
pade pade santi bhaṭā raṇodbhaṭā
na teṣu hiṃsārasa eṣa pūryate |
dhigīdṛśaṃ te nṛpate ! kuvikramaṃ
kṛpāśraye yaḥ kṛpaṇe patatriṇi ||132||
phalena mūlena ca vāribhūruhāṃ
munerivetthaṃ mama yasya vṛttayaḥ |
tvayādya tasminnapi daṇḍadhāriṇā
kathaṃ na patyā dharaṇī hṛṇīyate ||133||
itīdṛśaistaṃ viracayya vāṅmayaiḥ
sacitravailakṣyakṛpaṃ nṛpaṃ khagaḥ |
dayāsamudre sa tadāśaye'tithīcakāra kāruṇyarasāpagā giraḥ ||134||
madekaputrā jananī jarāturā
navaprasūtirvaraṭā tapasvinī |
gatistayoreṣa janastamardayannaho vidhe ! tvāṃ karuṇā ruṇaddhi na ||135||
muhūrtamātraṃ bhavanindayā dayāsakhāḥ sakhāyaḥ sravadaśravo mama |
nivṛttimeṣyanti paraṃ duruttarastvayaiva mātaḥ ! sutaśokasāgaraḥ ||136||
madarthasaṃdeśamṛṇālamantharaḥ
priyaḥ kiyaddūra iti tvayodite |
vilokayantyā rudato'tha pakṣiṇaḥ
priye ! sa kīdṛgbhavitā tava kṣaṇaḥ ||137||
kathaṃ vidhātarmayi pāṇipaṅkajāttava priyāśaityamṛdutvaśilpinaḥ |
viyokṣyase vallabhayeti nirgatā
lipirlalāṭantapaniṣṭhurākṣarā ||138||
ayi !svayūthyairaśanikṣatopamaṃ
mamādya vṛttāntamimaṃ batoditā |
mukhāni lolākṣi ! diśāmasaṃśayaṃ
daśāpi śūnyāni vilokayiṣyasi ||139||
mamaiva śokena vidīrṇavakṣasā
tvayā vicitrāṅgi ! vipadyate yadi |
tadāsmi daivena hato'pi hā hataḥ
sphuṭaṃ yataste śiśavaḥ parāsavaḥ ||140||
tavāpi hā hā virahātkṣudhākulāḥ
kulāyakūleṣu viluṭhya teṣu te |
cireṇa labdhā bahubhirmanorathairgatāḥ kṣaṇenāsphuṭitekṣaṇā mama ||141||
sutāḥ ! kamāhūya cirāya cuṃkṛtairvidhāya kamprāṇi mukhāni kaṃ prati |
kathāsu śiṣyadhvamiti pramīlya saḥ
strutasya sekādbubudhe nṛpāśruṇaḥ ||142||
itthamamuṃ vilapantamamuñcaddīnadayālutayāvanipālaḥ |
rupamadarśi dhṛto'si yadarthaṃ
gaccha yathecchamathetyabhidhāya ||143||
ānandajāśrubhiranusriyamāṇamārgānprākśokanirgamitanetrapayaḥ pravāhān |
cakre sa cakranibhacaṅkramaṇacchalena
nīrājanāṃ janayatāṃ nijabāndhavānām ||144||
śrīharṣaṃ kavirājarājimukuṭālaṃkārahīraḥ sutaṃ
śrīhīraḥ suṣuve jitendriyacayaṃ māmalladevī ca yam |
taccintāmaṇimantracintanaphale śṛṅgārabhaṅgyā mahākāvye cāruṇi naiṣadhīyacarite sargo'yamādirgataḥ ||145||

dvitīyaḥ sargaḥ
adhigatya jagatyadhīśvarāt
atha muktiṃ puruṣottamāttataḥ |
vacasāmapi gocaro na
yaḥ sa tāmānandamavindata dvijaḥ ||1||
adhunīta khagaḥ sa naikadhā
tanumutphullatanūruhīkṛtām |
karayantraṇadanturāntare
vyalikhaccañcupuṭena pakṣatī ||2||
ayamekatamena pakṣateradhim
adhyordhvagajaṅghamaṅghriṇā |
skhalanakṣaṇa eva śiśriye
drutakaṇḍūyitamaulirālayam ||3||
sa garudvanadurgadurgrahān
kaṭu kīṭāndaśataḥ sataḥ kvacit |
nunude tanukaṇḍu paṇḍitaḥ
paṭucañcūpuṭakoṭikuṭṭanaiḥ ||4||
ayametya taḍāganīḍajairlaghu paryavriyatātha śaṅkitaiḥ |
udaḍīyata vaikṛtātkaragrahajādasya vikasvarasvaraiḥ ||5||
vahato bahuśaivalakṣmatāṃ
dhṛtarudrākṣamadhuvrataṃ khagaḥ |
sa nīlasya yayau karaṃ
punaḥ sarasaḥ kokanadabhramādiva ||6||
patagaścirakālalālanāt
ativiśrambhamavāpito nu saḥ ||7||
nṛpamānasamiṣṭamānasaḥ
sa nimajjatkutukāmṛtormiṣu |
avalambitakarṇaśaṣkulīkalasīkaṃ racayannavocata ||8||
mṛgayā na vigīyate
nṛpairapi dharmāgamamarmapāragaiḥ |
smarasundara ! māṃ yadat
yajastava dharmaḥ sa dayodayojjvalaḥ ||9||
abalasvakulāśinojhaṣān
nijanīḍadrumapīḍinaḥ khagān |
anavadyatṛṇārdino mṛgān
mṛgayādhāya na bhūbhujāṃ ghnatām ||10||
yadavādiṣamapriyaṃ tava
priyamādhāya nunutsurasmi tat |
kṛtamātapasaṃjvaraṃ taroḥ
abhivṛṣyāmṛtamaṃśumāniva ||11||
upanamramayācitaṃ hitaṃ
parihartu na tavāpi sāṃpratam |
karakalpajanāntarādvidheḥ
śucitaḥ prāpi sa hi pratigrahaḥ ||12||
patagena mayā jagatpateḥ
upakṛtyaiva tava kiṃ prabhūyate |
iti vedmi na tu tyajanti
māṃ tadapi pratyupakartumartayaḥ ||13||
acirādupakarturācaretatha vātmaupayikīmupakriyām |
pṛthuritthamathāṇurastu sā
na viśeṣe viduṣāmiha grahaḥ ||14||
bhavitā na vicāracāru
cettadapi śravyamidaṃ madīritam |
khagavāgiyamityato'pi
kiṃ na mudaṃ dhāsyati kīragīriva ||15||
sa jayatyarisārthasārthakīkṛtanāmā kila bhīmabhūpatiḥ |
yamavāpya vidarbhabhūḥ prabhuṃ
hasati dyāmapi śakrabhartṛkām ||16||
damanādamanākpraseduṣastanayāṃ tathyagirastapodhanāt |
varamāpa sa diṣṭaviṣṭapatritayānanyasadṛgguṇodayām ||17||
bhuvanatrayasubhruvāmasau
damayantī kamanīyatāmadam |
udiyāya yatastanuśriyā
damayantīti tato'bhidhāṃ dadhau ||18||
śriyameva paraṃ dharādhipāhguṇasindhoruditāmavehi tām |
vyavadhāvapi vā vidhoḥ kalāṃ
mṛḍacūḍānilayāṃ na veda kaḥ ||19||
cikuraprakarā jayanti te
viduṣī mūrdhani sābibharti yān |
paśunāpyapuraskṛtena tattulanāmicchatu cāmareṇa kaḥ ||20||
svadṛśorjanayanti sāntvanāṃ
khurakaṇḍūyanakaitavānmṛgāḥ |
jitayorudayatpramīlayoḥ
tadakharvekṣaṇaśobhayā bhayāt ||21||
api lokayugaṃ dṛśāvapi
śrutadṛṣṭā ramaṇīguṇā api |
śrutigāmitayā damasvasuḥ
vyatibhāte sutarāṃ dharāpate ! ||22||
nalinaṃ malinaṃ vivṛṇvatī
pṛṣatīmaspṛśatī tadīkṣaṇe |
api khañjanamañjanāñcite
vidadhāte rucigarvadurvidham ||23||
adharaṃ kila bimbanāmakaṃ
phalamasmāditi bhavyamanvayam |
labhate'dharabimbamityadaḥ
padamasyā radanacchadaṃ vadat ||24||
hṛtasāramivendumaṇḍalaṃ
damayantīvadanāya vedhasā |
kṛtamadhyabilaṃ vilokyate
dhṛtagambhīrakhanīkhanīlima ||25||
dhṛtalāñchanagomayāñcanaṃ
vidhumālenapāṇḍuraṃ vidhiḥ |
bhramayatyuciṭaṃ vidarbhajānananīrājanavardhamānakam ||26||
suṣamāviṣaye parīkṣaṇe
nikhilaṃ padmamabhāji tanmukhāt |
adhunāpi na maṅgalakṣaṇaṃ
salilonmajjanamujjhati sphuṭam ||27||
dhanuṣī ratipañcabāṇayoḥ
udite viśvajayāya tadbhruvau |
nalike na taduccanāsike
tvayi nālīkavimuktikāmayoḥ ||28||
sadṛśī tava śūra ! sā paraṃ
jaladurgasthamṛṇālajidbhujā |
api mitrajuṣāṃ saroruhāṃ
gṛhayāluḥ karalīlayā śriyaḥ ||29||
vayasī śiśutātaduttare
sudṛśi svābhividhiṃ vidhitsunī |
vidhināpi na romarekhayā
kṛtasīmnī pravibhajya rajyataḥ ||30||
api tadvapuṣi prasapratoḥ
gamite kāntijharairagādhatām |
smarayauvanayoḥ khalu dvayoḥ
plavakumbhau bhavataḥ kucāvumau ||31||
kalase nijahetudaṇḍajaḥ
kimu cakrabhramakāritāguṇaḥ |
sa taduccakucau bhavanprabhājharacakrabhramamātanoti yat ||32||
bhajate khalu ṣaṇmukhaṃ śikhī
cikurairnirmitabarhagarhaṇaḥ |
api jambharipuṃ damasvasurjitakumbhaḥ kucaśobhayebharāṭ ||33||
udaraṃ natamadhyapṛṣṭhatāsphuṭadaṅguṣṭhapadena muṣṭinā |
caturaṅgulamadhyanirgatatribalibhrāji kṛtaṃ damasvasuḥ ||34||
udaraṃ parimāti muṣṭinā
kutukī ko'pi damasvasuḥ kimu |
dhṛtataccaturaṅgulīva yadvalibhirmāti sahemakāñcibhiḥ ||35||
pṛthuvartulatannitambakṛnmihirasyandanaśilpaśikṣayā |
vidhirekakacakracāriṇaṃ
kimu nirmitsati mānmathaṃ ratham ||36||
tarumūruyugena sundarī
kimu rambhāṃ pariṇāhinā param |
taruṇīmapi jiṣṇureva
tāṃ dhanadāpatyatapaḥ phalastanīm ||37||
jalaje ravisevayeva ye
padametatpadatāmavāpatuḥ |
dhruvametya rutaḥ sahaṃsakīkurutaste vidhipatradaṃpatī ||38||
śritapuṇyasaraḥ saritkathaṃ
na samādhi kṣapitākhilakṣapam |
jalajaṃ gatimetu mañjulāṃ
damayantīpadanāmni janmani ||39||
sarasīḥ pariśīlituṃ mayā
gamikarmīkṛtanaikanīvṛtā |
atithitvamanāyi sā dṛśoḥ
sadasatsaṃśayagocarodarī ||40||
avadhṛtya divo'pi yauvatairnasahādhītavatīmimāmaham |
katamastu vidhāturāśaye
patirasyā vasatītyacintayam ||41||
anurūpamimaṃ nirūpayannatha sarveṣvapi pūrvapakṣatām |
yuvasu vyapanetumakṣamaḥ
tvayi siddhāntadhiyaṃ nyaveśayam ||42||
anayā tava rūpasīmayā
kṛtasaṃskāravibodhanasya me |
ciramapyavalokitādya sā
smṛtimārūḍhavatī śucismitā ||43||
tvayi vīra ! virājate paraṃ
damayantīkilakiñcitaṃ kila |
taruṇīstana eva dīpyate
maṇihārāvalirāmaṇīyakam ||44||
tava rūpamidaṃ tayā vinā
viphalaṃ puṣpamivāvakeśinaḥ |
iyamuddhadhanā vṛthāvanī
svavanī saṃpravadatpikāpi kā ||45||
anayā surakāmyamānayā
saha yogaḥ sulabhastu na tvayā |
dhanasaṃvṛtayāmbudāgame
kumudeneva niśākaratviṣā ||46||
tadahaṃ vidadhe tathā tathā
damayantyāḥ savidhe tava stavam |
hṛdaye nihitastayā bhavānapi nendreṇa yathāpanīyate ||47||
tava saṃmatimeva kevalāmadhigantuṃ dhigidaṃ niveditam |
bruvate hi phalena sādhavo
na tu kaṇṭhena nijopayogitām ||48||
tadidaṃ viśadaṃ vacomṛtaṃ
paripīyābhyuditaṃ dvijādhipāt |
atitṛptatayā vinirmame
sa tadudgāramiva smitaṃ sitam ||49||
parimṛjya bhujāgrajanmanā
patagaṃ kokanadena naiṣadhaḥ |
mṛdu tasya mude'kiradgiraḥ
priyavādāmṛtakūpakaṇṭhajāḥ ||50||
na tulāviṣaye tavākṛtirna vacovartmani te suśīlatā |
tvadudāharaṇākṛtau guṇā
iti samāmudrakasāramudraṇā ||51||
na suvarṇamayī tanuḥ paraṃ
nanu kiṃ vāgapi tāvakī tathā |
na paraṃ pathi pakṣapātitānavalambe kimu mādṛśe'pi sā ||52||
bhṛśatāpabhṛtā mayā
bhavānmarudāsādi tuṣārasāravān |
dhanināmitaraḥ satāṃ punarguṇavatsaṃnidhireva sannidhiḥ ||53||
śataśaḥ śrutimāgataiva sā
trijaganmohamahauṣadhirmama |
amunā tava śaṃsitena tu
svadṛśaivādhigatāmavaimi tām ||54||
akhilaṃ viduṣāmanāvilaṃ
suhṛdā ca svahṛdā ca paśyatām |
savidhe'pi nasūkṣmasākṣiṇī
vadanālaṃkṛtimātramakṣiṇī ||55||
amitaṃ madhu tatkathā mama
śravaṇaprāghuṇakīkṛtā janaiḥ |
madanānalabodhane bhavetkhaga ! dhāyyā dhigadhairyadhāriṇaḥ ||56||
viṣamo malayāhimaṇḍalīviṣaphūtkāramayo mayohitaḥ |
kharga ! kālakalatradigbhavaḥ pavanastadvirahānalaidhasā ||57||
pratimāsamasau niśāpatiḥ
khaga ! saṃcchati yaddinādhipam |
kimu tīvrataraistataḥ karairmama dāhāya sa dhairyataskaraiḥ ||58||
kusumāni yadi smareṣavo
na tu vajraṃ viṣavallijāni tat |
hṛdayaṃ yadamūmuhannanūrmama
yaccātitarāmatītapan ||59||
tadihānavadhau nimajjato
mama kaṃdarpaśarādhinīraghau |
bhava pota ivāvalambanaṃ
vidhinākasmikasṛṣṭasaṃnidhiḥ ||60||
athavā bhavataḥ pravartanā
na kathaṃ piṣṭamiyaṃ pinaṣṭa naḥ |
svata eva satāṃ parārthatā
grahaṇānāṃ hi yathā yathārthatā ||61||
tava vartmani vartatāṃ śivaṃ
punarastu tvaritaṃ samāgamaḥ |
ayi ! sādhaya sādhayepsitaṃ
smaraṇīyāḥ samaye vayaṃ vayaḥ ||62||
iti taṃ sa visṛjya dhairyavānnṛpatiḥ sūnṛtavāgbṛhaspatiḥ |
aviśadvanaveśma vismitaḥ
smṛtilagnaiḥ kalahaṃsaśaṃsitaiḥ ||63||
atha bhīmasutāvalokanaiḥ
saphalaṃ kartumahastadeva saḥ |
kṣitimaṇḍalamaṇḍanāyitaṃ
nagaraṃ kuṇḍinamaṇḍajo yayau ||64||
prathamaṃ pathi locanātithiṃ
pathikaprārthitasiddhiśaṃsinam |
kalasaṃ jalasaṃbhṛtaṃ puraḥ
kalahaṃsaḥ kalayāṃbabhūva saḥ ||65||
avalambya didṛkṣayāmbare
kṣaṇamāścaryarasālasaṃ gatam |
sa vilāsavane'vanībhṛtaḥ
phalamaikṣiṣṭa rasālasaṃgatam ||66||
nabhasaḥ kalabhairupāsitaṃ
jaladairbhūritarakṣupaṃ nagaram |
sa dadarśa pataṅgapuṃgavo
viṭapacchannatarakṣupannagam ||67||
sa yayau dhutapakṣatiḥ kṣaṇaṃ
kṣaṇamūrdhvāyanadurvibhāvanaḥ |
vitatīkṛtaniścalacchadaḥ
kṣaṇamālokakadattakautukaḥ ||68||
tanudīdhitidhārayā
rayādgatayā lokavilokanāmasau |
chadahema kaṣannivālasatkaṣapāṣāṇanibhe nabhastale ||69||
vinamadbhiradhaḥ sthitaiḥ khagairjhaṭiti śyenanipātaśaṅkibhiḥ |
sa niraikṣi dṛśaikayopari
syadajhāṃkāritapatrapaddhatiḥ ||70||
dadṛśe na janena yannasau
bhuvi tacchāyamavekṣya tatkṣaṇāt |
divi dikṣu vitīrṇacakṣuṣā
pṛthuvegadrutamuktadṛkpathaḥ ||71||
na vanaṃ pathi śiśriye'munā
kvacidapyuccataradrucārutam |
na saṃgotrajamanvavādi
vā gativegaprasaradrucā rutam ||72||
atha bhīmabhujena pālitā
nagarī mañjurasau dharājitā |
patagasya jagāma dṛkpathaṃ
haraśailopamasaudharājitā ||73||
dayitaṃ prati yatra saṃtatā
ratihāsā iva rejire bhuvaḥ |
sphaṭikopalavigrahā
gṛhāḥ śaśabhṛdbhittaniraṅkabhittayaḥ ||74||
nṛpanīlamaṇīgṛhatviṣāmupadheryatra bhayena bhāsvataḥ |
śaraṇārthamuvāsa vāsare'pyasadāvṛttyudayattamaṃ tamaḥ ||75||
sitadīpramaṇiprakalpite
yadagāre hasadaṅkarodasi |
nikhilānniśi pūrṇimā tithīnūpatasthe'tithirekikā tithiḥ ||76||
sudatījanamajjanārpitairghusṛṇairyatra kaṣāyitāśayā |
na niśākhilayāpi vāpikā
prasasāda grahileva māninī ||77||
kṣaṇanīravayā yayā niśi
śritavaprāvaliyogapaṭṭayā |
maṇiveśmamayaṃ sma nirmalaṃ
kimapi jyotirabāhyamijyate ||78||
vilalāsa jalāśayodare
kvacana dyauranubimbiteva yā |
parikhākapaṭasphuṭasphuṭatpratibimbānavalambitāmbuni ||79||
vrajate divi yadgṛhāvalīcalacelāñcaladaṇḍatāḍanāḥ |
vyatarannaruṇāya viśramaṃ
sṛjate helihayālikālanām ||80||
kṣitigarbhadharāmbarālayaiḥ
talamadhyoparipūriṇāṃ pṛthak |
jagatāṃ kila yākhilādbhutājani sārairnijacihnadhāribhiḥ ||81||
dadhadambudanīlakaṇṭhatāṃ
vahadatyacchasudhojjalaṃ vapuḥ |
kathamṛcchatu yatra nāma na
kṣitibhṛnmandiramindumaulitām ||82||
bahurūpakaśālabhañjikāmukhacandreṣu kalaṅkaraṅkavaḥ |
yadanekakasaudhakaṃdharāharibhiḥ kukṣigatīkṛtā iva ||83||
balisadmadivaṃ sa tathyavāg
upari smāha divo'pi nāradaḥ |
adharātha kṛtā yayeva sā
viparītājani bhūvibhūṣayā ||84||
pratihaṭṭapathe gharaṭṭajāt
pathikāhvānadasaktusaurabhaiḥ |
kalahānna ghanādyadutthitāt
adhunāpyujjhati ghargharasvaraḥ ||85||
varaṇaḥ kanakasya māninīṃ
divamaṅkādamarādrirāgatām |
ghanaratnakapāṭapakṣatiḥ
parirabhyānunayannuvāsa yām ||86||
analaiḥ pariveṣametya yā
jvaladarkopalavaprajanmabhiḥ |
udayaṃ layamantarā raveravahadbāṇapurīparārdhyatām ||87||
bahukambumaṇirvarāṭikāgaṇanāṭatkarakarkaṭotkaraḥ |
himavālukayācchavālukaḥ
paṭu dadhvāna yadāpaṇārṇavaḥ ||88||
yadagāraghaṭāṭṭakuṭṭimasravadindūpalatundilāpayā |
mumuce na pativrataucitī
praticandrodayamabhragaṅgyā ||89||
rucayo'stamitasya bhāsvataḥ
skhalitā yatra nirālayāḥ kila |
anusāyamabhurvilepanāpaṇakaśmīrajapaṇyavīthayaḥ ||90||
vitataṃ vaṇijāpaṇe'khilaṃ
paṇituṃ yatra janena vīkṣyate |
munineva mṛkaṇḍusūnunā
jagatīvastu purodare hareḥ ||91||
samameṇamadairyadāpaṇe
tulayansaurabhalobhaniścalam |
paṇitā na janāravairavait
api guñjantamaliṃ malīmasam ||92||
ravikāntamayena setunā
sakalāhaṃ jvalanāhitoṣmaṇā |
śiśire niśi gacchatāṃ purā
caraṇau yatra dunoti no himam ||93||
vidhudīdhitijena yatpathaṃ
payasā naiṣadhaśīlaśītalam |
śaśikāntamayaṃ tapāgame
kalitīvrastapati sma nātapaḥ ||94||
parikhāvalayacchalena yā
na pareṣāṃ grahaṇasya gocaraḥ |
phaṇibhāṣitabhāṣyaphakkikāviṣamā kuṇḍalanāmavāpitā ||95||
mukhapāṇipadākṣṇi paṅkajai
racitāṅgeṣvapareṣu campakaiḥ |
svayamādita yatra bhāmajā
smarapūjākusumasrajaḥ śriyam ||96||
jaghanastanabhāragauravād
viyadālambya vihartumakṣamāḥ |
dhruvamapsaraso'vatīrya yāṃ
śatamadhyāsata tatsakhījanaḥ ||97||
sthitiśālisamastavarṇatāṃ
na kathaṃ citramayī bibhartu yā |
svarabhedamupaitu yā kathaṃ
kalitānalpamukhāravā na vā ||98||
svarucāruṇayā patākayā
dinamarkeṇa samīyuṣottṛṣaḥ |
lilihurbahudhā sudhākaraṃ
niśi māṇikyamayā yadālayāḥ ||99||
lilihe svarucā patākayā
niśi jihvānibhayā sudhākaram |
śritamarkakaraiḥ pipāsu
yannṛpasadmāmalapadmarāgajam ||100||
amṛtadyutilakṣma pītayā
militaṃ yadvalabhīpatākayā |
valayāyitaśeṣaśāyinaḥ
sakhitāmādita pītavāsasaḥ ||101||
aśrāntaśrutipāṭhapūtarasanāvirbhūtabhūristavājihmabrahmamukhaughavighnitanavasvargakriyākelinā |
pūrva gādhisutena sāmighaṭitā muktā nu mandākinī
yatprāsādadukūlavalliranilāndolairakheladdivi ||102||
yadativimalanīlaveśmaraśmibhramaritabhāḥ śucisaudhavastravalliḥ |
alabhata śamanasvasuḥ śiśutvaṃ divasakarāṅkatale calā luṭhantī ||103||
svaprāṇeśvaranarmaharmyakaṭakātithyagrahāyotsukaṃ
pāthodaṃ nijakelisaudhaśikharādāruhya yatkāminī |
sākṣādapsaraso vimānakalitavyomāna evābhavadyanna prāya nimeṣamabhratarasā yāntī rasādadhvani ||104||
vaidarbhīkeliśaile marakataśikharādutthitairaṃśudarbhairbrahmāṇḍāghātabhagnasyadajamadatayā hrīdhṛtāvāṅmukhatvaiḥ |
kasyā nottānagāyā divi surasurabherāsyadeśaṃ gatāgrairyadgogrāsapradānavratasukṛtamaviśrāntamujjṛmbhate sma ||105||
vidhukaraparirambhādātmaniṣyandapūrṇaiḥ
śaśidṛṣadupaklṛptairālavālaistarūṇām |
viphalitajalasekaprakriyāgauraveṇa
vyaraci sa hṛtacittastatra bhaimīvanena ||106||
atha kanakapatatrastava tāṃ rājaputrīṃ
sadasi sadṛśabhāsāṃ visphurantīṃ sakhīnām |
uḍupariṣadi madhyasthāyiśītāṃśulekhānukaraṇapaṭulakṣmīmakṣilakṣīcakāra ||107||
bhramaṇarayavikīrṇasvarṇabhāsā khagena
kvacana patanayogyaṃ deśamanviṣyatādhaḥ |
mukhavidhumadasīyaṃ sevituṃ lambamānaḥ
śaśiparidhirivordhvaṃ maṇḍalastena tene ||108||
anubhavati śacītthaṃ sā ghṛtācīmukhābhirna saha sahacarībhirnandanānandamuccaiḥ |
iti matirudayāsīt pakṣiṇaḥ prekṣya bhaimīṃ
vipinabhuvi sakhībhiḥ sārdhamābaddhakelim ||109||
śrīharṣaṃ kavirājarājimukuṭālaṃkārahīraḥ sutaṃ
śrīhīraḥ suṣuve jitendriyacayaṃ māmalladevī ca yam |
dvaitīyīkatayā mito'yamagamattasya prabandhe mahākāvye cāruṇi naiṣadhīyacarite sargo nisargojjvalaḥ ||110||

tṛtīyaḥ sargaḥ
ākuñcitābhyāmatha pakṣatibhyāṃ
namovibhāgāttarasāvatīrya |
niveśadeśātatadhūtapakṣaḥ
papāta bhūmāvupabhaimi haṃsaḥ ||1||
ākasmikaḥ pakṣapuṭāhatāyāḥ
kṣitestadā yaḥ svana uccacāra |
drāganyavinyastadṛśaḥ sa tasyāḥ
saṃbhrāntamantaḥ karaṇaṃ cakāra ||2||
netrāṇi vaidarbhasutāsaṃkhīnāṃ
vimuktatattadviṣayagrahāṇi |
prāpustamekaṃ nirupākhyarūpaṃ
brahmeva cetāṃsi yatavratānām ||3||
haṃsaṃ tanau sannihitaṃ carantaṃ
munermanovṛttiriva svikāyām |
grahītukāmādariṇā śayena
yatnādasau niścalatāṃ jagāhe ||4||
tāmiṅgitairapyanumāya māyāmayaṃ na bhaimyā viyadutpapāta |
tatpāṇimātmoparipātukaṃ
tu moghaṃ vitene plutilāghavena ||5||
vyarthīkṛtaṃ patrarathena tena
tathā'vasāya vyavasāyamasyāḥ |
parasparāmarpitahastatālaṃ
tatkālamālībhirahasyatālam ||6||
uccāṭanīyaḥ karatālikānāṃ
dānādidānīṃ bhavatībhireṣaḥ |
yānveti māṃ duhyati mahmameva
sātretyupālambhi tayālivargaḥ ||7||
dhṛtālpakopā hasite sakhīnāṃ
chāyeva bhāsvantamabhiprayātuḥ |
śyāmātha haṃsasya karānavāpteḥ
mandākṣalakṣyā lagati sma paścāt ||8||
śastā na haṃsābhimukhī punaste
yātreti tābhiśchalahasyamānā |
sāha sma naivāśakunībhavenme
bhāvipriyāvedaka eṣa haṃsaḥ ||9||
haṃso'pyasau haṃsagateḥ sudatyāḥ
puraḥ puraścāru calanvamāse |
vailakṣyahetorgatimetadīyām
agre'nukṛtyopahasannivoccaiḥ ||10||
pade pade bhāvini bhāvinī taṃ
yathā karaprāpyamavaiti nūnam |
tathā sakhelaṃ calatā latāsu
pratārya tenācakṛṣe kṛśāṅgī ||11||
ruṣā niṣiddhālijanāṃ yadaināṃ
chāyādvitīyāṃ kalayāṃcakāra |
tadā śramāmbhaḥ kaṇabhūṣitāṅgīṃ
sa kīravanmānuṣavāgavādīt ||12||
aye ! kiyadyāvadupaiṣi dūraṃ
vyarthe pariśrāmyasi vā kimittham |
udeti te bhīrapi kiṃ nu bāle !
vilokayantyā na ghanā vanālīḥ ||13||
vṛthārpayantīmapathe padaṃ tvāṃ
marullalatpallavapāṇikampaiḥ |
ālīva paśya pratiṣedhatāyaṃ
kapotahuṃkāragirā vanālī ||14||
dhāryaḥ kathaṃkāramahaṃ bhavatyā
viyadvihārī vasudhaikagatyā |
aho ! śiśutvaṃ tava khaṇḍitaṃ na
smarasya sakhyā vayasāpyanena ||15||
sahasrapatrāsanapatrahaṃsavaṃśasya patrāṇi patatriṇaḥ smaḥ |
asmādṛśāṃ cāṭurasāmṛtāni
svarlokaloketaradurlabhāni ||16||
svargāpagāhemamṛṇālinīnāṃ
nālāmṛṇālāgrabhujo bhajāmaḥ |
annānurūpāṃ tanurūpaṛddhiṃ
kārye nidānāddhi guṇānadhīte ||17||
dhāturniyogādiha naiṣadhīyaṃ
līlāsaraḥ sevitumāgateṣu |
haimeṣu haṃseṣvahameka eva
bhramāmi bhūlokavilokanotkaḥ ||18||
vidheḥ kadācidbhramaṇīvilāse
śramāturebhyaḥ svamahattarebhyaḥ |
skandhasya viśrāntimadāṃ tadādi
śrāmyāmi nāviśramaviśvago'pi ||19||
bandhāya divye na tiraści kaścit
pāśādirāsāditapauruṣaḥ syāt |
ekaṃ vinā mādṛśi tannarasya
svarbhogabhāgyaṃ viralodayasya ||20||
iṣṭena pūrtena nalasya vaśyāḥ
svarbhogamatrāpi sṛjantyamartyāḥ |
mahīruhā dohadasekaśakterākālikaṃ korakamudgiranti ||21||
suvarṇaśailādavatīrya tūrṇaṃ
svarvāhinīvārikaṇāvakīrṇaiḥ |
taṃ vījayāmaḥ smarakelikāle
pakṣairnṛpaṃ cāmarabaddhasakhyaiḥ ||22||
kriyeta cetsādhuvibhakticintā
vyaktistadā sā prathamābhidheyā |
yā svaujasāṃ sādhayituṃ vilāsaiḥ
tāvatkṣamānāmapadaṃ bahu syāt ||23||
rājā sa yajvā vibudhavrajatrā
kṛtvādhvarājyopamayaiva rājyam |
bhuṅkte śritaśrotriyasātkṛtaśrīḥ
pūrvaṃ tvaho śeṣamaśeṣamantyam ||24||
dāridryadāridraviṇaughavarṣai
ramoghameghavratamarthisārthe |
saṃtuṣṭamiṣṭāni tamiṣṭadevaṃ
nāthanti ke nāma na lokanātham ||25||
asmatkila śrotrasudhāṃ vidhāya
rambhā ciraṃ bhāmatulāṃ nalasya |
tatrānuraktā tamanāpya bheje
tannāmagandhānnalakūbaraṃ sā ||26||
svarlokamasmābhiritaḥ prayātaiḥ
kelīṣu tadgānaguṇānnipīya |
hā heti gāyanyadaśoci tena
nāmnaiva hāhā harigāyano'bhūt ||27||
śṛṇvansadārastadudārabhāvaṃ
hṛṣyanmuhurloma pulomajāyāḥ |
puṇyena nālokata lokapālaḥ
pramodabāṣpāvṛtanetramālaḥ ||28||
sāpīśvare śṛṇvati tadguṇaudhān
prasahya ceto harato'rdhaśambhuḥ |
abhūdaparṇāṅguliruddhakarṇā
kadā na kaṇḍūyanakaitavena ||29||
alaṃ sajandharmavidhau vidhātā
ruṇaddhi maunasya miṣeṇa vāṇīm |
tatkaṇṭhamāliṅgya rasasya tṛptāṃ
na veda tāṃ vedajaḍaḥ sa vakrām ||30||
śriyastadāliṅganabhūrna bhūtā
vratakṣatiḥ kāpi pativratāyāḥ |
samastabhūtātmatayā na bhūtaṃ
tadbharturīrṣyākaluṣāṇunāpi ||31||
dhik taṃ vidheḥ pāṇimajātalajjaṃ
nirmāti yaḥ parvaṇi pūrṇamindum |
manye sa vijñaḥ smṛtatanmukhaśrīḥ
kṛtvārdhamaujjhaddharamūrdhni yastam ||32||
nilīyate hrīvidhuraḥ svajaitraṃ
śrutvā vidhustasya mukhaṃ mukhānnaḥ |
sūre samudrasya kadāpi pūre
kadācidabhrabhramadabhragarbhe ||33||
saṃjñāpya naḥ svadhvajabhṛtyavargān
daityāriratyabjanalāsyanutyai |
tatsaṃkucannābhisarojapītād
dhāturvilajjaṃ ramate ramāyām ||34||
rekhābhirāsye gaṇanādivāsya
dvātriṃśatā dantamayībhirantaḥ |
caturdaśāṣṭādaśa cātra vidyā
dvedhāpi santīti śaśaṃsa vedhāḥ ||35||
śriyau narendrasya nirīkṣya tasya
smarāmarendrāvapi na smarāmaḥ |
vāsena tasminkṣamayośca samyagbuddhau na dadhmaḥ khalu śeṣa buddhau ||36||
vinā patatraṃ vinatātanūjaiḥ samīraṇairīkṣaṇalakṣaṇīyaiḥ |
manobhirāsīdanaṇupramāṇairna laṅghitā dikkatamā tadaśvaiḥ ||37||
saṃgrāmabhūmīṣu bhavatyarīṇām
asrairnadīmātṛkatāṃ gatāsu |
tadbāṇadhārāpavanāśanānāṃ
rājavrajīyairasubhiḥ subhikṣam ||38||
yaśo yadasyājani saṃyugeṣu
kaṇḍulabhāvaṃ bhajatā bhujena |
hetorguṇādeva digāpagālī
kūlaṃkaṣatvavyasanaṃ tadīyam ||39||
yadi trilokī gaṇanāparā syāttasyāḥ samāptiryadi nāyuṣaḥ syāt |
pāreparārdhe gaṇitaṃ yadi syādgaṇeyaniḥśeṣa guṇo'pi sa syāt ||40||
avāritadvāratayā tiraścāmantaḥpure tasya niviśya rājñaḥ |
gateṣu ramyeṣvadhikaṃ viśeṣam
adhyāpayāmaḥ paramāṇumadhyāḥ ||41||
pīyūṣadhārānadharābhirantaḥ
tāsāṃ rasodanvati majjayāmaḥ |
rambhādisaubhāgyarahaḥkathābhiḥ
kāvyena kāvyaṃ sṛjatādṛtābhiḥ ||42||
kābhirna tatrābhinavasmarājñāviśvāsanikṣepavaṇik kriye'ham |
jihreti yannaiva kuto'pi tiryak
kaścittiraścastrapate na tena ||43||
vārtāpi nāsatyapi sānyameti
yogādarandhre hṛdi yāṃ nirundhe |
virañcinānānanavādadhauta
samādhiśāstraśrutipūrṇakarṇaḥ ||44||
nalāśrayeṇa tridivopabhogaṃ
tavānavāpyaṃ labhate batanyā |
kumudvatīvenduparigraheṇa
jyotsnotsavaṃ durlabhamambujinyā ||45||
tannaiṣadhānūḍhatayā durāpaṃ
śarma tvayāsmatkṛtacāṭujanma |
rasālavanyā madhupānuviddhaṃ
saubāgyamaprāptavasantayeva ||46||
tasyaiva vā yāsyasi kiṃ na hastaṃ
dṛṣṭaṃ manaḥ kena vidheḥ praviśya |
ajātapāṇigrahaṇāsi tāvat
rūpasvarūpātiśayāśrayaśca ||47||
niśā śaśāṅkaṃ śivayā girīśaṃ
śriyā hariṃ yojayataḥ pratītaḥ |
vidherapi svārasikaḥ prayāsaḥ
paraspara yogyasamāgamāya ||48||
velātigastraiṇaguṇaābdhivaiṇiḥ
na yogayogyāsi naletareṇa |
saṃdarbhyate darbhaguṇena mallīmālā na mṛdvī bhṛśakarkaśena ||49||
vidhiṃ vadhūsṛṣṭimapṛcchameva
tadyānayugyo nalakeliyogyām |
tvannāmavarṇā iva karṇapītā
mayāsya saṃkrīḍati cakricakre ||50||
anyena patyā tvayi yojitāyāṃ
vijñatvakīrtyā gatajanmano vā |
janāpavādārṇavamuttarītuṃ
vidhā vidhātuḥ katamā tarīḥ syāt ||51||
āstāṃ tadaprastutacintayālaṃ
mayāsi tanvi ! śramitātivelam |
so'haṃ tadāgaḥ parimārṣṭukāmaḥ
kimīpsitaṃ te vidadhe'bhidhehi ||52||
itīrayitvā virarāma patrī
sa rājaputrīhṛdayaṃ bubhutsuḥ |
hrade gabhīre hṛdi cāvagāḍhe
śaṃsanti kāryāvataraṃ hi santaḥ ||53||
kiṃcittiraścīnavilolamauliḥ
vicintya vācyaṃ manasā muhūrtam |
patatriṇaṃ sā pṛthivīndraputrī
jagāda vakreṇa tṛṇīkṛtenduḥ ||54||
dhikcāpale vatsimavatsalatvaṃ
yatpreraṇāduttaralībhavantyā |
samīrasaṅgādiva nīrabhaṅgyā
mayā taṭasthastvamupadruto'si ||55||
ādarśatāṃ svacchatayā prayāsi
satāṃ sa tāvatkhalu darśanīyaḥ |
āgaḥ puraskurvati sāgasaṃ māṃ
yasyātmanīdaṃ pratibimbitaṃ te ||56||
anāryamapyācaritaṃ kumāryā
bhavānmama kṣāmyatu saumya ! tāvat |
haṃso'pi devāṃśatayāsi vandyaḥ
śrīvatsalakṣmeva hi matsyamūrtiḥ ||57||
matprītimādhitsasi kāṃ tvadīkṣāmudaṃ madakṣṇorapi yātiśetām |
nijāmṛtairlocanasecanādvā
pṛthakkiminduḥ sṛjati prajānām ||58||
manastu yaṃ nojjhati jātu yātu
manorathaḥ kaṇṭhapathaṃ kathaṃ saḥ |
kā nāma bālā dvijarājapāṇigrahābhilāṣaṃ kathayedalajjā ||59||
vācaṃ tadīyāṃ paripīya mṛdvīṃ
mṛdvīkayā tulyarasāṃ sa haṃsaḥ |
tatyāja toṣaṃ parapuṣṭaghuṣṭe
ghṛṇāṃ ca vīṇākvaṇite vitena ||60||
mandākṣamandākṣaramudramuktvā
tasyāṃ samākuñcitavāci haṃsaḥ |
tacchaṃsite kiṃcana saṃśayāluḥ
girā mukhāmbhojamayaṃ yuyoja ||61||
kareṇa vāñcheva vidhuṃ vidhartuṃ
yamitthamātthādariṇī tamartham |
pātuṃ śrutibhyāmapi nādhikurve
varṇe śrutervarṇa ivāntimaḥ kim ||62||
avāpyate vā kimiyadbhavatyā
cittaikapadyāmapi vidyate yaḥ |
yatrāndhakāraḥ kila cetaso'pi
jihmetarairbrahma tadapyavāpyam ||63||
īśāṇimaiśvaryavivartamadhye
lokeśalokeśayalokamadhye |
tiryañcamapyañca mṛṣānabhijñarasajñatopajñasamajñamajñam ||64||
madhye śrutīnāṃ prativeśinīnāṃ
sarasvatī vāsavatī mukhe naḥ |
hriyeva tābhyaścalatīyamaddhā
pathānna satsaṅgaguṇena naddhā ||65||
paryaṅkatāpannasarasvadaṅkāṃ
laṅkāṃ purīmapyabhilāṣi cittam |
kutrāpi cedvastuni te prayāti
tadapyavehi svaśaye śayālu ||66||
itīritā patrarathena tena
hrīṇā ca hṛṣṭā ca babhāṇa bhaimī |
ceto nalaṅkāmayate madīryaṃ
nānyatra kutrāpi ca sābhilāṣam ||67||
vicintya bālājanaśīlaśailaṃ
lajjānadīmajjadanaṅganāgam |
ācaṣṭa vispaṣṭamabhāṣamāṇāmenāṃ sa cakrāṅgapataṅgaśakraḥ ||68||
nṛpeṇa pāṇigrahaṇe spṛheti
nalaṃ manaḥ kāmayate mameti |
āśleṣi na śleṣakaverbhavatyāḥ
ślokadvayārthaḥ sudhiyā mayā kim ||69||
tvaccetasaḥ sthairyaviparyayaṃ tu
saṃbhāvya bhāvyasmi tamajña eva |
lakṣye hi bālāhṛdi lolaśīle
darāparāddheṣurapi smaraḥ syāt ||70||
mahīmahendraḥ khalu naiṣadhenduḥ
tadvodhanīyaḥ kathamitthameva |
prayojanaṃ sāṃśayikaṃ pratīdṛk
pṛthagjaneneva sa madvidhena ||71||
piturniyogena nijecchayā vā
yuvānamanyaṃ yadi vā vṛṇīṣe |
tvadarthamarthitvakṛti pratītiḥ
kīdṛṅmayi syānniṣadheśvarasya ||72||
tvapāyi kiṃ śaṅkitavikriye'smin
nadhikriye vā viṣaye nidātum |
itaḥ pṛthakprārthayase tu yadyat
kurve tadurvīpatiputri ! sarvam ||73||
śravaḥ praviṣṭā iva tadgirastā
vidhūya vaimatyadhutena mūrdhnā |
ūce hriyo'pi ślathitānurodhā
punardharitrīpuruhūtaputrī ||74||
madanyadānaṃ prati kalpanā yā
vedastvadīye hṛdi tāvadeṣā |
niśo'pi sometarakāntaśaṅkā
moṃkāramagresaramasya kuryāḥ ||75||
sarojinīmānasarāgavṛtteḥ
anarkasaṃparkamatarkayitvā |
madanyapāṇigrahaśaṅkiteyamaho mahīyastava sāhasikyam ||76||
sādhu tvayā tarkitametadeva
svenānalaṃ yatkila saṃśrayiṣye |
vināmunā svātmani tu prahartu
mṛṣāgiraṃ tvāṃ nṛpatau na kartum ||77||
madvipralabhyaṃ punarāha yastvāṃ
tarkaḥ sa kiṃ tatphalavāci mūkaḥ |
aśakyaśaṅkāvyabhicārahetuḥ
vāṇī na vedā yadi santu ke tu ||78||
anaiṣadhāyaiva juhoti tātaḥ
kiṃ māṃ kṛśānau na śarīraśeṣām |
īṣṭe tanūjanmatanoḥ sa nūnaṃ
matprāṇanāthastu nalastathāpi ||79||
tadekadāsītvapadādudagre
madīpsite sādhu vidhitsutā te |
ahelināṃ kiṃ nalinī vidhatte
sudhākareṇāpi sudhākareṇa ||80||
tadekalubdhe hṛdi me'sti labdhuṃ
cintā na cintāmaṇimapyanarṣyam |
citte mamaikaḥ sakalatrilokīsāro nidhiḥ padmamukhaḥ sa eva ||81||
śrutaḥ sa dṛṣṭaśca haritsu mohāt
dyātaḥ sa nīrandhritabuddhidhāram |
mamādya tatprāptirasuvyayo vā
haste tavāste dvayamekaśeṣaḥ ||82||
saṃcīyatāmāśrutapālanotthaṃ
matprāṇaviśrāṇanajaṃ ca puṇyam |
nivāryatāmārya ! vṛthā viśaṅkā
bhadre'pi mudreyamaye ! bhṛśaṃ kā ||83||
alaṃ vilaṅghya priyavijña ! yācñāṃ
kṛtvāpi vāgyaṃ vividhaṃ vidheye |
yaśaḥ pathādāśravatāpadotthāt
khalu skhalitvāstakhaloktikhelāt ||84||
svajīvamapyārtamude dadadbhyaḥ
tava trapā nedṛśabaddhamuṣṭeḥ |
mahyaṃ madīyānyadasūnaditsoḥ
dharmaḥ karādbhraśyati kīrtidhautaḥ ||85||
dattvātmajīvaṃ tvayi jīvade'pi
śudhyāmi jīvādhikade tu kena |
vidhehi tanmāṃ tvadṛṇānyaśoddhum
amudradāridryasamudramagnām ||86||
krīṇīṣva majjīvitameva paṇvam
anyanna cedasti tadastu puṇyam |
jīveśadātaryadi te na dātuṃ
yaśo'pi tāvatprabhavāmi gātum ||87||
varāṭikopakriyayāpi labhyān
nebhyāḥ kṛtajñānathavādriyante |
prāṇaiḥ paṇaḥ svaṃ nipuṇaṃ bhaṇantaḥ
krīṇanti tāneva tu hanta santaḥ ||88||
sa bhūbhṛdaṣṭāvapi lokapālāḥ
tairme tadekāgradhiyaḥ prasede |
na hītarasmāddhaṭate yadetya
svayaṃ tadāptipratibhūrmamābhūḥ ||89||
akāṇḍamevātmabhuvārjitasya
bhūtvāpi mūlaṃ mayi vīraṇasya |
bhavānna me kiṃ naladatvametya
kartā hṛdaścandanalepakṛtyam ||90||
alaṃ vilambya tvarituṃ hi velā
kārye kila sthairyasahe vicāraḥ |
gurūpadeśaṃ pratimeva tīkṣṇā
pratīkṣate jātu na kālamartiḥ ||91||
abhyarthanīyaḥ sa gatena rājā
tvayā na śuddhāntagato madartham |
priyāsyadākṣiṇyavalātkṛto hi
tadodayedanyavadhūniṣedhaḥ ||92||
śuddhāntasaṃbhoganitāntatuṣṭe
na naiṣadhe kāryamidaṃ nigādyam |
apāṃ hi tṛptāya na vāridhārā
svāduḥ sugandhiḥ svadate tuṣārā ||93||
tvayā vidheyā na giro madarthāḥ
kudhā kaduṣṇe hṛdi naiṣadhasya |
pittena dūne rasane sitāpi
tiktāyate haṃsakulāvataṃsa ! ||94||
dharāturāsāhi madarthayācñā
kāryā na kāryāntaracumbicitte |
tadārthitasyānavabodhanidrā
bibhartyavajñācaraṇasya mudrām ||95||
vijñena vijñāpyamidaṃ narendre
tasmāttvayāsminsamayaṃ samīkṣya |
ātyantikāsiddhivilambisidhdyoḥ
kāryasya kāryasya śubhā vibhāti ||96||
ityuktavatyā yadalopi lajjā
sānaucitī cetasi naścakāstu |
smarastu sākṣī tadadoṣatāyām
unmādya yastattadavīvadattām ||97||
unmattamāsādya haraḥ smaraśca
dvāvapyasīmāṃ mudamudvahete |
pūrvaḥ paraspardhitayā prasūnaṃ nūnaṃ
dvitīyo virahādhidūnam ||98||
tathābhidhātrīmatha rājaputrīṃ
nirṇīya tāṃ naiṣadhabaddharāgām |
amoci cañcūpuṭamaunamudrā
vihāyasā tena vihasya bhūyaḥ ||99||
idaṃ yadi kṣmāpatiputri ! tattvaṃ
paśyāmi tanna svavidheyamasmin |
tvāmuccakaistāpayatā nṛpaṃ ca
pañceṣuṇaivājani yojaneyam ||100||
tvadvaddhabuddherbahirindriyāṇāṃ
tasyopavāsavratināṃ tapobhiḥ |
tvamadya labdhvāmṛtatṛptibhājāṃ
svadevabhūyaṃ caritārthamastu ||101||
tulyāvayormūrtirabhūnmadīyā
dagdhā paraṃ sāsya na tāpyate'pi |
ityabhyasūyanniva dehatāpaṃ
tasyātanustvadvirahādvidhatte ||102||
lipiṃ dṛśā bhittivibhūṣaṇaṃ tvāṃ
nṛpaḥ pibannādaranirnimeṣaḥ |
cakṣurjharairarpitamātmacakṣūrāgaṃ sa dhatte racitaṃ tvayā nu ||103||
pāturdṛśālekhyanayīṃ nṛpasya
tvāmādarādastanimīlayāste |
mamedamityaśruṇi netravṛtteḥ
prīternimeṣacchidayā vivādaḥ ||104||
tvaṃ hṛdgatā bhaimi ! bahirgatāpi
prāṇāyitā nāsikayāsyagatyā |
na citramākrāmati tatra citram
etanmano yadbhavadekavṛtti ||105||
ajasramārohasi dūradīrgho
saṃkalpasopānatatiṃ tadīyām |
śvāsānsa varṣatyadhikaṃ punaryadyānāttava tvanmayatāṃ tadāpya ||106||
hṛttasya yanmantrayate rahastvāṃ
tadvyaktamāmantrayate mukhaṃ yat |
tadvairipuṣpāyudhamitracandrasakhyaucitī sā khalu tanmukhasya ||107||
sthitasya rātrāvadhiśayya śayyāṃ
mohe manastasya nimajjayantī |
āliṅgya yā cumbati locane sā
nidrādhunā na tvadṛte'ṅganā vā ||108||
smareṇa nistakṣya vṛthaiva bāṇaiḥ
lāvaṇyaśeṣāṃ kṛśatāmanāyi |
anaṅgatāmapyayamāpyamānaḥ
spardhāṃ na sārdhe vijahāti tena ||109||
tvatprāpakāttrasyati nainaso'pi
tvayyeṣa dāsye'pi na lajjate yat |
smareṇa bāṇairatitakṣya tīkṣṇaiḥ
lūnaḥ svabhāvo'pi kiyānkimasya ||110||
smāraṃ jvaraṃ ghoramapatrapiṣṇoḥ
siddhāgadaṅkāracaye cikitsau |
nidānamaunādaviśadviśālā
sāṅkrāmikī tasya rujeva lajjā ||111||
bibheti ruṣṭāsi kiletyakasmāt
sa tvāṃ kilāpeti hasatyakāṇḍe |
yāntīmiva tvāmanu yātyahetoḥ
uktastvayeva prativakti mogham ||112||
bhavadviyogācchidurārtidhārāyamasvasurmajjati niḥśaraṇyaḥ |
mūrcchāmayadvīpamahāndhyapaṅke
hā hā mahībhṛdbhaṭakuñjaro'yam ||113||
savyāpasavyatyajanāddviruktaiḥ
pañceṣubāṇaiḥ pṛthagarjitāsu |
daśāsu śeṣā khalu taddaśā yā
tayā nabhaḥ puṣpyatu korakeṇa ||114||
tvayi smarādheḥ satatāsmitena
prasthāpito bhūmibhṛtāsmi tena |
āgatya bhūtaḥ saphalo bhavatyā
bhāvapratītyā guṇalobhavatyāḥ ||115||
dhanyāsi vaidarbhi ! guṇairudāraiḥ
yayā samākṛṣyata naiṣadho'pi |
itaḥ stutiḥ kaḥ khalu candrikāyā
yadabdhimapyuttaralīkaroti ||116||
nalena bhāyāḥ śaśinā niśeva
tvayā sa bhāyānniśayā śaśīva |
punaḥ punastadyugayugvidhātā
yogyāmupāste nu yuvāṃ yuyukṣuḥ ||117||
stanadvaye tanvi ! paraṃ tavaiva
pṛtho yadi prāpsyati naiṣadhasya |
analpavaidagdhyavivardhinīnāṃ
patrāvalīnāṃ valanā samāptim ||118||
ekaḥ sudhāṃśurna kathaṃcana syāt
tṛptikṣamastvannayanadvayasya |
tvallocanāsecanakastadastu
nalāsyaśītadyutisadvitīyaḥ ||119||
aho tapaḥ kalpatarurnalīyaḥ
tvatpāṇijāgrasphuradaṅkuraśrīḥ |
tvadbhrūyugaṃ yasya khalu dvipatrī
tavādharo rajyati yatkalambaḥ ||120||
yaste navaḥ pallavitaḥ karābhyāṃ
smitena yaḥ korakitastavāste |
aṅgamradimnā tava puṣpito yaḥ
stanaśriyā yaḥ phalitastavaiva ||121||
kāṃsīkṛtāsītkhalu maṇḍalīndoḥ
saṃsaktaraśmiprakarā smareṇa |
tulā ca nārācalatā nijaiva
mithonurāgasya samīkṛtau vām ||122||
sattvasrutasvedamadhūtthasāndre
tatpāṇipadme madanotsaveṣu |
lagnotthitāstvatkucapatralekhāḥ
tannirgatāstaṃ praviśantu bhūyaḥ ||123||
bandhāḍhyanānāratamallayuddha
pramoditaiḥ kelivane marudbhiḥ |
prasūnavṛṣṭiṃ punaruktamuktāṃ
pratīcchataṃ bhaimi ! yuvāṃ yuvānau ||124||
anyonyasaṃgamavaśādadhunā vibhātāṃ
tasyāpi te'pi manasī vikasadvilāse |
sraṣṭuṃ punarmanasijasya tanuṃ pravṛttamādāvivi hyaṇukakṛtparamāṇuyugamam ||125||
kāmaḥ kausumacāpadurjayamamuṃ jetuṃ nṛpaṃ tvāṃ dhanurvallīmavraṇavaṃśajāmadhiguṇāmāsādya mādyatyasau |
grīvālaṃkṛtipaṭṭasūtralatayā pṛṣṭhe kiyallambayā
bhrājiṣṇuṃ kaṣarekhayeva nivasatsindūrasaundaryayā ||126||
tvahgucchāvalimauktikāni gulikāstaṃ rājahaṃsaṃ vibhorvedhyaṃ viddhi manobhuvaḥ svamapi tāṃ mañjuṃ dhanurmañjarīm |
yannityāṅkanivāsalālitatamajyābhujyamānaṃ lasannābhīmadhyabilā vilāsamakhilaṃ romālirālambate ||127||
puṣpeṣuścikureṣu te śaracayaṃ tvadbhālamūle dhanū
raudre cakṣuṣi tajjitastanumanubhrāṣṭraṃ ca yaścikṣipe |
nirvidyāśrayadāśrayaṃ sa vitanustvāṃ tajjayāyādhunā
patrālistvadurojaśailanilayā tatparṇaśālāyate ||128||
ityālapatyatha patatriṇi tatra bhaimīṃ
sakhyaścirāttadanusaṃdhiparāḥ parīyuḥ |
śarmāstu te visṛja māmiti so'pyudīrya
vegājjagāma niṣadhādhiparājadhānīm ||129||
cetojanmaśaraprasūnamadhubhirvyāmiśratāmāśrayattpreyodūtapataṅgapuṅgavagavīhaiyaṅgavīnaṃ rasāt |
svādaṃ svādamasīmamiṣṭasurabhi prāptāpi tṛptiṃ na sā
tāpaṃ prāya nitāntamantaratulāmānarccha mūrcchāmapi ||130||
tasyā dṛśo nṛpatibandhumanuvrajantyāstaṃ
vāpyavāri na cirādavadhīvabhūva |
pārśve'pi vipracakṛṣe yadanenadṛṣṭeḥ
ārādapi vyavadadhe na tu cittavṛtteḥ ||131||
astitvaṃ kāryasiddheḥ sphuṭamatha kathayanpakṣateḥ kampabhedaiḥ
ākhyātuṃ vṛttametanniṣadhanarapatau sarvamekaḥ pratasthe |
kāntāre nirgatāsi priyasakhi ! padavī vismṛtā kiṃ nu mugdhe |
mā rodīrehi yāmetyupahṛtavacaso ninyuranyāṃ vayasyāḥ ||132||
sarasi nṛpamapaśyadyatra tattīrabhājaḥ
smarataralamaśokānokahasyopamūlam |
kisalayadalatalpamlāyinaṃ prāpa taṃ sa
jvaladasamaśareṣuspardhipuṣpārddhimauleḥ ||133||
paravati damayanti ! tvāṃ na kiṃcidvadāmi
drutamupanama kiṃ māmāha sā śaṃsa haṃsa ! |
iti vadati nale'sau tacchaśaṃsopanamraḥ
priyamanu sukṛtāṃ hi svaspṛhāyā vilambaḥ ||134||
kathitamapi narendraḥ śaṃsayāmāsa haṃsaṃ
kimiti kimiti pṛcchanbhāṣitaṃ sa priyāyāḥ |
adhigatamatha sāndrānandamādhvīkamattaḥ
svayamapi śatakṛtvastattathānvācacakṣe ||135||
śrīharṣaṃ kavirājarājimukuṭālaṃkārahīraḥ sutaṃ
śrīhīraḥ suṣuve jitendriyacayaṃ māmalladevī ca yam |
tārtīyīkatayā mito'yamagamattasya prabandhe mahākāvye cāruṇi naiṣadhīyacarite sargo nisargojjvalaḥ ||136||

caturtha sargaḥ
atha nalasya guṇaṃ guṇamātmabhūḥ
surabhi tasya yaśaḥ kusumaṃ dhanuḥ |
śrutipathopagataṃ sumanastayā
tamiṣumāśu vidhāya jigāya tām ||1||
yadatanujvarabhāktanute sma
sā priyakathāsarasīrasamajjanam |
sapadi tasya cirāntaratāpinī
pariṇatirviṣamā samapadyata ||2||
dhruvamadhītavaṃtīyamadhīratāṃ
dayitadūtapatadgatavegataḥ |
sthitivirodhakarīṃ dvyaṇukodarī
taduditaḥ sa hi yo yadanantaraḥ ||3||
atitamāṃ samapādi jaḍāśayaṃ
smitalavasmaraṇe'pi tadānanam |
ajani paṅgurapāṅganijāṅgaṇabhramikaṇe'pi tadīkṣaṇakhañjanaḥ ||4||
kimu tadantarubhau bhiṣajau divaḥ
smaranalau viśataḥ sma vigāhitum |
tadabhikena cikitsitumāśu tāṃ
makhabhujāmadhipena niyojitau ||5||
kusumacāpajatāpasamākuṃ
kamalakomalamaikṣyata tanmukham |
aharaharvahadabhyadhikādhikāṃ
raviruciglapitasya vidhorvidhām ||6||
taruṇatātaraṇidyutinirmitadraḍhima tatkucakumbhayugaṃ tadā |
analasaṃgatitāpamupaitu no
kusumacāpakulālavilāsajam ||7||
adhṛta yadvirahoṣmaṇi bhajjitaṃ
manasijena tadūruyugaṃ tadā |
spṛśati tatkadanaṃ kadalītaruḥ
yadi marūjvaladūṣaradūṣitaḥ ||8||
smaraśarāhatinirmitasaṃjvaraṃ
karayugaṃ hasati sma damasvasuḥ |
anapidhānapatattapanātapaṃ
tapanipītasaraḥ sarasīruham ||9||
madanatāpabhareṇa vidīrya no
yadudapāti hṛdā damanasvasuḥ |
nibiḍapīnakucadvayayantraṇā
tabhaparādhamadhātpratibadhnatī ||10||
niviśate yadi śūkaśikhā pade
sṛjati sā kiyatīmiva na vyathām |
mṛdunorvitanotu kathaṃ na tāmavanibhṛttu niviśya hṛdi sthitaḥ ||11||
manasi santamiva priyamīkṣituṃ
nayanayoḥ spṛhayāntarupetayoḥ |
grahaṇaśaktirabhūdidamīyayoḥ
api na saṃmukhavāstuni vastuni ||12||
hṛdi damasvasuraśrujharaplute
pratiphaladvirahāttamukhānateḥ |
hṛdayabhājamarājata cumbituṃ
nalamupetya kilāgami tanmukham ||13||
suhṛdamagnimudañcayituṃ smaraṃ
manasi gandhavahena mṛgīdṛśaḥ |
akali niḥśvasitena vinirgamānumitanihnutaveśanamāyitā ||14||
virahapāṇḍimarāgatamomaṣīśitimatannijapītimavarṇakaiḥ |
daśa diśaḥ khalu taddṛgakalpayallipikarī nalarūpakacitritāḥ ||15||
smarakṛtāṃ hṛdayasya muhurdaśāṃ bahu
vadanniva niḥśvasitānilaḥ |
vyadhita vāsasi kampamadaḥ śrite
trasati kaḥ sati nāśrayabādhane ||16||
karapadānanalocananāmabhiḥ
śadatalaiḥ sutanorvirahajvare |
ravimaho bahupītacaraṃ cirād
aniśatāpamiṣādudasṛjyata ||17||
udayati sma tadadbhutamālibhiḥ
dharaṇibhṛdbhuvi tatra vimṛśya yat |
anumito'pi ca bāṣpanirīkṣaṇād
dvyavibhicacāra na tāpakaro nalaḥ ||18||
hṛdi vidarbhabhuvaḥ praharañśarai
ratipatirniṣadhādhipateḥ kṛte |
kṛtatadantaragasvadṛḍhavyadhaiḥ
phaladanītiramūrcchadalaṃ khalu ||19||
vidhuramāni tayā yadi bhānumān
kathamaho sa tu taddhṛdayaṃ tathā |
api viyogabharāsphuṭanasphuṭīkṛtadṛṣattvamajijvaladaṃśubhiḥ ||20||
hṛdayadattasaroruhayā tayā
kva sadṛgastu viyoganimagnayā |
priyadhanuḥ parirabhya hṛdā rattiḥ
kimanumartumaśeta citārciṣi ||21||
analabhāvamiyaṃ svanivāsino
na virahasya rahasyamabudhyata |
praśamanāya vidhāya tṛṇānyasūñjvalati tatra yadujjhitumaihata ||22||
prakṛtiretu guṇaḥ sa na yoṣitāṃ
kathamimāṃ hṛdayaṃ mṛdu nāma yat |
tadiṣubhiḥ kusumairapi dunvatā
suvivṛtaṃ vibudhena manobhuvā ||23||
riputarā bhavanādaviniryatīṃ
vidhurucirgṛhajālabilairnu tām |
itarathātmanivāraṇaśaṅkayā
jvarayituṃ viśaveśadharāviśat ||24||
hṛdi vidarbhabhuvo'śrubhṛti sphuṭaṃ
vinamadāsyatayā pratibimbitam |
mukhadṛgoṣṭhamaropi manobhuvā
tadupamākusumānyakhilāḥ śarāḥ ||25||
virahapāṇḍukapolatale vidhuḥ
vyadhita bhīmabhuvaḥ pratibimbitaḥ |
anupalakṣyasitāṃśutayā mukhaṃ
nijasakhaṃ sukhamaṅkamṛgārpaṇāt ||26||
virahatāpini candanapāṃśubhiḥ
vapuṣi sārpitapāṇḍimamaṇḍanā |
viṣadharābhabisābharaṇā dadhe
ratipatiṃ prati śaṃbhuvibhīṣikām ||27||
vinihitaṃ paritāpini candanaṃ
hṛdi tayā dhṛtabudbudamābabhau |
upanamansuhṛdaṃ hṛdayeśayaṃ
vidhurivāṅkagatoḍuparigrahaḥ ||28||
smarahutāśanadīpitayā tayā
bahu muhuḥ sarasaṃ sarasīruham |
śrayitumardhapathe kṛtamantarā
śvasitanirmitamarmaramujjhitam ||29||
priyakaragrahamevamavāpsyati
snanayugaṃ tava tāmyati kiṃ nviti |
jagadaturnihite hṛdi nīraje
davathukuṅmalena pṛthustanīm ||30||
tvaditaro na hṛdāpi mayā dhṛtaḥ
patiritīva nalaṃ hṛdayeśayam |
smarahavirbhuji bodhayati sma sā
virahapāṇḍutayā nijaśuddhatām ||31||
virahataptatadaṅganiveśitā
kamalinī nimiṣaddalamuṣṭibhiḥ |
kimapanetumaceṣṭata kiṃ parābhavitumaihata taddavathuṃ pṛthum ||32||
iyamanaṅgaśarāvalipannagakṣatavisāriviyogaviṣāvaśā |
śaśikaleva kharāṃśukarārditā
karuṇanīranidhau nidadhau na kam ||33||
jvalati manmathavedanayā nije
hṛdi tayārdramṛṇālalatārpitā |
svajayinostrapayā savidhasthayoḥ
malinatāmabhajadbhujayorbhṛśam ||34||
pikalateśrutikampini śaivalaṃ
hṛdi tayā nihitaṃ vicaladbabhau |
satatatadgatahṛcchayaketunā
hṛtamiva svatanūghanagharṣiṇā ||35||
na khalu mohavaśena tadānanaṃ
nalamanaḥ śaśikāntamabodhi tat |
itarathā rāśino'bhyudaye tataḥ
kathamasusruvadasrumayaṃ payaḥ ||36||
ratipatervijayāstramiṣuryathā
jayati bhīmasutāpi tathaiva sā |
svaviśikhāniva pañcatayā tato
niyatamaihata yojayituṃ sa tām ||37||
śaśimayaṃ dahanāstramuditvaraṃ
manasijasya vimṛśya viyoginī |
jhaṭiti vāruṇamaśrumiṣādasau
taducitaṃ pratiśastramupādade ||38||
atanunā navamambudamāmbudaṃ
sutanurastramudastamavekṣya sā |
ucitamāyataniḥśvasitacchalācchsanaśastramamuñcadamuṃ prati ||39||
ratipatigrahitānilahetitāṃ
pratiyatī sudatī malayānile |
tadurutāpabhayāttamṛṇālikāmayamiyaṃ bhujagāstramivādita ||40||
nyadhita taddhṛdi śalyamiva dvayaṃ
virahitāṃ ca tathāpi ca jīvitam |
kimatha tatra nihatya nikhātavāvratipatiḥ stanabilvayugena tat ||41||
atiśaravyayatā madanena tāṃ
nikhilapuṣpamayasvaraśaravyayāt |
sphuṭamakāri phalānyapi muñcatā
tadurasi stanatālayugārpaṇam ||42||
atha muhurbahuninditacandrayā
stutavidhuṃtudayā ca tayā muhuḥ |
patitayā smaratāpamaye gade
nijagade'śruvimiśramukhī sakhī ||43||
narasurābjabhuvāmiva yāvatā
bhavati yasya yugaṃ yadanehasā |
virahiṇāmapi tadgatavadyuvakṣaṇamitaṃ na kathaṃ gaṇitāgame ||44||
janudhatta satī smaratāpitā
himavato na tu tanmahimādṛtā |
jvalati bhālatale likhitaḥ satīviraha eva harasya na locanam ||45||
dahanajā na pṛthurdavathuvyathā
virahajaiva pṛthuryadi nedṛśam |
dahanamāśu viśanti kathaṃ striyaḥ
priyamapāsumupāsitumuddhurāḥ ||46||
hṛdi luṭhanti kalā nitarāmamūrvirahiṇīvadhapaṅkakalaṅkitāḥ |
kumudasakhyakṛtastu bahiṣkṛtāḥ
sakhi ! vilokaya durvinayaṃ vidhoḥ ||47||
ayi ! vidhuṃ paripṛccha guroḥ
kutaḥ sphuṭamaśikṣyata dāhavadānyatā |
glapitaśaṃbhugalādgaralāttvayā
kimudadhau jaḍa ! vā vaḍavānalāt ||48||
ayamayogivabhūvadhapātakaiḥ
bhramimavāpya divaḥ khalu pātyate |
śitiniśādṛṣadi sphuṭadutpatatkaṇagaṇādhikatārakitāmbaraḥ ||49||
tvamabhidhehi vidhuṃ sakhi ! madgirā
kimidamīdṛgadhikriyate tvayā |
na gaṇitaṃ yadi janma payonidhau
haraśiraḥsthitibhūrapi vismṛtā ||50||
nipatatāpi na mandarabhūbhṛtā
tvamudadhau śaśalācchana ! cūrṇitaḥ |
api munerjaṭharārciṣi jīrṇatāṃ
vata gato'si na pītapayonidheḥ ||51||
kimasubhirglapitairjaḍa ! manyase
mayi nimajjatu bhīmasutāmanaḥ |
mama kila śrutimāha tadarthikāṃ
nalamukhenduparāṃ vibudhaḥ smaraḥ ||52||
mukharaya svayaśonavaḍiṇḍimaṃ
jalanidheḥ kulamujjvalayādhunā |
api gṛhāṇa vadhūvadhapauruṣaṃ
hariṇalāñchana ! muñca kadarthanām ||53||
niśi śaśin ! bhaja kaitavabhānutāmasati bhāsvati tāpaya pāpa ! mām |
ahamahanyavalokayitāsmi te
punaḥ aharpatinihnutadarpatām ||54||
śaśakalaṅka ! bhayaṃkara ! mādṛśāṃ
jvalasi yanniśi bhūpatiṃ śritaḥ |
tadbhṛtasya tavedṛśabhūtatād
bhutakarī paramūrdhavidhūnanī ||55||
śravaṇapūratamāladalāṅkuraṃ
śaśikuraṅgamukhe sakhi ! nikṣipa |
kimapi tundilitaḥ sthagayatyamuṃ
sapadi tena taducchasimi kṣaṇam ||56||
asamaye mati runmiṣati dhruvaṃ
karagataiva gatā yadiyaṃ kuhūḥ |
punarupaiti nirudhyanivāsyate
sakhi ! mukhaṃ na vidhoḥ punarīkṣyate ||57||
ayi ! mamaiṣa cakoraśiśurmuneḥ
vrajati sindhupibasya na śiṣyatām |
aśitumabdhimadhītavato'sya ca
śaśikarāḥ pibataḥ kati śīkarāḥ ||58||
kuru kare gurumevakamayoghanaṃ
bahirato mukuraṃ ca kuruṣva me |
viśati tatra yadaiva vidhustadā
sakhi ! sukhādahitaṃ jahi taṃ drutam ||59||
udara eva dhṛtaḥ kimudanvatā
na viṣamo vaḍavānalavadvidhuḥ |
viṣavadujjhitamapyamunā na sa
smaraharaḥ kimamuṃ bubhuje vibhuḥ ||60||
asitamekasurāśitamapyabhūnna
punareṣa vidhurviśadaṃ viṣam |
api nipīya surairjanitakṣayaṃ
svayamudeti punarnavamārṇavam ||61||
virahivargavadhavyasanākulaṃ
kalaya pāpamaśeṣakalaṃ vidhum |
suranipītasudhākarapāpakaṃ
grahavido viparītakathāḥ katham ||62||
virahibhirbahumānamavāpi yaḥ
sa bahulaḥ khalu pakṣa ihājani |
tadamitiḥ sakalairapi yatra taiḥ
vyaraci sā ca tithiḥ kimamākṛtā ||63||
svariputīkṣṇasudarśanavibhramāt
kimu vidhuṃ grasate sa vidhuṃtudaḥ |
nipatitaṃ vadane kathamanyathā
balikarambhanibhaṃ nijamujjhati ||64||
vadanagarbhagataṃ na nijecchayā
śaśinamujjhati rāhurasaṃśayam |
aśita eva galatyayamatyayaṃ
sakhi ! vinā galanālabilādhvanā ||65||
ṛjudṛśaḥ kathayanti purāvido
madhubhidaṃ kila rāhuśiraśchidam |
virahimūrdhamidaṃ nigadanti na
kva nu śaśī yadi tajjaṭharānalaḥ ||66||
smarasakhau rucibhiḥ smaravairiṇā
makhamṛgasya yathā dalitaṃ śiraḥ |
sapadi saṃdadhaturbhiṣajau divaḥ
sakhi ! tathā tamaso'pi karotu kaḥ ||67||
nalavimastakitasya raṇe ripoḥ
milati kiṃ na kabandhagalena vā |
mṛtibhiyā bhṛśamutpatatastam
āgrahaśirastadasṛgdṛḍhabandhanam ||68||
sakhi ! jarāṃ paripṛccha tamaḥ śiraḥ
samamasau dadhatāpi kabandhatām |
magadharājavapurdalayugmavatkim
iti na vyatisīvyati ketunā ||69||
vada vidhuṃtudamāli ! madīritaiḥ
tyajasi kiṃ dvijarājadhiyā ripum |
kimu divaṃ punareti yadīdṛśaḥ
patita eṣa niṣevya hi vāruṇīm ||70||
dahati kaṇṭhamayaṃ khalu tena kiṃ
garuḍavaddvijavāsanayojjhitaḥ |
prakṛtirasya vidhuṃtuda ! dāhikā
mayi nirāgasi kā vada vipratā ||71||
sakalayā kalayā kila daṃṣṭrayā
samavadhāya yamāya vinirmitaḥ |
virahiṇīgaṇacarvaṇasādhanaṃ
vidhurato dvijarāja iti śrutiḥ ||72||
smaramukhaṃ haranetrahutāśanāj
jvaladidaṃ cakṛṣe vidhinā vidhuḥ |
bahuvidhena viyogivadhainasā
śaśamiṣādatha kālikayāṅkitaḥ ||73||
(dvijapatigrasanāhitapātaka
prabhavakuṣṭhasitīkṛtavigrahaḥ |
virahiṇīvadanendujighatsayā
sphurati rāhurayaṃ na niśākaraḥ ||1||)
iti vidhorvividhoktivagarhaṇaṃ
vyavahitasya vṛtheti vimṛśya sā |
atitarāṃ dadhatī virahajvaraṃ
hṛdayabhājamupālabhata smaram ||74||
hṛdayamāśrayase yadi mamākaṃ
jvalayasītthamanaṅga ! tadeva kim |
svayamapi kṣaṇadagdhanijendhanaḥ kva
bhavitāsi hatārā ! hutāśavat ||75||
purabhidā gamitastvamadṛśyatāṃ
trinayanatvapariplutiśaṅkayā |
smara ! niraiṣyata kasyacanāpi na
tvayi kimakṣigate nayanaistribhiḥ ||76||
sahacaro'si rateriti viśrutiḥ
tvayi vasatyapi me na ratiḥ kutaḥ |
atha na saṃprati saṃgatirasti vāmanumṛtā na bhavantamiyaṃ kila ||77||
rativiyuktamanātmaparajña ! kiṃ
svamiva māmapi tāpitavānasi |
kathamatāpabhṛtastava saṃgamāditarathā hṛdayaṃ mama dahyate ||78||
anumamāra na māra ! kathaṃ nu sā
ratiratiprathitāpi pativratā |
iyadanāthavadhūvadhapātakī
dayitayāpi tayāsi kimujjhitaḥ ||79||
sugata eva vijitya jitendriyaḥ
tvadurukīrtitanuṃ yadanāśayat |
tava tanūmavaśiṣṭavatīṃ tataḥ
samiti bhūtamayīmaharaddharaḥ ||80||
phalamalabhyata yatkusumaistvayā
viṣamanetramanaṅga ! vigṛhṇatā |
ahada nītiravāptabhayā tato
na kusumairapi vigrahamicchati ||81||
api dhayannitarāmaravatsudhāṃ
trinayanātkathamāpitha tāṃ daśām |
bhaṇa rateradharasya rasādarāt
amṛtamāptaghṛṇaḥ khalu nāpibaḥ ||82||
bhuvanamohanajena kimenasā
tava babhūva pareta ! piśācatā |
yadadhunā virahādhamalīmasām
abhibhavanbhramasi smara ! madvidhām ||83||
bata dadāsi na mṛtyumapi smara !
skhalati te'kṛpayā na dhanuḥ karāt |
atha mṛto'si mṛtena ca mucyate
na khalu muṣṭi rurīkṛtabandhanaḥ ||84||
dṛgupahatyapamṛtyuvirūpatāḥ
śamayate'paranirjarasevitā |
atiśayāndhyavapuḥ kṣatipāṇḍutāḥ
smara ! bhavanti bhavantyamupāsituḥ ||85||
smara ! nṛśaṃsatamastvamato vidhiḥ
sumanasaḥ kṛtavānbhavadāyudham |
yadi dhanurdṛḍhamāśugamāyasaṃ
tava sṛjettrijagatpralayaṃ vrajet ||86||
smarariporiva ropaśikhī purāṃ
dahatu te jagatāmapi mā trayam |
iti vidhistvadiṣūnkusumāni kiṃ
madhubhirantarasiñcadanirvṛtaḥ ||87||
vidhiranaṃśamamedyamavekṣya te
janamanaḥ khalu lakṣamakalpayat |
api sa vajramadāsyata cettadā
tvadiṣubhirvadaliṣyadasāvapi ||88||
api vidhiḥ kusumāni tavāśugān
smara ! vidhāya na nirvṛtimāptavān |
adita pañca hi te sa niyamya tāṃ
stadapi tairbata jarjaritaṃ jagat ||89||
upaharanti na kasya suparvaṇaḥ
sumanasaḥ kati pañca suradrumāḥ |
tava tu hīnatayā pṛthagekikāṃ
dhigiyatāpi na te'ṅga ! vigarhaṇā ||90||
kusumamapyatidurṇayakāri te
kimu vitīrya dhanurvidhiragrahīt |
kimakṛtaiṣa tavaikatadāspade
dvayamabhūdadhunā hi nalabhruvau ||91||
ṣaḍṭatavaḥ kṛpayā svakamekakaṃ
kusumamakramananditanandanāḥ |
dadati ṣaḍbhavate kurute bhavān
dhanurivaikamiṣūnivapañca taiḥ ||92||
yadatanustvamidaṃ jagate hitaṃ
kva sa munistava yaḥ sahate hatīḥ |
viśikhamāśravaṇaṃ paripūrṇa ced
avicaladbhutamujjhitumīśiṣe ||93||
saha tayā smara ! bhasma jhaṭityabhūḥ
paśupatiṃ prati yāmiṣumagrahīḥ |
dhruvamabhūdadhunā vitanoḥ śaraḥ
tava pikasvara eva sa pañcamaḥ ||94||
smara ! sa madduritairaphalīkṛto
bhagavato'pi bhavaddahanaśramaḥ |
surahitāya hutātmatanuḥ punaḥ
nanu janurdivi tatkṣaṇamāpitha ||95||
virahiṇo vimukhasya vidhūdaye
śamanadikpavanaḥ sa na dakṣiṇaḥ |
sumanaso namayannaṭanau dhanuḥ
tava tu bāhurasau yadi dakṣiṇaḥ ||96||
kimu bhavantamumāpatirekakaṃ
madamudāndhamayogijanāntakam |
yadajayattata eva na gīyate
sa bhagavānmadanāndhakamṛtyujit ||97||
tvamiva ko'pi parāpakṛtau kṛtī
na dadṛśe na ca manmatha ! śuśruve |
svamadaho dahanājjvalatātmanā
jvalayituṃ parirabhya jaganti yaḥ ||98||
tvamucitaṃ nayanārciṣi śaṃbhunā
bhuvanaśāntikahomahaviḥ kṛtaḥ |
tava vayasyamapāsya madhuṃ madhuṃ
hatavatā hariṇā bata kiṃ kṛtam ||99||
iti kiyadvacasaiva bhṛśaṃ priyādharapipāsu tadānanamāśu tat |
ajani pāṃśumapriyavāgjvalan
madanaśoṣaṇabāṇahateriva ||100||
priyasakhīnivahena sahātha sā
vyaracayadgiramardhasamasyayā |
hṛdayamarmaṇi manmathasāyakaiḥ
kṣatatamā bahu bhāṣitumakṣamā ||101||
akaruṇādava sūnaśarādasūn
sahajayāpadi dhīratayātmanaḥ |
asava eva mamādya virodhinaḥ
kathamarīnsakhi ! rakṣitumāttha mām ||102||
hitagiraṃ na śṛṇoṣi kimāśrave !
prasabhamapyava jīvitamātmanaḥ |
sakhi ! hitā yadi me bhavasīdṛśī
madarimicchasi yā mama jīvitam ||103||
amṛtadīdhitireṣa vidarbhaja !
bhajasi tāpamamuṣya kimaṃśubhiḥ |
yadi bhavanti mṛtāḥ sakhi ! candrikāḥ
śaśamṛtaḥ kva tadā paritapyate ||104||
vraja dhṛtiṃ tyaja bhītimahetukām
ayamacaṇḍamarīcirudañcati |
jvalayati sphuṭamātapamurmuraiḥ
anubhavaṃ vacasā sakhi ! lumpasi ||105||
ayi ! śape hṛdayāya tavaiva tad
yadi vadhorna rucerasi gocaraḥ |
ruciphalaṃ sakhi ! dṛśyata eva taj
jvalayati tvacamullalayatyasūn ||106||
vidhuvirodhititherabhidhāyinīṃ
nanu na kiṃ punaricchasi kokilām |
sakhi ! kimarthagaveṣaṇayā giraṃ
kirati seyamanarthamayīṃ mayi ||107||
hṛdaya eva tavāsti sa vallabhaḥ
tadapi kiṃ damayanti ! viṣīdasi |
hṛdi paraṃ na bahiḥ khalu vartate
sakhi ! payastata eva viṣadyate ||108||
sphuṭati hāramaṇau madanoṣmaṇā
hṛdayamapyanalaṃkṛtamadya te |
sakhi ! hatāsmi tadā yadi hṛdyapi
priyatamaḥ sa mama vyavadhāpitaḥ ||109||
idamudīrya tadaiva mumūrccha sā
manasi mūrcchitamanmathapāvakā |
kva sahatāmavalambalavacchidām
anupapattimatīmatiduḥkhitā ||110||
adhita kāpi mukhe salilaṃ sakhī
pyadhita kāpi sarojadalaiḥ stanau |
vyadhita kāpi hṛdi vyajanānilaṃ
nyadhita kāpi himaṃ sutanaustanau ||111||
upacacāra ciraṃ mṛduśītalaiḥ
jalajanālamṛṇālajalādibhiḥ |
priyasakhīnivahaḥ sa tathā kramād
iyamavāpa yathā laghu cetanām ||112||
atha kale ! kalaya śvasiti sphuṭaṃ
calati pakṣma cale ! paribhāvaya |
adharakampanamunnaya menake !
kimapi jalpati kalpalate ! śrṛṇu ||113||
racaya cārumati ! stanayorvṛtiṃ
kalaya keśini ! kaiśyamasaṃyatam |
avagṛhāṇa taraṅgiṇi ! netrayoḥ
jalajharāviti śuśruvire giraḥ ||114||
kalakalaḥ sa tadālijanānanād
udalasadvipulastvariteritaiḥ |
yamadhigamya sutālayamīyivān
dhṛtadaraḥ sa vidarbhapuraṃdaraḥ ||115||
kanyāntaḥ purabādhanāya yadadhākārānna doṣā nṛpaṃ
dvau mantripravaraśca tulyamagadaṃkāraśca tāvūcatuḥ |
devākarṇaya suśrutena carakasyoktena jāne'khilaṃ
syādasyā naladaṃ vinā na dalane tāpasya ko'pi kṣamaḥ ||116||
tābhyāmabhūdyugapadapyabhidhīyamānaṃ
bhedavyapākṛti mithaḥpratighātameva |
śrotre tu tasya papaturnṛpaterna kiṃcid
bhaimyāmaniṣṭaśataśaṅkitayākulasya ||117||
drutavigamitaviprayogacihnāmapi
tanayāṃ nṛpatiḥ padapraṇamrām |
akalayadasamāśugādhimagnāṃ
jhaṭiti parāśayavedino hi vijñāḥ ||118||
vyataradatha pitāśiṣaṃ sutāyai
nataśirase sahasonnamayya maulim |
dayitamabhimataṃ svayaṃvare ! tvaṃ guṇam
ayamāpnuhi vāsaraiḥ kiyadbhiḥ ||119||
tadanu sa tanujāsakhīravādīt
tuhinaṛtau gata eva hīdṛśīnām |
kusumamapi śarāyate śarīre tad
ucitamācaratopacāramasyāḥ ||120||
katipayadivasairvayasyayā vaḥ
svayamabhilaṣya variṣyate varīyān |
kraśimaśamanayānayā tadāptuṃ rucirucitātha bhavadvidhāvidhābhiḥ ||121||
evaṃ yadvadatā nṛpeṇa tanayā nāpṛcchi lajjāspadaṃ
yanmohaḥ smarabhūrakalpi vapuṣaḥ pāṇḍutvatāpādibhiḥ |
paccāśīḥ kapaṭādavādi sadṛśī syāttatra yā sāntvanā
tanmatyālijano manobdhimatanodānandamandākṣayoḥ ||122||
śrīharṣaṃ kavirājarājimukuṭālaṃkārahīraḥ sutaṃ
śrīhīraḥ suṣube jitendriyacayaṃ māmalladevī ca yam |
turyaḥ sthairyavicāraṇaprakaraṇabhrātaryayaṃ tanmahākāvye'tra vyagalannalasya carite sargo nisargojjvalaḥ ||123||

 

pañcama sargaḥ
yāvadāgamayate'tha narendrānsa
svayaṃvaramahāya mahīndraḥ |
tāvadeva ṛṣirindradidṛkṣuḥ
nāradastridaśadhāma jagāma ||1||
nātra citramanu taṃ prayayau
yatparvataḥ sa khalu tasya sapakṣaḥ |
nāradastu jagato gururuccairvismayāya gaganaṃ vilalaṅghe ||2||
gacchatā pathi vinaiva vimānaṃ
vyoma tena muninā vijagāhe |
sādhane hi niyamo'nyajanānāṃ
yogināṃ tu tapasākhilasiddhiḥ ||3||
khaṇḍitendrabhavanādyabhimānāllaṅghate sma munireṣa vimānān |
arthito'pyatithitāmanumene
naiva tatpatibhiraṅghrivinamraiḥ ||4||
tasya tāpanabhiyā tapanaḥ svaṃ
tāvadeva samakocayadarciḥ |
yāvadeva divasena śaśīva
drāgatapyata na tanmahasaiva ||5||
paryabhūddinamaṇirdvijarājaṃ
yatkarairahaha tena tadā tam |
paryabhūtkhalu karairdvijarājaḥ
karma kaḥ svakṛtamatra na bhuṅkte ||6||
viṣṭaraṃ taṭakuśālibhiraddhiḥ
pādyamardhyamatha kaccharuhābhiḥ |
padmavṛndamadhubhirmadhuparkaṃ
svargasindhuraditātithaye'smai ||7||
sa vyatītya viyadantaragādhaṃ
nākanāyakaniketanamāpa |
saṃpratīrya bhavasindhumanārdi
brahma śarmabharacāru yatīva ||8||
arcanābhirucitoccatarābhiḥ
cāru taṃ sadakṛtātithimindraḥ |
yāvadarhakaraṇaṃ kila sādhoḥ
pratyavāyadhutaye na guṇāya ||9||
nāmadheyasamatāsakhamadreradribhinmunimathādriyata drāk |
parvato'pi labhatāṃ kathamarcāṃ
na dvijaḥ sa vibudhaprabhulambhī ||10||
tadbhujādativitīrṇasaparyād
dayudrumānapi viveda munīndraḥ |
svaḥ sahasthitisuśikṣitayā
tāndānapāramitayaiva vadānyān ||11||
mudritānyajanasaṃkathanaḥ san
nāradaṃ balaripuḥ samavādīt |
ākaraḥ svaparabhūrikathānāṃ
prāyaśo hi suhṛdoḥ sahavāsaḥ ||12||
taṃ kathānukathanaprasṛtāyāṃ
dūramālapanakautukitāyām |
bhūbhṛtāṃ ciramanāgatihetuṃ
jñātumicchuravadacchatamanyuḥ ||13||
prāgiva prasuvate nṛpavaṃśāḥ
kiṃnu saṃprati na vīrakarīrān |
ye parapraharaṇaiḥ pariṇāme
vikṣatāḥ kṣititale nipatanti ||14||
pārthivaṃ hi nijamājiṣu vīrā
dūramūrdhvagamanasya virodhi |
gauravādvapurapāsya bhajante
matkṛtāmatithigauravaṛddhim ||15||
sābhiśāpamiva nātithayaste
māṃ yadadya bhagavannupayanti |
tena na śriyamimāṃ bahu manye
svodaraikabhṛtikāryakadaryām ||16||
pūrvapuṇyavibhavavyayalabdhāḥ
śrībharā vipada eva vimṛṣṭāḥ |
pātrapāṇikamalārpaṇamāsāṃ
tāsu śāntikavidhirvidhidṛṣṭaḥ ||17||
tadvimṛjya mama saṃśayaśilpi sphītamatra viṣaye sahasāgham |
bhūyatāṃ bhagavataḥ śrutisāraiḥ
adya vāgbhiraghamarṣaṇaṛgbhiḥ ||18||
ityudīrya maghavā vinayardhiṃ
vardhayannavahitatvabhareṇa |
cakṣuṣāṃ daśaśatīmanimeṣāṃ
tasthivānmunimukhe praṇidhāya ||19||
vīkṣya tasya vinaye paripākaṃ
pākaśāsanapadaṃ spṛśato'pi |
nāradaḥ pramadagadgadayoktyā
vismitaḥ smitapuraḥ saramūce ||20||
bhikṣitā śatamakhī sukṛtaṃ yat
tatpariśramavidaḥ svavibhūtau |
tatphale yadi paraṃ tava helā
kleśalabdhamadhikādaradaṃ tu ||21||
saṃsapadastava girāmapi dūrā
yanna nāma vinayaṃ vinayante |
śraddadhāti ka iveha na sākṣād
āha cedanubhavaḥ paramāptaḥ ||22||
śrībharānatithisātkaravāṇi
svopabhogaparatā na hiteti |
paśyato bahirivāntarapīyaṃ
dṛṣṭisṛṣṭiradhikā tava kāpi ||23||
āḥ svabhāvamadhurairanubhāvaiḥ
tāvakairatitarāṃ taralāḥ smaḥ |
dyāṃ praśādhi galitāvadhikālaṃ
sādhu sādhu vijayasva biḍaujaḥ ||24||
saṃkhyavikṣatatanusravadasrakṣālitākhilanijāghalaghūnām |
yattvihānupagamaḥ śṛṇu rājñāṃ
tajjagadyuvamudaṃ tamudantam ||25||
sā bhuvaḥ kimapi ratnamanarghe
bhūṣaṇaṃ jayati tatra kumārī |
bhīmabhūpatanayā damayantī
nāma yā madanaśastramamogham ||26||
saṃprati pratimuhūrtamapūrvā
kāpi yauvanajavena bhavantī |
āśikhaṃ sukṛtasārabhṛte sā
kvāpi yūni bhajate kila bhāvam ||27||
kathyate na katamaḥ sa iti tvaṃ
māṃ vivakṣurasi kiṃ caladoṣṭhaḥ |
ardhavartmani ruṇatsi na pṛcchāṃ
nirgameṇa na pariśramayainām ||28||
yatpathāvadhiraṇuḥ paramaḥ sā
yogidhīrapi na paśyati yasmāt |
bālayā nijamanaḥ paramāṇau
hrīdarīśayaharīkṛtamenam ||29||
sā śarasya kusumasya śaravyaṃ
sūcitā virahavācibharaṅgaiḥ |
tātacittamapi dhāturadhatta svasvayaṃvaramahāya sahāyam ||30||
manmathāya yadathādita rājñāṃ
hūtidūtyavidhaye vidhirājñām |
tena tatparavaśāḥ pṛthivīśāḥ
saṃgaraṃ garamivākalayanti ||31||
yeṣu yeṣu sarasā damayantī
bhūṣaṇeṣu yadi vāpi guṇeṣu |
tatra tatra kalayāpi viśeṣo yaḥ
sa hi kṣitibhṛtāṃ puruṣārthaḥ ||32||
śaiśavavyayadināvadhi tasyā
yauvanodayini rājasamāje |
ādarādaharahaḥ kusumeṣoḥ
ullalāsa mṛgayābhiniveśaḥ ||33||
ityamī vasumatīkamitāraḥ
sādarāstvadatithībhavituṃ na |
bhīmabhūsurabhuvorabhilāpe
dūramantaramaho nṛpatīnām ||34||
tena jāgradadhṛtirdivamāgāṃ
saṃkhyasaukhyamanusartumanu tvām |
yanmṛdhaṃ kṣitibhṛtāṃ na viloke
tannimagnamanasāṃ bhuvi loke ||35||
veda yadyapi na ko'pi bhavantaṃ
hanta hantrakaruṇaṃ viruṇaddhi |
pṛcchayase tadapi yena vivakaproñchanāya viṣaye rasasekaḥ ||36||
evamuktavati devaṛṣīndre
drāgabhedi maghavānanamudrā |
uttarottaraśubho hi vibhūnāṃ
ko'pi mañjulatamaḥ kramavādaḥ ||37||
kānuje mama nije danujārau
jāgrati svaśaraṇe raṇacarcā |
yadbhujāṅkamupadhāya jayāṅkaṃ
śarmaṇā svapimi vītaviśaṅkaḥ ||38||
viśvarūpakalanādupapannaṃ
tasya jaiminimunitvamudīye |
vigrahaṃ makhabhujāmasahiṣṇuḥ
vyarthatāṃ madaśaniṃ sa nināya ||39||
īdṛśāni munaye vinayābdhiḥ
tasthivān sa vacanānyupahṛtya |
prāṃśuniḥśvasitapṛṣṭhacarī vāṅ
nāradasya niriyāya nirojāḥ ||40||
svārasātalabhavāhavaśaṅkī
nirvṛṇomi na vasanvasumatyām |
dyāṃ gatasya hṛdi me durudarkaḥ
kṣmātaladvayabhaṭājivitarkaḥ ||41||
vīkṣitastvamasi māmatha gantuṃ
tanmanuṣyajagate'numanuṣva |
kiṃ bhuvaḥ parivṛḍhā na vivoḍhuṃ
tatra tāmupagatā vivadante ||42||
ityudīrya sa yayau munirurvo
svarpatiṃ pratinivartya balena |
vārito'pyanujagāma sayatnaṃ
taṃ kiyantyapi padānyaparāṇi ||43||
pāṇaye balariporatha bhaimīśītakomalakaragrahamarham |
bheṣajaṃ ciracitāśanivāsavyāpadāmupadideśa ratīśaḥ ||45||
nākalokabhiṣajoḥ suṣamā yā
puṣpacāpamapi cumbati saiva |
vedmi tādṛgabhiṣajyadasau taddvārasaṃkramitavaidyakavidyaḥ ||46||
mānuṣīmanusaratyatha patyau
kharvabhāvamavalambya maghonī |
khaṇḍitaṃ nijamasūcayaduccaiḥ
mānamānanasaroruhanatyā ||47||
yo maghoni divamuccaramāṇe
rambhayā malinimālamalambhi |
varṇa eva sa khalūjjvalamasyāḥ
śāntamāntaramabhāṣata bhaṅgyā ||48||
jīvitena kṛtamapsarasāṃ tat
prāṇamuktiriha yuktimatī naḥ |
ityanakṣaramavāci dhṛtācyā
dīrghaniḥśvasitanirgamitena ||49||
sādhu naḥ patanamevamitaḥ syāt
ityabhaṇyata tilottamayāpi |
cāmarasya patanena karābjāt
tadvilolanacaladbhujanālāt ||51||
urvaśī guṇavaśīkṛtaviśvā
tatkṣaṇastimitabhāvanibhena |
śakrasauhṛdasamāpanasīmaḥ
tambhakāryamapuṣadvapuṣaiva ||52||
kāpi kāmapi babhāṇa bubhutsuṃ
śṛṇvati tridaśabhartari kiṃcit |
eṣa kaśyapasutāmabhigantā
paśya kaśyapasutaḥ śatamanyuḥ ||53||
ālimātmasubhagatvasagarvā
kāpi śṛṇvati maghoni babhāṣe |
vīkṣaṇe'pi saghṛṇāsi nṛṇāṃ
kiṃ yāsi na tvamapi sārthaguṇena ||54||
anvayurdyutipayaḥ pitṛnāthāḥ
taṃmudātha haritāṃ kamitāraḥ |
vartma karṣatu puraḥ paramekastad
gatānugatiko na mahārghaḥ ||55||
preṣitāḥ pṛthagatho damayantyai
cittacauryacaturā nijadūtyaḥ |
tahuruṃ prati ca tairupahārāḥ
saṃkhyasaukhyakapaṭena nigūḍhāḥ ||56||
citramatra vibudhairapi yattaiḥ
svarvihāya bata bhūranusasre |
dyaurna kācidathavāsti niruḍhā
saiva sā calati yatra hi cittam ||57||
kiṃ ghanasya jaladherathavaivaṃ
naiva saṃśayitumapyalabhanta |
syandanaṃ paramadūramapaśyan
niḥsvanaśrutisahopanataṃ te ||59||
sūtaviśramadakautukibhāvaṃ
bhāvabodhacaturaṃ turagāṇām |
tatra netrajanuṣaḥ phalamete
naiṣadhaṃ bubudhire vibudhendrāḥ ||60||
vīkṣya tasya varuṇastaruṇatvaṃ
tadbabhāra nibiḍaṃ jaḍabhūyam |
naucitī jalapateḥ kimu sāsya
prājyavismayarasastimitasya ||61||
rūpamasya vinirūpya tathātim
lānimāpa ravivaṃśavataṃsaḥ |
kīrtyate yadadhunāpi sa devaḥ
kāla eva sakalena janena ||62||
yaṃ babhāra dahanaḥ khalu tāpaṃ
rūpadheyabharamasya vimṛśya |
tatra bhūdanalatā janikartrī mā
tadapyanalataiva tu hetuḥ ||63||
kāmanīyakamadhaḥ kṛtakāmaṃ
kāmamakṣibhiravekṣya tadīyam |
kauśikaḥ svamakhilaṃ paripaśyan
manyate sma khalu kauśikameva ||64||
rāmaṇīyakaguṇādvayavādaṃ
mūrtamutthitamamuṃ paribhāvya |
vismayāya hṛdayāni viteruḥ
tena teṣu na surāḥ prababhūvuḥ ||65||
praiyarūpakaviśeṣaniveśaiḥ
saṃvadadbhiramarāḥ śrutapūrvaiḥ |
eṣa eva sa nalaḥ kimitīdaṃ
mandamandamitaretaramūcuḥ ||66||
teṣu tadvidhavadhūvaraṇārhaṃ
bhūṣaṇaṃ sa samayaḥ sa rathādhvā |
tasya kuṇḍinapuraṃ pratisarpan
bhūpatervyavasitāni śaśaṃsuḥ ||67||
dharmarājasalileśahutāśaiḥ
prāṇātāṃ śritamamuṃ jagatastaiḥ |
prāpya hṛṣṭacalavistṛtatāpaiḥ
cetasā nibhṛtametadacinti ||68||
naiva naḥ priyatamobhayathāsau
yadyamuṃ na vṛṇute vṛṇute vā |
ekato hi dhigamūmaguṇajñam
anyataḥ kathamadaḥ pratilambhaḥ ||69||
māṃ variṣyati tadā yadi mat
to veda neyamiyadasya mahattvam |
īdṛśī ca kathamākalayitrī
madviśeṣamaparānnṛpaputrī ||70||
naiṣadhe bata vṛte damayantyā
vrīḍito nahi bahirbhavitāsmi |
svāṃ gṛhe'pi vanitāṃ kathamāsyaṃ
hrīnimīli khalu darśayitāhe ||71||
ityavetya manasātmavidheyaṃ
kiṃcana trivibudhī bubudhe na |
nākanāyakamapāsya tamekaṃ
sā sma paśyati parasparamāsyam ||72||
kiṃ vidheyamadhuneti vimugdhaṃ
svānugānanamavekṣya ṛbhukṣāḥ |
śaṃsati sma kapaṭe paṭuruccaiḥ
vañcanaṃ samabhilaṣya nalasya ||73||
sarvataḥ kuśalavānasi kiccitvaṃ
sa naiṣadha iti pratibhā naḥ |
svāsanārdhasuhṛdastvayi
rekhāṃ vīrasenanṛpateriva vidmaḥ ||74||
kva prayāsyasi naletyalamuktvā
yātrayātra śubhayājani yannaḥ |
tattayaiva phalasatvarayā tvaṃ
nādhvano'rdhamidamāgamitaḥ kim ||75||
eṣa naiṣadha ! sa daṇḍabhṛdeṣa
jvālajālajaṭilaḥ sa hutāśaḥ |
yādasāṃ sa patireṣa ca śeṣaṃ
śāsitāramadhigaccha surāṇām ||76||
arthito vayamamī samupemaḥ
tvāṃ naleti phalitārthamavehi |
adhvanaḥ kṣaṇamapāsya ca khedaṃ
kurmahe bhavati kāryanivedam ||77||
īdṛśīṃ giramudīrya biḍaujā
joṣamāsa na viśiṣya babhāṣe |
nātra citramabhidhākuśalatve
śaiśavāvadhi gururgururasya ||78||
arthināmahṛṣitākhilalomā
svaṃ nṛpaḥ sphuṭakadambakadambam |
arcanārthamiva taccaraṇānāṃ
sa praṇāmakaraṇādupaninye ||79||
durlabhaṃ digadhipaiḥ kimamībhiḥ
