tādṛśaṃ kathamaho madadhīnam |
īdṛśaṃ manasikṛtya virodhaṃ
naiṣadhena samaśāyi cirāya ||80||
jīvitāvadhi vanīyakamātraiḥ
yācyamānamakhilaiḥ sulabhaṃ yat |
arthine parivṛḍhāya surāṇāṃ kiṃ
vitīrya parituṣyati cetaḥ ||81||
bhīmajā ca hṛdi me paramāste
jīvitādapi dhanādapi gurvī |
na svameva mama sārhati yasyāḥ
ṣoḍaśīmapi kalāṃ kila norvī ||82||
mīyatāṃ kathamabhīpsitameṣāṃ
dīyatāṃ kathamayācitameva |
taṃ dhigastu kalayannapi vāñchām
arthivāgavasaraṃ sahate yaḥ ||83||
prāpitena caṭukākuviḍambaṃ
lambhitena bahuyācanalajjām |
arthinā yadaghamarjati dātā
tanna lumpati vilambya dadānaḥ ||84||
yatpradeyamupanīya vadānyaiḥ
dīyate salilamarthijanāya |
sārthanoktiviphalatvaviśaṅkā
trāsamūrcchadapamṛtyucikitsā ||85||
arthine na tṛṇavaddhanamātraṃ kiṃ
tu jīvanamapi pratipādyam |
evamāha kuśavajjaladāpī
davyadānavidhiruktividagdhaḥ ||86||
paṅkasaṃkaravigarhitamarhaṃ
na śriyaḥ kamalamāśrayaṇāya |
arthipāṇikamalaṃ vimalaṃ
tadvāsaveśma vidadhīta sudhīstat ||87||
yācamānajanamānasavṛtteḥ
pūraṇāya bata janma na yasya |
tena bhūmiratibhāravatīyaṃ
na drumairna giribhirna samudraiḥ ||88||
mā dhanāni kṛpaṇaḥ khalu jīvaṃ
stṛṣṇayārpayatu jātu parasmai |
tatra caiṣa kurute mama citraṃ
yattu nārpayati tāni mṛto'pi ||89||
māmamībhiriha yācitavadbhiḥ
dātṛjātamavamatya jagatyām |
yadyaśo mayi nivekṣitametan
niṣkrayo'stu katamastu tadīyaḥ ||90||
loka eṣa paralokamupetā
hā vihāya nidhane dhanamekaḥ |
ityamuṃ khalu tadasya ninīṣatyarthibandhurudayaddayacittaḥ ||91||
dānapātramadharmaṇamihaikagrāhi koṭiguṇitaṃ divi dāyi |
sādhureti sukṛtairyadi kartuṃ
pāralaukikakusīdamasīdat ||92||
evamādi sa vicintya muhūrtaṃ
tānavocata patirniṣadhānām |
arthidurlabhamavāpya saharṣān
yācyamānamukhamullasitaśri ||93||
nāsti janyajanakavyatibhedaḥ
satyamannajanito janadehaḥ |
vīkṣya vaḥ khalu tanūmamṛtādaṃ
dṛṅgimajjanamupaiti sudhāyām ||94||
mattapaḥ kva nu tanu kva phalaṃ vā
yūyamīkṣaṇapathaṃ vrajatheti |
īdṛśaṃ pariṇamanti punarnaḥ
pūrvapūruṣatapāṃsi jayanti ||95||
pratyatiṣṭhipadilāṃ khalu devīṃ
karma sarvasahanavratajanma |
yūyamapyahaha pūjanamasyā
yannijaiḥ sṛjatha pādapayojaiḥ ||96||
jīvitāvadhi kimapyadhikaṃ vā
yanmanīṣitamito naraḍimbhāt |
tena vaścaraṇamarcatu so'yaṃ
brūta vastu punarastu kimīdṛk ||97||
evamuktavati vītaviśaṅke
vīrasenatanaye vinayena |
vakrabhāvaviṣamāmatha śakraḥ
kāryakaitavagururgiramūce ||98||
pāṇipīḍanamahaṃ damayantyāḥ
kāmayemahi mahīmihikāṃśo ! |
dūtyamatra kuru naḥ smarabhītiṃ
nirjitasmara ! cirasya nirasya ||99||
āsate śatamadhikṣiti bhūpāḥ
toyarāśirasi te khalu kūpāḥ |
kiṃ grahā divi na jāgrati te te
bhāskarasya katamastulayāste ||100||
viśvadṛśvanayanā vayameva
tvahguṇāmbudhimagādhamavemaḥ |
tvāmihaivamaniveśya rahasye
nirvṛtiṃ nahi labhemahi sarve ||101||
śuddhavaṃśajanito'pi guṇasya
sthānatāmanubhavannapi śakraḥ |
kṣipnuremṛjumāśu sapakṣaṃ
sāyakaṃ dhanurivājani vakraḥ ||102||
tena tena vacasaiva maghonaḥ
sa sma veda kapaṭaṃ paṭuruccaiḥ |
ācarattaducitāmatha vāṇīmārjavaṃ hi kuṭileṣu na nītiḥ ||103||
seyamuccataratā duritānām
anyajanmani mayaiva kṛtānām |
yuṣmadīyamapi yā mahimanaṃ
jetumicchati kathāpathapāram ||104||
vittha cittamakhilasya na kuryāṃ
dhuryakāryaparipanthi tu maunam |
hrīrgirāstu varamastu punarmā
svīkṛtaiva paravāgaparāstā ||105||
yanmatau vimaladarpaṇikāyāṃ
saṃmukhasthamakhilaṃ khalu tattvam |
te'pi kiṃ vitarathedṛśamājñāṃ
yā na yasya sadṛśī vitarītum ||106||
yāmi yāmiha varītumaho
tadṛtatāṃ nu karavāṇi kathaṃ vaḥ |
īdṛśāṃ na mahatāṃ bata jātā
vañcane mama tṛṇasya ghṛṇāpi ||107||
udbhramāmi virahānmuhurasyā
mohamemi ca muhūrtamahaṃ yaḥ |
bruta vaḥ prabhavitāsmi rahasyaṃ
rakṣituṃ sa kathamīdṛgavasthaḥ ||108||
yāṃ manorathamayīṃ hṛdi kṛtvā
yaḥ śvasimyatha kathaṃ sa tadagre |
bhāvaguptimavalambitumīśe
durjayā hi viṣayā viduṣāpi ||109||
yāmikānanupamṛdya ca mādṛk
tāṃ nirīkṣitumapi kṣamate kaḥ |
rakṣilakṣajayacaṇḍacaritre
puṃsi viśvasiti kutra kumārī ||110||
ādadhīci kila dātṛkṛtārghaṃ
prāṇamātrapaṇasīma yaśo yat |
ādade kathamahaṃ priyayā
tat prāṇataḥ śataguṇena paṇena ||111||
arthanā mayi bhavadbhirivāsyai
kartumarhati mayāpi bhavatsu |
bhīmajārthaparayācanacāṭau
yūyameva guravaḥ karaṇīyāḥ ||112||
arthitāḥ prathamato damayantīṃ
yūyamanvahamupāsya mayā yat |
hrīrna cedvyatiyatāmapi tadvaḥ
sā mamāpi sutarāṃ na tadastu ||113||
kuṇḍinendrasutayā kila pūrvaṃ
māṃ varītumurarīkṛtamāste |
vrīḍameṣyati paraṃ mayi dṛṣṭe
svīkariṣyati na sā khalu yuṣmān ||114||
tatprasīdata vidhatta na khedaṃ
dūtyamatyasadṛśaṃ hi mamedam |
hāsyataiva sulabhā na tu sādhyaṃ
tadvidhitsubhiranaupayikena ||115||
īdṛśāni gaditāni tadānīm
ākalayya sa nalasya balāriḥ |
śaṃsati sma kimapi smayamānaḥ
svānugānanavilokanalolaḥ ||116||
nābhyadhāyi nṛpate !
bhavatedaṃ rohiṇīramaṇavaṃśabhuvaiva |
lajjate na rasanā tava
vāmyādarthiṣu svayamurīkṛtakāmyā ||117||
bhaṅguraṃ na vitathaṃ na kathaṃ vā
jīvalokamavalokayasīmam |
yena dharmayaśasī parihātuṃ
dhīraho calati dhīra ! tavāpi ||118||
kaḥ kule'jani jaganmukuṭe vaḥ
prārthakepsitamapūri na yena |
indurādirajaniṣṭa kalaṅkī
kaṣṭamatra sa bhavānapi mā bhūt ||119||
yāpadṛṣṭirapi yā mukhamudrā
yācamānamanu yā ca na tuṣṭiḥ |
tvādṛśasya sakalaḥ sa kalaṅkaḥ
śītabhāsi śaśakṛḥ paramaṅkaḥ ||120||
nākṣarāṇi paṭhatā kimapāṭhi
prasmṛtaḥ kimathavā paṭhito'pi |
itthamarthicayasaṃśaya dolākhelanaṃ khalu cakāra nakāraḥ ||121||
abravīttamanalaḥ kva naledaṃ
labdhamujjhasi yaśaḥ śaśikalpam |
kalpavṛkṣapatimarthinamitthaṃ
nāpa ko'pi śatamanyumihānyaḥ ||122||
na vyahanyata kadāpi mudaṃ yaḥ
svaḥ sadāmupanayannabhilāṣaḥ |
tatpade tvadabhiṣekakṛtāṃ naḥ
sa tyajatvasamatāmadamadya ||123||
abravīdatha yamastamahṛṣṭaṃ
vīrasenakuladīpa ! tamastvām |
yatkimapyabhibubhūṣati
tatkiṃ candravaṃśavasateḥ sadṛśaṃ te ||124||
rohaṇaḥ kimapi yaḥ kaṭhinānāṃ
kāmadhenurapi yā paśureva |
nainayorapi vṛthā'bhavadarthī
hī vidhitsurasi vatsa ! kimetat ||125||
yācitaścirayati kva nu dhīraḥ
prāṇane kṣaṇamapi pratibhūḥ kaḥ |
śaṃsati dvinayanī dṛḍhanidrāṃ
drāṅgimeṣamiṣaghūrṇanapūrṇā ||126||
abhrapuṣpamapi ditsati śītaṃ
sārthinā vimukhatā yadabhāji |
stokakasya khalu cañcupuṭena
mlānirullasati taddhanasaṅghe ||127||
ūcivānucitamakṣaramenaṃ
pāśapāṇirapi pāṇimudasya |
kīrtireva bhavatāṃ priyadārā
dānanīrajharamauktikahārā ||128||
carma varma kila yasya nabhedyaṃ
yasya vajramayamasthi ca tau cet |
sthāyināviha na karṇadadhīcī
tanna dharmamavadhīraya dhīra ! ||129||
adya yāvadapi yena nibaddhau
na prabhū vicalituṃ balivindhyau |
āsthitāvitathatāguṇapāśaḥ
tvādṛśā sa viduṣā durapāsaḥ ||130||
preyasī jitasudhāṃśumukhaśrīḥ
yā na muñcati digantagatāpi |
bhaṅgisaṅgamakuraṅgadṛgarthe
kaḥ kadarthayati tāmapi kīrtim ||131||
yānvaraṃ prati pare'rthayitāra
ste'pi yaṃ vayamaho sa punastvam |
naiva naḥ khalu manorathamātraṃ
śūra ! pūraya diśo'pi yaśobhiḥ ||132||
arthitā tvayi gateṣu sureṣu
mlānadānajanijoruyaśaḥ śrīḥ |
adya pāṇḍu gaganaṃ suraśākhī
kevalena kusumena vidhattam ||133||
pravasate bharatārjunavainyaṃvat
smṛtidhṛto'pi nala ! tvamabhīṣṭadaḥ |
svagamanāphalatāṃ yadi śaṅkase
tadaphalaṃ nikhilaṃ khalu maṅgalam ||134||
iṣṭaṃ na prati te pratiśrutirabhūdyādya svarāhlādinī
dharmārthā sṛja tāṃ śrutipratibhaṭīkṛtyānvitākhyāpadām |
tvatkīrtiḥ punatī punastribhuvanaṃ śubhrādvayādeśanāddravyāṇāṃ śitipītalohitaharinnāmānvayaṃ lumpatu ||135||
yaṃ prāsūta sahasrapādudabhavatpādena khañjaḥ kathaṃ
sa cchāyātanayaḥ sutaḥ kila pituḥ sādṛśyamanvicchati |
etasyottaramadya naḥ samajani tvattejasāṃ laṅghane
sāhasrairapi paṅguraṅghribhirabhivyaktībhavanbhānumān ||136||
ityākarṇya kṣitīśastridaśapariṣadastā giraścāṭugarbhā
vaidarbhīkāmuko'pi prasabhavinihitaṃ dūtyabhāraṃ babhāra |
aṅgīkāraṃ gate'sminnamaraparivṛḍhaḥ saṃbhṛtānandamūce
bhūyādantardhisiddheranuvihitabhavaccittatā yatra tatra ||137||
śrīharṣaṃ kavirājarājimukuṭālaṃkārahīraḥ sutaṃ
śrīhīraḥ suṣuve jitendriyacayaṃ māmalladevī ca yam |
tasya śrīvijayapraśastiracanātātasya navye mahākāvye cāruṇi naiṣadhīyacarite sargo'gamatpañcamaḥ ||138||
ṣaṣṭha sargaḥ
dūtyāya daityāripateḥ pravṛtto
dviṣāṃ niṣeddhā niṣadhapradhānam |
sa bhīmabhūmīpatirājadhānīṃ
lakṣīcakārātha rathasyadasya ||1||
bhaimyā samaṃ nājagaṇadviyogaṃ
sa dūtadharme sthiradhīradhīrāḥ |
payodhipāne munirantarāyaṃ
durvāramapyaurvasivaurvaśeyaḥ ||2||
nalapraṇālīmiladambujākṣīsaṃvādapīyūṣapipāsavaste |
tadadhvavīkṣārthamivānimeṣā
deśasya tasyābharaṇībabhūvuḥ ||3||
tāṃ kuṇḍinākhyāpadamātraguptām
indrasya bhūmeramarāvatīṃ saḥ |
manorathaḥ siddhimiva kṣaṇena
rathastadīyaḥ puramāsasāda ||4||
bhaimīpadasparśakṛtārtharathyā
seyaṃ purītyutkalikākulastām |
nṛpau nipīya kṣaṇamīkṣaṇābhyāṃ
bhṛśaṃ nirāśvāsa suraiḥ kṣatāśaḥ ||5||
svidyatpramodāśrulavena vāmaṃ
romāñcabhṛtpakṣmabhirasya cakṣuḥ |
anyatpunaḥ kampramapi sphurattvāt
tasyāḥ puraḥ prāpa navopabhogam ||6||
rathādasau sārathinā sanāthā
drājāvatīryātha puraṃ viveśa |
nirgatya bimbādiva bhānavīyāt
saudhākaraṃ maṇḍalamaṃśusaṅghaḥ ||7||
citraṃ tadā kuṇḍinaveśinaḥ sā
nalasya mūrtirvavṛte na dṛśyā |
babhūva taccitrataraṃ tathāpi
viśvaikadṛśyaiva yadasya mūrtiḥ ||8||
janairvidagdhairbhavanaiśca mugdhaiḥ
pade pade vismayakalpavallīm |
tāṃ gāhamānāsya ciraṃ nalasya
dṛṣṭiryayau rājakulātithitvam ||9||
helāṃ dadhau rakṣijane'strasajje
līnaścarāmīti hṛdā lalajje |
drakṣyāmi bhaimīmiti saṃtutoṣa
dūtaṃ vicintya svamasau śuśoca ||10||
athopakāryāmamarendrakāryāt
kakṣāsu rakṣādhikṛtairadṛṣṭaḥ |
bhaimīṃ didṛkṣurbahu dikṣu trakṣuḥ
diśannasau tāmaviśadviśaṅkaḥ ||11||
ayaṃ ka ityanyanivārakāṇāṃ
girā vibhūrdvāri vibhujya kaṇṭham |
dṛśaṃ dadau vismayanistaraṅgāṃ
sa laṅghitāyāmapi rājasiṃhaḥ ||12||
antaḥ purāntaḥ sa vilokya bālāṃ
kāṃcitsamālabdhumasaṃvṛtorum |
nimīlitākṣaḥ parayā bhramantyā
saṃghaṭṭamāsādya camaccakāra ||13||
anādisargasraji vānubhūtā
citreṣu vā bhīmasutā nalena |
jāteva yadvā jitaśambarasya
sā śāmbarīśilpamalakṣi dikṣu ||14||
alīkabhaimīsahadarśanānna
tasyānyakanyāpsaraso rasāya |
bhaimībhramasyaiva tataḥ prasādād
bhaimībhramastena na tāsvalambhi ||15||
bhaimīnirāśe hṛdi manmathena
dattasvahastādvirahādvihastaḥ |
sa tāmalīkāmavalokya tatra
kṣaṇādapaśyanvyaṣadadvibuddhaḥ ||16||
priyāṃ vikalpopahṛtāṃ sa yāvad
digīśasaṃdeśamajalpadalpam |
adṛśyavāgbhīṣitabhūribhīrubhavo ravastāvadacetayattam ||17||
paśyansa tasminmarutāpi tanvyāḥ
stanau parispraṣṭumivāstavastrau |
akṣāntapakṣāntamṛgāṅkamāsyaṃ
dadhāra tiryagvalitaṃ vilakṣaḥ ||18||
antaḥpure vistṛtavāguro'pi
bālāvalīnāṃ valitairguṇaughaiḥ |
na kālasāraṃ hariṇaṃ tadakṣit
vayaṃ prabhurbaddhumabhūnmanobhūḥ ||19||
dormūlamālokya kacaṃ rurutsoḥ
tataḥ kucau tāvanulepayantyāḥ |
nābhīmathaiṣa ślathavāsaso'nu
mimīla dikṣu kramakṛṣṭacakṣuḥ ||20||
mīlanna śeke'bhimukhāgatābhyāṃ
dhartuṃ nipīḍya stanasāntarābhyām |
svāṅgānyapeto vijagau sa paścāt
pumaṅgasaṅgotpulake punaste ||21||
nimīlanaspaṣṭavilokanābhyāṃ
kadarthitastāḥ kalayankaṭākṣaiḥ |
sa rāgadarśīva bhṛśaṃ lalajje
svataḥ satāṃ hrīḥ parato'tigurvī ||22||
romāñcitāṅgīmanu tatkaṭākṣaiḥ
bhrāntena kāntena raternidiṣṭaḥ |
moghaḥ śaraughaḥ kusumāni nābhūt
taddhairyapūjāṃ prati paryavasyan ||23||
hitvaiva vartmaikamiha bhramantyāḥ
sparśaḥ striyāḥ sutyaja ityavetya |
catuṣpathasyābharaṇaṃ babhūva
lokāvalokāya satāṃ sa dīpaḥ ||24||
udvartayantyā hṛdaye nipatya
nṛpasya dṛṣṭirnyavṛtaddrutaiva |
viyogivairātkucayornakhāṅkaiḥ
ardhendulīlairgalahasteteva ||25||
tanvīmukhaṃ drāgadhigatya candraṃ
viyoginastasya mimīlitābhyām |
dvayaṃ draḍhīyaḥ kṛtamīkṣaṇābhyāṃ
tadindutā ca svasarojatā ca ||26||
catuṣpathe taṃ vinimīlitākṣaṃ
caturdigetāḥ sukhamagrahīṣyan |
saṃghaṭṭaya tasminbhṛśabhīnivṛtāt
tastā eva tadvartma na cedadāsyan ||27||
saṃghaṭṭayantyāstarasātmabhūṣāhīrāṅkuraprotadukūlahārī |
diśā nitambaṃ paridhāpya tanvyāḥ
tatpāpasaṃtāpamavāpa bhūpaḥ ||28||
hataḥ kayācitpathi kandukena
saṃghaṭṭya bhinnaḥ karajaiḥ kayāpi |
kayācanāktaḥ kucakuṅkumena
saṃbhuktakalpaḥ sa babhūva tābhiḥ ||29||
chāyāmayaḥ praikṣi kayāpi hāre
nije sa gacchannatha nekṣyamāṇaḥ |
taccintayāntarniracāyi cāru
svasyaiva tanvyā hṛdayaṃ praviṣṭaḥ ||30||
tacchāyasaundaryanipītadhairyāḥ
pratyekamāliṅgadamū ratīśaḥ |
ratipratidvandvitamāsu nūnaṃ
nāmūṣu nirṇītaratiḥ kathaṃcit ||31||
tasmādadṛśyādapi nātibibhyuḥ
tacchāyarūpāhitamohalolāḥ |
manyanta evādṛtamanmathājñāḥ
prāṇānapi svānsudṛśastṛṇāni ||32||
jāgarti tacchāyadṛśāṃ purā yaḥ
spṛṣṭe ca tasminvisasarpa kampaḥ |
drute drutaṃ tatpadaśabdabhītyā
svahastitaścārudṛśāṃ paraṃ saḥ ||33||
ullāsyatāṃ spṛṣṭanalāṅgamaṅgaṃ
tāsāṃ nalacchāyapibā'pi dṛṣṭiḥ |
aśmaiva ratyāstadanarti patyā
chede'pyabodhaṃ yadaharṣi loma ||34||
yasminnalaspṛṣṭakametya hṛṣṭā
bhūyo'pi taṃ deśamagānmṛgākṣī |
nipatya tatrāsya dharārajaḥsthe
pāde prasīdeti śanairavādīt ||35||
bhramannamuṣyāmupakārikāyām
āyasya bhaimīvirahātkraśīyān |
asau muhuḥ saudhaparamparāṇāṃ
vyadhatta viśrāntimadhityakāsu ||36||
ullikhya haṃsena dale nalinyāḥ
tasmai yathādarśi tathaiva bhaimī |
tenābhilikhyopahṛtasvahārā
kasyā na dṛṣṭājani vismayāya ||37||
kaumāragandhīni nivārayantī
vṛttāni romāvalivetracihnā |
sālikhya tenaikṣyata yauvanīyad
vaḥsthāmavasthāṃ paricetukāmā ||38||
paśyāḥ puraṃghrīḥ prāta sāndracandrarajaḥkṛtakrīḍakumāracakre |
citrāṇi cakre'dhvani cakravarticihnaṃ tadaṅghripratimāsu cakram ||39||
tāruṇyapuṇyāmavalokayantyoḥ
anyonyameṇekṣaṇayorabhikhyām |
madhye muhūrtaṃ sa babhūva gacchan
nākasmikācchādanavismayāya ||40||
puraḥsthitasya kvacidasya bhūṣāratneṣu nāryaḥ pratibimbitāni |
vyomanyadṛśyeṣu nijānyapaśyan
vismitya vismitya sahasrakṛtvaḥ ||41||
tasminviṣajyārdhapathāttapātaṃ
tadaṅgarāgacchuritaṃ nirīkṣya |
vismeratāmāpuravismarantyaḥ
kṣiptaṃ mithaḥ kandukamindumukhyaḥ ||42||
puṃsi svabhartṛvyatiriktabhūte
bhūtvāpyanīkṣāniyamavratinyaḥ |
chāyāsu rūpaṃ bhuvi vīkṣya tasya
phalaṃ dṛśorānaśire mahiṣyaḥ ||43||
vilokya tacchāyamatarki tābhiḥ
patiṃ prati svaṃ vasudhāpi dhatte |
yathā vayaṃ kiṃ madanaṃ tathainaṃ
trinetranetrānalakīlanīlam ||44||
rūpaṃ praticchāyikayopanītam
āloki tābhiryadi nāma kāmam |
tathāpi nāloki tadasya rūpaṃ
hāridrabhaṅgāya vitīrṇabhaṅgam ||45||
bhavannadṛśyaḥ pratibimbadehavyūhaṃ vitanvanmaṇikuṭṭimeṣu |
puraṃ parasya praviśanviyogī
yogīva citraṃ sa rarāja rājā ||46||
pumānivāsparśi mayā vrajantyā
chāyā mayā puṃsa iva vyaloki |
bruvannivātarki mayāpi kaścit
iti sma sa straiṇagiraḥ śṛṇoti ||47||
ambāṃ praṇatyopanatā natāṅgī
nalena bhaimī pathi yogamāpa |
sa bhrāntibhaimīṣu na tāṃ vyavikta
sā taṃ ca nādṛśyatayā dadarśa ||48||
prasūprasādādhigatā prasūnamālā nalasyodbhramavīkṣitasya |
kṣiptāpi kaṇṭhāya tayopakaṇṭhe
sthitaṃ tamālambata satyameva ||49||
sragvāsanādṛṣṭajanaprasādaḥ
satyeyamityadbhutamāpa bhūpaḥ |
kṣiptāmadṛśyatvamitāṃ ca mālāmālokya tāṃ vismiyate sma bālā ||50||
anyonyamanyatravadīkṣamāṇau
paraspareṇādhyuṣite'pi deśe |
āliṅgitālīkaparasparāntaḥ
tathyaṃ mithastau pariṣasvajāte ||51||
sparśe tamasyādhigatāpi bhaimī
mene punarbhrāntimadarśanena |
nṛpaḥ sa paśyannapi tāmudītaḥ
tambho na dhartuṃ sahasā śaśāka ||52||
sparśātiharṣādṛtasatyamatyā
pravṛtya mithyāpratilabdhabādhau |
punarmithastathyamapi spṛśantau
na śraddadhāte pathi tau vimugdhau ||53||
sarvatra saṃvādyamabādhamānau
rūpaśriyātithyakaraṃ paraṃ tau |
na śekatuḥ kelirasādvirantu
malīkamālokya parasparaṃ tu ||54||
parasparasparśarasormisekāt
tayoḥ kṣaṇaṃ cetasi vipralambhaḥ |
snehātidānādiva dīpikārciḥ
nimiṣya kiṃciddvaguṇaṃ didīpe ||55||
veśmāpa sā dhairyaviyogayogād
bodhaṃ ca mohaṃ ca muhurdadhānā |
punaḥ punastatra puraḥ sa paśyan
babhrāmatāṃ subhruvamudbhrameṇa ||56||
padbhyāṃ nṛpaḥ saṃcaramāṇa eṣa
ciraṃ paribhramya kathaṃkathaṃcit |
vidarbharājaprabhavābhirāmaṃ
prāsādamabhraṃkaṣamāsasāda ||57||
sakhīśatānāṃ sarasairvilāsaiḥ
smarāvarodhabhramamāvahantīm |
vilokayāmāsa sabhāṃ sa bhaimyāḥ
tasya pratolīmaṇivedikāyām ||58||
kaṇṭhaḥ kimasyāḥ pikaveṇuvīṇāḥ
tisro jitāḥ sūcayati trirekhaḥ |
ityantarastūyata yatra kāpi
nalena bālā kalamālapantī ||59||
etaṃ nalaṃ taṃ damayanti ! paśya
tyajārtimityālikulaprabodhān |
śrutvā sa nārīkaravartisārīmukhātsvamāśaṅkata yatra dṛṣṭam ||60||
yatraikayālīkanalīkṛtālī
kaṇṭhe mṛṣābhīmabhavībhavantyā |
tadṛkpathe dauhadikopanītā
śālīnamādhāyi madhūkamālā ||61||
candrābhamābhraṃ tilakaṃ dadhānā
tadvannijāsyendukṛtānubimbam |
sakhīmukhe candrasame sasarja
candrānavasthāmiva kāpi yatra ||62||
dalodare kāñcanaketakasya
kṣaṇānmasībhāvukavarṇalekham |
tasyaiva yatra svamanaṅgalekhaṃ
lilekha bhaimī nakhalekhinībhiḥ ||63||
vilekhituṃ bhīmabhuvo lipīṣu
sakhyā'tivikhyātibhṛtāpi yatra |
aśāki līlākamalaṃ na pāṇiḥ
apāri karṇotpalamakṣi naiva ||64||
bhaimīmupāvīṇayadetya yatra
kalipriyasya priyaśiṣyavargaḥ |
gandharvavadhvaḥ svaramadhvarīṇatatkaṇṭhanālaikadhurīṇavīṇaḥ ||65||
nāvā smaraḥ kiṃ harabhītirgupte
payodhare khelati kumbha eva |
ityardhacandrābhanakhāṅkacumbikucā sakhī yatra sakhībhirūci ||66||
smarāśugībhūya vidarbhasubhruvakṣo yadakṣabhi khalu prasūnaiḥ |
srajaṃ sṛjantyā tadaśodhi teṣu
yatraikayā sūciśikhāṃ nikhāya ||67||
smarāśugībhūya vidarbhasubhrū
vakṣo yadakṣobhi khalu prasūnaiḥ |
srajaṃ sṛjantyā tadaśodhi tiṣu
yatraikayā suciśikhāṃ nikhāya ||67||
yatrāvadattāmatibhīya bhaimī
tyaja tyajedaṃ sakhi ! sāhasikyam |
tvameva kṛtvā madanāya datse
bāṇānprasūnāni guṇena sajjām ||68||
ālikhya sakhyāḥ kucapatrabhaṅgī
madhye sumadhyā makarīṃ kareṇa |
yatrālapattāmidamāli ! yānaṃ
manye tvadekāvalinākanadyāḥ ||69||
tāmeva sā yatra jagāda bhūyaḥ
payodhiyādaḥ kucakumbhayoste |
seyaṃ sthitā tāvakahṛcchayāṅkapriyāstu vistārayaśaḥ praśastiḥ ||70||
śārīṃ carantīṃ sakhi ! mārayaitām
ityakṣadāye kathite kayāpi |
yatra svaghātabhramabhīruśārīkākūtthasākūtahasaḥ sa jajñe ||71||
bhaimīsamīpe sa nirīkṣya yatra
tāmbūlajāmbūnadahaṃsalakṣmīm |
kṛtapriyādūtyamahopakāram
arālamohadraḍhimānamūhe ||72||
tasminniyaṃ seti sakhīsamāje
nalasya saṃdehamatha vyudasyan |
apṛṣṭa eva sphuṭamāvacakṣe
sa ko'pi rūpātiśayaḥ svayaṃ tām ||73||
bhaimīvinodāya mudā sakhībhiḥ
tadākṛtīnāṃ bhuvi kalpitānām |
nātarki madhye sphuṭamapyudītaṃ
tasyānubimbaṃ maṇivedikāyām ||74||
hutāśakīnāśajaleśadūtīḥ
nirākariṣyoḥ kṛtakākuyācñāḥ |
bhaimyā vacobhiḥ sa nijāṃ tadāśāṃ
nyavartayaddūramapi prayātām ||75||
vijñaptimantaḥ sabhayaḥ sa bhaimyā
madhyesabhaṃ vāsavasambhalīyām |
saṃbhālayāmāsa bhṛśaṃ kṛśāśaḥ
tadālivṛndairabhinandyamānām ||76||
lipirna daivī supaṭhā bhuvīti
tubhyaṃ mayi preṣitavācikasya |
indrasya dūtyāṃ racaya prasādaṃ
vijñāpayantyāmavadhānadānaiḥ ||77||
salīlamāliṅganayopapīḍam
anāmayaṃ pṛcchati vāsavastvām |
śeṣastvadāśleṣakathāpanidraiḥ
tadromabhiḥ saṃdidiśe bhavatyai ||78||
yaḥ preryamāṇo'pi hṛdā maghonaḥ
tvadarthanāyāṃ hriyamāpadāgaḥ |
svayaṃvarasthānajuṣastamasya
badhāna kaṇṭhaṃ varaṇasrajāśu ||79||
nainaṃ tyaja kṣīradhimanthanādyaiḥ
asyānujāyodgamitāmaraiḥ śraḥ |
asmai vimathyekṣurasodamanyāṃ
śrāmyantu notthāpayituṃ śriyaṃ te ||80||
lokasraji dyaurdivi cāditeyā
apyāditeyeṣu mahānmahendraḥ |
kiṃkartumarthī yadi so'pi rāgāj
jāgarti kakṣā kimataḥ parāpi ||81||
padaṃ śatenāpa makhairyadindraḥ
tasmai sa te yācanacāṭukāraḥ |
kuru prasādaṃ tadalaṃkuruṣva
svīkārakṛdbhrūnaṭanaśrameṇa ||82||
mandākinīnandanayorvihāre
deve dhave devari mādhave ca |
śreyaḥ śriyā yātari yacca sakhyāṃ
taccetasā bhavini ! bhāvayasva ||83||
rajyasva rājye jagatāmitīndrāt
yācñāpratiṣṭhāṃ labhase tvameva |
laghūkṛtasvaṃ baliyācanena
tatprāptaye vāmanamāmananti ||84||
yāneva devānnamasi trikālaṃ
na tatkṛtaghnīkṛtiraucitī te |
prasīda tānapyanṛṇānvidhātuṃ
patiṣyatastvatpadayostrisaṃdhyam ||85||
ityuktavatyā nihitādareṇa
bhaimyā gṛhītā maghavatprasādaḥ |
srakpārijātasya ṛte nalāśāṃ
vāsairaśeṣāmapurūpadāśām ||86||
ārye ! vicāryālamiheti kāpi
yogyaṃ sakhi ! syāditi kācanāpi |
oṃkāra evottaramastu vastu
maṅgalyamatreti ca kāpyavocāt ||87||
anāśravā vaḥ kimahaṃ kadāpi
vaktuṃ viśeṣaḥ paramasti śeṣaḥ |
itīrite bhīmajayā na dūtīm
āliṅgadālīśca mudāmiyattā ||88||
bhaimīṃ ca dūtyaṃ ca na kiṃcidāpam
iti svayaṃ bhāvayato nalasya |
ālokamātrādyadi tanmukhendoḥ
abhūnna bhinnaṃ hṛdayāravindam ||89||
īṣatsmitakṣālitasṛkvibhāgā
dṛksaṃjñayā vāritatattadāliḥ |
srajā namaskṛtya tayaiva śakraṃ
tāṃ bhīmabhūruttarayāṃcakāra ||90||
stutau maghonastyaja sāhasikyaṃ
vaktuṃ kiyattaṃ yadi veda vedaḥ |
mṛṣottaraṃ sākṣiṇi hṛtsu nṝṇām
ajñātṛvijñāpi mamāpi tasmin ||91||
ājñāṃ tadīyāmanu kasya nāma
nakārapāruṣyamupaitu jihvā |
prahvā tu tāṃ mūrdhni vidhāya mālāṃ
bālāparādhyāmi viśeṣavāgbhiḥ ||92||
tapaḥ phalatvena hareḥ kṛpeyamimaṃ tapasyeva janaṃ niyuṅke |
bhavatyupāyaṃ prati hi pravṛttāvupeyamādhuryamadhairyasarji ||93||
śuśrūṣitāhe tadahaṃ tameva
patiṃ mude'pi vratasaṃpade'pi |
viśeṣaleśo'yamadevadehamaṃśāgataṃ tu kṣitibhṛttayeha ||94||
aśrauṣamindrādariṇīrgiraste
satīvratātipratilomatīvrāḥ |
svaṃ prāgahaṃ prādiṣi nāmarāya
kiṃ nāma tasmai manasā narāya ||95||
tasminvimṛśyaiva vṛte hṛdaiṣā
maindrī dayā māmanutāpikābhūt |
nirvātukāmaṃ bhavasaṃbhavānāṃ
dhīraṃ sukhānāmavadhīraṇeva ||96||
varṣeṣu yadbhāratamāryadhuryāḥ
stuvanti gārhasthyamivāśrameṣu |
tatrāsmi patyurvarivasyayeha
śarmormikirmīritadharmalipsuḥ ||97||
svarge satāṃ śarma paraṃ na dharmā
bhravantiṃ bhūmāviha tacca te ca |
śakyā makhenāpi mudo'marāṇāṃ
kathaṃ vihāya trayamekamīhe ||98||
sādhorapi svaḥ khalu gāmitādho
gamī sa tu svargamitaḥ prayāṇe |
ityāyatī cintayato hṛdi dve
dvayorudarkaḥ kimu śarkare na ||99||
prakṣīṇa evāyuṣi karmakṛṣṭe
narānna tiṣṭhatyupatiṣṭhate yaḥ |
bubhukṣate nākamapathyakalpaṃ
dhīrastamāpātasukhonmukhaṃ kaḥ ||100||
itīndradūtyāḥ prativācamardhe
pratyuhya saiṣābhidadhe vayasyāḥ |
kiṃcidvivakṣollasadoṣṭhalakṣmījitāpanidraddalapaṅkajāsyāḥ ||101||
anādidhāvisvaparamparāyā
hetusrajaḥ srotasi veśvare vā |
āyattadhīreṣa janastadāryāḥ !
kimīdṛśaḥ paryanuyogayogyaḥ ||102||
nityaṃ niyatyā paravatyaśeṣe
kaḥ saṃvidāno'pyanuyogayogyaḥ |
acetanā sā ca na vācamarhed
vaktā tu vaktraśramakarma bhuṅkte ||103||
kramelakaṃ nindati komalecchuḥ
kramelakaḥ kaṇṭakalampaṭastam |
prītau tayoriṣṭabhujoḥ samāyāṃ
madhyasthatā naikataropahāsaḥ ||104||
guṇā haranto'pi harernaraṃ me
na rocamānaṃ parihāpayanti |
na lokamālokayathāpavargā
trivargamarvāñcamamuñcamānam ||105||
ākīṭamākaiṭabhavairi tulyaḥ
svābhīṣṭalābhātkṛtakṛtyabhāvaḥ |
bhinnaspṛhāṇāṃ prati cārthamarthaṃ
dviṣṭatvamiṣṭatvamapavyavastham ||106||
adhvāgrajāgrannibhṛtāpadandhuḥ
bandhuryadi syātpratibandhumarhaḥ |
joṣaṃ janaḥ kāryavidastu vastu
pracchayā nijecchā padavīṃ mudastu ||107||
itthaṃ pratīpoktimatiṃ sakhīnāṃ
vilupya pāṇḍityabalena bālā |
api śrutasvarpatimantrisūktiṃ
dūtīṃ babhāṣe'dbhutalolamaulim ||108||
paretabharturmanaseva dūtīṃ
nabhasvatevānilasakhyabhājaḥ |
trisrotasevāmbupatestadāśu
sthirāsthamāyātavatīṃ nirāstham ||109||
bhūyo'rthamenaṃ yadi māṃ tvamāttha
tadā padāvālabhase maghonaḥ |
satīvrataistīvramimaṃ tu mantum
antarvaraṃ vajriṇi mārjitāsmi ||110||
itthaṃ punarvāgavakāśanāśān
mahendradūtyāmapayātavatyām |
viveśa lolaṃ hṛdayaṃ nalasya
jīvaḥ punaḥ kṣībamiva prabodhaḥ ||111||
śravaṇapuṭayugena svena sādhūpanītaṃ
digadhipakṛpayāptādīdṛśaḥ saṃvidhānāt |
alabhata madhu bālārāgavāgutthamitthaṃ
naṣadhajanapadendraḥ pātumānandasāndram ||112||
śrīharṣaṃ kavirājarājimukuṭālaṃkārahīraḥ sutaṃ
śrīhīraḥ suṣuve jitendriyacayaṃ mamalladevī ca yam |
ṣaṣṭhaḥ khaṇḍanakhaṇḍato'pi sahajātkṣodakṣame tanmahākāvye'yaṃ vyagalannalasya carite sargo nisargojjvalaḥ ||113||

saptamaḥ sargaḥ
atha priyāsādanaśīlanādau
manorathaḥ pallavitaściraṃ yaḥ |
vilokanenaiva sa rājaputryāḥ
patyā bhuvaḥ pūrṇavadabhyamāni ||1||
pratipratīkaṃ prathamaṃ priyāyām
athāntarānandasudhāsamudre |
tataḥ pramodāśruparamparāṇāṃ
mamajjatustasya dṛśau nṛpasya ||2||
brahmādvayasyānvabhavatpramodaṃ
romāgra evāgranirīkṣite'syāḥ |
yathaucitītthaṃ tadaśeṣadṛṣṭāvatha smarādvaitamudaṃ tathāsau ||3||
velāmatikramya pṛthuṃ mukhendoḥ
ālokapīyūṣarasena tasyāḥ |
nalasya rāgāmbunidhau vivṛddhe
tuṅgau kucāvāśrayataḥ sma dṛṣṭi ||4||
magnā sudhāyāṃ kimu tanmukhendoḥ
lagnā sthitā tatkucayāḥ kimantaḥ |
cireṇa tanmadhyamamuñcatāsya
dṛṣṭiḥ kraśīyaḥ skhalanādbhiyā nu ||5||
priyāṅgapānthā kucayornivṛtya
nivṛtya lolā naladṛgbhramantī |
babhautamāṃ tanmṛganābhilepatamaḥ samāsāditadigbhrameva ||6||
vibhramya taccārunitambacakre
dūtasya dṛktasya khalu skhalantī |
sthirā cirādāsya tadūrurambhāḥ
tambhāvupāśliṣya kareṇa gāḍham ||7||
vāsaḥ paraṃ netramahaṃ na netraṃ
kimu tvamāliṅgya tanmayāpi |
uronitambopu kuru prasādam
itīva sā tatpadayoḥ papāta ||8||
dṛśoryathākāmamathopahṛtsa
sa preyasīmālikulaṃ ca tasyāḥ |
idaṃ pramodādbhutasaṃbhṛtena
mahīmahendro manasā jagāda ||9||
pade vidhāturyadi manmatho vā
mamābhiṣicyeta manoratho vā |
tadā ghaṭetāpi na vā tadetat
pratipratīkādbhutarūpaśilpam ||10||
taraṅgiṇī bhūmibhṛtaḥ prabhūtā
jānāmi śrṛṅgārarasasya seyam |
lāvaṇyapūro'jani yauvanena
tasyāṃ tathoccaistanatāghanena ||11||
asyāṃ vapurvyūhavidhānavidyāṃ
kiṃ dyotayāmāsa navāṃ sa kāmaḥ |
pratyaṅgasaṅgasphuṭalabdhabhūmā
lāvaṇyasīmā yadimāmupāste ||12||
jambālajālātkimakarṣi jambūnadyā na hāridranibhaprabheyam |
apyaṅgayugmasya na saṅgacihnam
unnīyate danturatā yadatra ||13||
satyeva sāmye sadṛśādaśeṣād
guṇāntareṇoccakṛṣe yadaṅgaiḥ |
asyāstataḥ syāttulanāpi nāma
vastu tvamīṣāmupamāpamānaḥ ||14||
purākṛtistraiṇamimāṃ vidhātum
abhūdvidhātuḥ khalu hastalekhaḥ |
yeyaṃ bhavadbhāvipuraṃghnisṛṣṭiḥ
sāsyai yaśastajjayajaṃ pradātum ||15||
bhavyāni hānīraguretadaṅgād
yathā yathānarti tathā tathā taiḥ |
asyādhikasyopamayopamātā
dātā pratiṣṭhāṃ khalu tebhya eva ||16||
nāsparśi dṛṣṭāpi vimohikeyaṃ
doṣairaśeṣaiḥ svabhiyeti manye |
anyeṣu tairākulitastadasyāṃ
vasatyasāpatnyasukhī guṇaughaḥ ||17||
aujjhi priyāṅgairghṛṇayaiva rūkṣā
na vāridurgāttu varāṭakasya |
na kaṇṭakairāvaraṇācca kāntiḥ
dhūlībhṛtā kāñcanaketakasya ||18||
pratyaṅgamasyāmabhikena rakṣāṃ
kartuṃ maghoneva nijāstramasti |
vajraṃ ca bhūṣāmaṇimūrtidhāri
niyojitaṃ taddayutikārmukaṃ ca ||19||
asyāḥ sapakṣaikavidhoḥ kacaughaḥ
sthāne mukhasyopari vāsamāpa |
pakṣasthatāvadbahucandrako'pi
kalāpināṃ yena jitaḥ kalāpaḥ ||20||
asyā yadāsyena purastiraśca
tiraskṛtaṃ śītarucāndhakāram |
sphuṭasphuṭadbhaṅgikacacchalena
tadeva paścādidamasti baddham ||21||
asyāḥ kacānāṃ śikhinaśca kiṃnu
vidhiṃ kalāpau vimateragātām |
tenāyamebhiḥ kimapūji puṣpaiḥ
bhartsi dattvā sa kimardhacandram ||22||
keśāndhakārādatha dṛśyabhālasthalalārdhacandrā sphuṭamaṣṭamīyam |
enāṃ yadāsādya jagajjayāya
manobhuvā siddhirasādhi sādhu ||23||
pauṣpaṃ dhanuḥ kiṃ madanasya dāhe
śyāmībhavatkesaraśeṣamāsīt |
vyadhāddvidheśastadapi krudhā kiṃ
bhaimībhruvau yena vidhiryadhatta ||24||
bhrūmyāṃ priyāyā bhavatā manobhūcāpena cāpe ghanasārabhāvaḥ |
nijāṃ yadaploṣadaśāmapekṣya
saṃpratyanenādhikavīryatārji ||25||
smāraṃ dhanuryadvidhunojjhitāsyā
yāsyena bhūtena ca lakṣmalekhā |
etadbhruvau janma tadāpa yugmaṃ
līlācalatvocitabālabhāvam ||26||
iṣutrayeṇaiva jagattrayasya
vinirjayātpuṣpamayāśugena |
śeṣā dvibāṇī saphalīkṛteyaṃ
priyādṛgambhojapade'bhiṣicya ||27||
seyaṃ mṛduḥ kausumacāpayaṣṭiḥ
smarasya muṣṭigrahaṇārhamadhyā |
tanoti naḥ śrīmadapāṅgamuktāṃ
mohāya yā dṛṣṭiśaraughavṛṣṭim ||28||
āghūrṇitaṃ pakṣmalamakṣipadmaṃ
prāntadyutiśvaityajitāmṛtāṃśu |
asyā ivāsyāścaladindranīlagolāmalaśyāmalatāratāram ||29||
karṇotpalenāpi mukhaṃ sanāthaṃ
labheta netradyutinirjitena |
yadyetadīyena tataḥ kṛtārthā
svacakṣuṣī kiṃ kurute kuraṅgī ||30||
tvacaḥ samuttārya dalāni rītyā
mocātvacaḥ pañcaṣapāṭanāyām |
sāraurgṛhītairvidhirutpalaughāt
asyāmabhūdīkṣaṇarūpaśilpī ||31||
cakoranetraiṇadṛgutpalānāṃ
nimeṣayantreṇa kimeṣa kṛṣṭaḥ |
sāraḥ sudhodgāramayaḥ prayatnaiḥ
vidhātumetannayane vidhātuḥ ||32||
ṛṇīkṛtā kiṃ hariṇībhirāsīt
asyāḥ sakāśānnayanadvayaśrīḥ |
bhūyoguṇeyaṃ sakalā balādyat
tābhyo'nayā'labhyata bibhyatībhyaḥ ||33||
dṛśau kimasyāścapalasvabhāve
na dūramākramya mitho miletām |
na cetkṛtaḥ syādanayoḥ prayāṇe
vighnaḥ śravaḥ kūpanipātabhītyā ||34||
kedārabhājā śiśirapraveśāt
puṇyāya manye mṛtamutpalinyā |
jātā yatastatkusumekṣaṇeyaṃ
yātaśca tatkorakadṛkcakoraḥ ||35||
nāsādasīyā tilapuṣpatūṇaṃ
jagattrayavyastaśaratrayasya |
śvāsānilāmodabharānumeyāṃ
dadhaddvibāṇīṃ kusumāyudhasya ||36||
bandhūkabandhūbhavadetadasyā
mukhendunānena sahojjihānā |
rāgaśriyā śaiśavayauvanīyāṃ
svamāha saṃdhyāmadharoṣṭhalekhā ||37||
asyā mukhendāvadharaḥ sudhābhūbimbasya yuktaḥ pratibimba eṣaḥ |
tasyātha vā śrīrdrumabhāji deśe
saṃbhāvyamānāsya tu vidrume sā ||38||
jāne'tirāgādidameva bimbaṃ
bimbasya ca vyaktamito'dharatvam |
dvayorviśeṣāvamākṣamāṇāṃ
nāmni bhramo'bhūdanayorjanānām ||39||
madhyopakaṇṭhāvadharoṣṭhabhāgau
bhātaḥ kimapyucchṛsitau yadasyāḥ |
tatsvapnasaṃbhogavitīrṇadantadaṃśena kiṃ vā na mayāparāddham ||40||
vidyā vidarbhendrasutādharoṣṭhe
nṛtyanti katyantarabhedabhājaḥ |
itīva rekhābhirapaśramastāḥ
saṃkhyātavān kautukavānvidhātā ||41||
saṃbhujyamānādya mayā niśānte
svapne'nubhūtā madhurādhareyam |
asīmalāvaṇyaradacchadetthaṃ
kathaṃ mayaiva pratipadyate vā ||42||
yadi prasādīkurute sudhāṃśoḥ
eṣā sahasrāṃśamapi smitasya |
tatkaumudīnāṃ kurute tameva
nimicchaya devaḥ saphalaṃ sa janma ||43||
candrādhikaitanmukhacandrikāṇāṃ
darāyataṃ tatkiraṇāddhanānām |
puraḥ sarasrastapṛṣaddvitīyaṃ
radāvalidvandvati binduvṛndam ||44||
seyaṃ mamaitadvirahārtimūrcchā
tamīvibhātasya vibhāti saṃdhyā |
mahendrakāṣṭhāgatarāgakartrī
dvijairamībhiḥ samupāsyamānā ||45||
rājau dvijānāmiha rājadantāḥ
saṃbibhrati śrotriyabibhramaṃ yat |
udvegarāgādimṛjāvadātāḥ
catvāra ete tadavaimi muktāḥ ||46||
śirīṣakoṣādapi komalāyā
vedhā vidhāyāṅgamaśeṣamasyāḥ |
prāptaprakarṣaḥ sukumārasarge
samāpayadvāci mṛdutvamudrām ||47||
prasūnabāṇādvayavādinī sā
kāciddvijenopaniṣatpikena |
asyāḥ kimāsyadvijarājato vā
nādhīyate bhaikṣabhujā tarubhyaḥ ||48||
padmāṅkasadmānamavekṣya lakṣmīm
ekasya viṣṇoḥ śrayaṇātsapatnīm |
āsyendumasyā bhajate jitābjaṃ
sarasvatī tadvijigīṣayā kim ||49||
kaṇṭhe vasantī caturā yadasyāḥ
sarasvatī vādayate vipañcīm |
tadeva vāgbhūya mukhe mṛgākṣyāḥ
śrotuḥ śrutau yāti sudhārasatvam ||50||
vilokitāsyā mukhamunnamayya
kiṃ vedhaseyaṃ suṣamāsamāptau |
dhṛtyudbhavā yaccibuke cakāsti
nimne manāgaṅguliyantraṇeva ||51||
priyāmukhībhūya sukhī sudhāṃśuḥ
jayatyayaṃ rāhubhayavyayena |
imāṃ dadhārādharabimbalīlāṃ
tasyaiva bālaṃ karacakravālam ||52||
asyā mukhasyāstu na pūrṇamāsyaṃ
pūrṇasya jitvā mahimā himāṃśum |
bhrūlakṣma khaṇḍaṃ dadhadardhaminduḥ
bhālastṛtīyaḥ khalu yasya bhāgaḥ ||53||
vyadhatta dhātā mukhapadmamasyāḥ
samrājamambhojakule'khile'pi |
sarojarājau sṛjato'dasīyāṃ
netrābhidheyāvata eva sevām ||54||
divārajanyo ravisomabhīte
candrāmbuje nikṣipataḥ svalakṣmīm |
āsya yadāsyā na tadā tayoḥ śrīekaśriyedaṃ tu kadā na kāntam ||55||
asyā mukhaśrīpratibimbameva
jalācca tātānmukurācca mitrāt |
abhyarthya dhattaḥ khalu padmacandrau
vibhūṣaṇaṃ yācitakaṃ kadācit ||56||
arkāya patye khalu tiṣṭhamānā
bhṛṅgairmitāmakṣibhirambukelau |
bhaimīmukhasya śriyamambujinyo
yācanti vistāritapadmahastāḥ ||57||
asyā mukhenaiva vijitya nityaspardhī milatkuṅkumaroṣabhāsā |
prasahya candraḥ khalu nahyamānaḥ
syādeva tiṣṭhatpariveṣapāśaḥ ||58||
vidhorvidhirbimbaśatāni lopaṃ
lopaṃ kuhūrātriṣu māsi māsi |
abhaṅguraśrīkamamuṃ kimasyā
mukhendumasthāpayadekaśeṣam ||59||
kapolapatrānmakarātsaketuḥ
bhrūmyāṃ jigīṣurdhanuṣā jaganti |
ihāvalambyāsti ratiṃ manobhū
rajyadvayasyo madhunādhareṇa ||60||
viyogabāṣpāñcitanetrapadmacchadmārpitotsargapayaḥ prasūnau |
karṇo kimasyā ratitatpatibhyāṃ
nivedyapūpau vidhiśilpamīdṛk ||61||
ihāviśadyena pathātivakraḥ
śāstraughaniṣyandasudhāpravāhaḥ |
so'syāḥ śravaḥ patrayuge praṇālirekheva dhāvatyabhikarṇakūpam ||62||
asyā yadaṣṭādaśa saṃvibhajya
vidyāḥ śrutī dadhraturardhamardham |
karṇāntarutkīrṇagabhīrarekhaḥ
kiṃ tasya saṃkhyaiva navā navāṅkaḥ ||63||
manye'munā karṇalatāmayena
pāśadvayena cchiduretareṇa |
ekākipāśaṃ varuṇaṃ vijigye'–
naṅgīkṛtāyāsatatī ratīśaḥ ||64||
ātmaiva tātasya caturbhujasya
jātaścaturdorucitaḥ smaro'pi |
taccāpayoḥ karṇalate bhruvorjye
vaṃśatyagaṃśau cipiṭe kimasyāḥ ||65||
grīvādbhutaivāvaṭuśobhitāpi
prasādhitā māṇavakena seyam |
āliṅgyatāmapyabalambamānā
surūpatābhāgakhilordhvakāyā ||66||
kavitvagānapriyavādasatyān
yasyā vidhātā vyadhitādhikaṇṭham |
rekhātrayanyāsamiṣādamīṣāṃ
vāsāya so'yaṃ vibabhāja sīmāḥ ||67||
bāhū priyāyā jayatāṃ mṛṇālaṃ
dvandve jayo nāma na vismayo'smin |
uccaistu taccitramamuṣya bhagnaḥ
yolokyate nirvyathanaṃ yadantaḥ ||68||
ajīyatāvartaśubhaṃyunābhyāṃ
dorbhyāṃ mṛṇālaṃ kimu komalābhyām |
niḥsūtramāste ghanapaṅkamṛtsu
mūrtāsu nākīrtiṣu tannimagnam ||69||
rajyannakhasyāṅgulipañcakasya
miṣādasau haiṅgulapadmatūṇe |
haimaikapuṅkhāsti viśuddhaparvā
priyākare pañcaśarī smarasya ||70||
asyāḥ karaspardhanagardhanarddhiḥ
bālatvamāpat khalu pallavo yaḥ |
bhūyo'pi nāmādharasāmyagarvaṃ
kurvankathaṃ vāstu na sa pravālaḥ ||71||
asyaiva sargāya bhavatkarasya
sarojasṛṣṭirmama hastalekhaḥ |
ityāha dhātā hariṇekṣaṇāyāṃ
kiṃ hastalekhīkṛtayā tayā'syām ||72||
kiṃ narmadāyā mama seyamasyā
dṛśyābhito bāhulatāmṛṇālī |
kucau kimuttasthaturantarīye
smaroṣmaśuṣyattarabālyavāraḥ ||73||
tālaṃ prabhu syādanukartumetā
vutthānasusthau patitaṃ na tāvat |
paraṃ ca nāśritya taruṃ mahāntaṃ
kucau kṛśāṅgyāḥ svata eva tuṅgau ||74||
etatkucaspardhitayā ghaṭasya
khyātasya śāstreṣu nidarśanatvam |
tasmācca śilpānmaṇikādikārī
prasiddhanāmājani kumbhakāraḥ ||75||
gucchālayasvacchatamodabindu
vṛndābhamuktāphalaphenilāṅke |
māṇikyahāsya vidarbhasubhrū
payodhare rohati rohitaśrīḥ ||76||
niḥśaṅkasaṃkocitapaṅkajoyam
asyāmudīto mukhamindubimbaḥ |
citraṃ tathāpi stanakokayugmaṃ
na stokamapyañcati viprayogam ||77||
ābhyāṃ kucābhyāmibhakumbhayoḥ śrīrādīyate'sāvanayorna tābhyām |
bhayena gopāyitamauktikau tau
pravyaktamuktabharaṇāvimau yat ||78||
karāgrajāgracchatakoṭirarthī
yayorimau tau tulayetkucau cet |
sarvaṃ tadā śrīphalamunmadiṣṇu
jātaṃ vaṭīmapyadhunā na labdhum ||79||
stanāvaṭe candanapaṅkile'syā
jātasya yāvadyuvamānasānām |
hārāvalīratnamayūkhadhārākārāḥ sphuranti skhalanasya rekhāḥ ||80||
kṣīṇena madhye'pi satodareṇa
yatprāpyate nākramaṇaṃ valibhyaḥ |
sarvāṅgaśuddhau tadanaṅgarājya
vijṛmbhitaṃ bhīmabhuvīha citram ||81||
madhyaṃ tanūkṛtya yadīdamīyaṃ
vedhā na dadhyātkamanīyamaṃśam |
kena stanau saṃprati yauvane'syāḥ
sṛjedananyapratimāṅgayaṣṭeḥ ||82||
gaurīva patyā subhagā kadācit
karteyamapyardhatanūsamasyām |
itīva madhye vidadhe vidhātā
romāvalīmecakasūtramasyāḥ ||83||
romāvalīrajjumurojakumbhau
gambhīramāsādya ca nābhikūpam |
sadraṣṭitṛṣṇā viramedyadi syān
naiṣāṃ bataiṣā sicayena guptiḥ ||84||
unmūlitālānavilābhanābhiḥ
chinnaskhalacchṛṅkhalaromarājiḥ |
mattasya seyaṃ madanadvipasya
prasvāpavaproccakucāstu vāstu ||85||
romāvalibhrūkusumaiḥ svamaurvīcāpeṣubhirmadhyalalāṭamūrdhni |
vyastairapi sthāsnubhiretadīyairjaitraḥ sa citraṃ ratijānivīraḥ ||86||
puṣpāṇi bāṇāḥ kucamaṇḍanāni
bhruvau dhanurbhālamalaṃkariṣṇu |
romāvalī madhyavibhūṣaṇaṃ jyā
tathāpi jetā ratijāniretaiḥ ||87||
asyāḥ khalu granthinibaddhakeśamallīkadambapratibimbaveṣāt |
smarapraśastī rajatākṣareyaṃ
pṛṣṭhasthalīhāṭakapaṭṭikāyām ||88||
cakreṇa viśvaṃ yudhi matsyaketuḥ
piturjitaṃ vīkṣya sudarśanena |
jagajjigīṣatyamunā nitambam
ayena kiṃ durlabhadarśanena ||89||
romavalīdaṇḍanitambacakre
guṇaṃ ca lāvaṇyajalaṃ ca bālā |
tāruṇyamūrteḥ kucakumbhakartuḥ
bibharti śaṅkeḥ sahakāricakram ||90||
aṅgena kenāpi vijetumasyā
gaveṣyate kiṃ calapatrapatram |
na cedviśeṣāditaracchadebhyaḥ
tasyāstu kāmpastu kuto bhayena ||91||
bhrūścitralekhā ca tilottamāsyā
nāsā ca rambhā ca yadūrusṛṣṭiḥ |
dṛṣṭā tataḥ pūrayatīyamekān
ekāpsaraḥ prekṣaṇakautukāni ||92||
rambhāpi kiṃ cihnayati prakāṇḍaṃ
na cātmanaḥ svena na caitadūru |
svasyaiva yenopari sā dadānā
patrāṇi jāgartyanayorbhrameṇa ||93||
vidhāya mūrdhānamadaścaraṃ cenmuñcettapobhiḥ smarasāmabhāvam |
jāḍyaṃ ca nāñcetkadalī balīyastadā yadi syādidamūrucāruḥ ||94||
ūruprakāṇḍadvitayena tanvyāḥ
karaḥ parājīyata vāraṇīyaḥ |
yuktaṃ hriyā kuṇḍalanacchalena
gopāyati svaṃ mukhapuṣkaraṃ saḥ ||95||
asyāṃ munīnāmapi mohamūhe
bhṛgurmahānyatkucaśailaśīlī |
nānāradāhlādi mukhaṃ śritoruḥ
vyāso mahābhāratasargayogyaḥ ||96||
kramādgatā pīvaratādhijaṅghaṃ
vṛkṣādhirūḍhaṃ viduṣī kimasyāḥ |
api bhramībhaṅgibhirāvṛtāṅgaṃ
vāso latāveṣṭitakapravīṇam ||97||
arundhatīkāmapuraṃghrilakṣmījanbhadviṣaddāranavāmbikānām |
caturdaśīyaṃ tadihocitaiva
gulphadvayāptā yadadṛśyasiddhiḥ ||98||
asyāḥ padau cārutayā mahāntāvapekṣya saukṣmyāllavabhāvabhājaḥ |
jātā pravālasya mahīruhāṇāṃ
jānīmahe pallavaśabdalabdhiḥ ||99||
jagadvadhūmūrdhasu rūpadarpād
yadetayādāyi padāravindam |
tatsāndrasindūraparāgarāgaiḥ
dhruvaṃ pravālaprabalāruṇaṃ tat ||100||
ruṣāruṇā sarvaguṇairjayantyā
bhaimyāḥ padaṃ śrīḥ sma vidhervṛṇīte |
dhruvaṃ sa tāmacchalayadyataḥ sā
bhṛśāruṇaitatpadabhāgvibhāti ||101||
yānena tanvyā jitadantināthau
pādābjarājau pariśuddhapārṣṇī |
jāne na śuśrūṣayituṃ svamicchū
natena mūrdhnā katarasya rājñaḥ ||102||
karṇākṣidantacchadabāhupāṇipādādinaḥ svākhilatulyajetuḥ |
udvegabhāgadvayatābhimānādihaiva vedhā vyadhita dvitīyam ||103||
tuṣāraniḥśeṣitamabjasargaṃ
vidhātukāmasya punarvidhātuḥ |
pañcasvihāsyāṅghrikareṣvabhikhyābhikṣādhunā mādhukarīsadṛkṣā ||104||
eṣyanti yāvadgaṇanāddigantān
nṛpāḥ smarārtāḥ śaraṇe praveṣṭum |
ime padābje vidhināpi sṛṣṭāḥ
tāvatya evāṅgulayo'tra lekhāḥ ||105||
priyānakhībhūtavato mudedaṃ
vyadhādvidhiḥ sādhudaśatvamindoḥ |
etatpadacchadmasarāgapadmasaubhāgyabhāgyaṃ kathamanyathā syāt ||106||
yaśaḥ padāṅguṣṭhanakhau mukhaṃ ca
bibharti pūrṇenducatuṣṭayaṃ yā |
kalā catuḥṣaṣṭirupaitu vāsaṃ
tasyāṃ kathaṃ subhruvi nāma nāsyām ||107||
sṛṣṭātiviśvā vidhinaiva tāvat
tasyāpi nītopari yauvanena |
vaidagdhyamadhyāpya manobhuveyam
avāpitā vākpathapārameva ||108||
iti sa cikurādārabhyaināṃ nakhāvadhi varṇayan
hariṇaramaṇīnetrāṃ citrāmbudhau taradantaraḥ |
hṛdayabharaṇodvelanandaḥ sakhīvṛtabhīmajānayanaviṣayībhāve bhāvaṃ dadhāra dharādhipaḥ ||109||
śrīharṣaṃ kavirājarājimukuṭālaṃkārahīraḥ sutaṃ
śrīhīraḥ suṣuve jitendriyacayaṃ māmalladevī ca yam |
gauḍarvīśakulapraśastibhaṇitibhrātaryayaṃ tanmahākāvye cāruṇi vairasenicarite sargo'gamatsaptamaḥ ||110 ||


aṣṭamaḥ sargaḥ
athādbhutenāstanimeṣamudram
unnidralomānamamuṃ yuvānam |
dṛśā parustāḥ sudṛśaḥ samastāḥ
sutā ca bhīmasya mahīmaghonaḥ ||1||
kiyacciraṃ daivatabhāṣitāni
nihnotumenaṃ prabhavantu nāma |
palālajālaiḥ pihitaḥ svayaṃ hi
prakāśamāsādayatīkṣuḍimbhaḥ ||2||
apāṅgamapyāya dṛśorna raśmiḥ
nalasya bhaimīmabhilaṣya yāvat |
smarāśugaḥ subhruvi tāvadasyāṃ
pratyaṅgamāpuṅkhaśikhaṃ mamajja ||3||
yadakramaṃ vikramaśaktisāmyād
upācaraddvāvapi pañcabāṇaḥ |
kathaṃ na vaimatyamamuṣya cakre
śarairanardhārdhavibhāgabhāgmiḥ ||4||
tasminnalo'sāviti sānvarajyat
kṣaṇaṃ kṣaṇaṃ kveha sa ityudāsta |
punaḥ sma tasyāṃ valate'sya cittaṃ
dūtyādanenātha punarnyavarti ||5||
kayācidālokya nalaṃ lalajje
kayāpi tadbhāsi hṛdā mamajje |
taṃ kāpi mene smarameva kanyā
bheje manobhūvaśabhūyamanyā ||6||
kastvaṃ kuto veti na jātu śekuḥ
taṃ praṣṭumapyapratibhātibhārāt |
uttasthurabhyutthitivāñchayeva
nijāsanānnaikarasāḥ kṛśāṅgyaḥ ||7||
svācchandyamānandaparaṃparāṇāṃ
bhaimī tamālokya kimapyavāpa |
mahārayaṃ nirjhariṇīva vārām
āsādya dhārādharakelikālam ||8||
tatraiva magnā yadapaśyadagre
nāsyā dṛgasyāṅgamayāsyadanyat |
nādāsyadasyai yadi buddhidhārāṃ
vicchidya vicchidya cirānnimeṣaḥ ||9||
dṛśāpi sāliṅgitamaṅgamasya
jagrāha nāgrāvagatāṅgaharṣaiḥ |
aṅgāntare'nantaramīkṣite tu
nivṛtya sasmāra na pūrvadṛṣṭam ||10||
hitvaikamasyāpaghanaṃ viśantī
taddṛṣṭiraṅgāntarabhuktisīmām |
ciraṃ cakārobhayalābhalobhāt
svabhāvalolā gatamāgataṃ ca ||11||
nirīkṣitaṃ cāṅgamavīkṣitaṃ ca
dṛśā pibantī rabhasena tasya |
samānamānandamiyaṃ dadhānā
viveda bhedaṃ na vidarbhasubhrūḥ ||12||
sūkṣme ghane naiṣadhakeśapāśe
nipatya nispandatarībhavadbhyām |
tasyānubandhaṃ na vimocya gantum
apāri tallocanakhañjanābhyām ||13||
bhūlokabharturmukhapāṇipādapadmaiḥ parīrambhamavāpya tasya |
damasvasurdṛṣṭisarojarājiḥ
ciraṃ na tatyāja sabandhubandham ||14||
tatkālamānandamayī bhavantī
bhavattarānirvacanīyamohā |
sā muktasaṃsāridaśārasābhyāṃ
dvisvādamullāsamabhuṅkta miṣṭam ||15||
dūte nalaśrībhṛti bhāvibhāvā
kalaṅkinīyaṃ jani meti nūnam |
na saṃvyadhānnaiṣadhakāyamāyaṃ
vidhiḥ svayaṃdūtamimāṃ pratīndram ||16||
puṇye manaḥ kasya munerapi syāt
pramāṇamāste yadadhe'pi dhāvat |
taccinti cittaṃ parameśvarastu
bhaktasya hṛṣyatkaruṇo ruṇaddhi ||17||
sālīkadṛṣṭe madanonmadiṣṇuḥ
yathāpa śālīnatamā na maunam |
tathaiva tathye'pi nale na lebhe
mugdheṣu kaḥ satyamṛṣāvivekaḥ ||18||
vyarthībhavadbhāvapidhānayatnā
svareṇa sātha ślathagadgadena |
sakhīcaye sādhvasabaddhavāci
svayaṃ tamūce namadānanenduḥ ||19||
natvā śiroratnarucāpi pādyaṃ
saṃpādyamācāravidātithibhyaḥ |
priyākṣarālīrasadhārayāpi
vaidhī vidheyā madhuparkatṛptiḥ ||20||
svātmāpi śīlena tṛṇaṃ vidheyaṃ
deyā vihāyāsanabhūrnijāpi |
ānandabāṣpairapi kalpyamambhaḥ
pṛcchā vidheyā madhubhirvacobhiḥ ||21||
padopahāre'nupanamratāpi
saṃbhāvyate'pāṃ tvarayāparādhaḥ |
tatkartumarhāñjalisañjanena
svasaṃbhṛtiḥ prāñjalatāpi tāvat ||22||
purā parityajya mayātyasarji
svamāsanaṃ tatkimiti kṣaṇaṃ na |
anarhamapyetadalaṃkriyeta
prayātumīhā yadi cānyato'pi ||23||
nivedyatāṃ hanta samāpayantau
śirīṣakoṣabhradimābhimānam |
pādau kiyaddūramimau prayāse
nidhitsate tucchadayaṃ manaste ||24||
anāyi deśaḥ katamastvayādya
vasantamuktasya daśāṃ vanasya |
tvadāptasaṃketatayā kṛtārthā
śravyāpi nānena janena saṃjñā ||25||
tīrṇaḥ kimarṇonidhireva naiṣa
surakṣite'bhūdiha yatpraveśaḥ |
phalaṃ kimetasya tu sāhasasya
na tāvadadyāpi viniścinomi ||26||
tava praveśe sukṛtāni hetuḥ
manye madakṣṇorapi tāvadatra |
na lakṣito rakṣibhaṭairyadābhyāṃ
pīto'si tanvā jitapuṣpadhanvā ||27||
yathākṛtiḥ kācana te yathā vā
dauvārikāndhaṃkaraṇī ca śaktiḥ |
rucyo rucībhirjitakāñcanībhiḥ
tathāsi pīyūṣabhujāṃ sanābhiḥ ||28||
na manmathastvaṃ sa hi nāstimūrtiḥ
na cāśvineyaḥ sa hi nādvitīyaḥ |
cihnaiḥ kimanyairathavā taveyaṃ
śrīreva tābhyāmadhiko viśeṣaḥ ||29||
ālokatṛptīkṛtaloka ! yastvām
asūta pīyūṣamayūkhamenam |
kaḥ spardhituṃ dhāvati sādhu sārdham
udanvatā nanvayamanvavāyaḥ ||30||
bhūyo'pi bālā nalasundaraṃ taṃ
matvāmaraṃ rakṣijanākṣibandhāt |
ātithyacāṭūnyapadiśya tatsthāṃ
śriyaṃ priyasyāstuta vastutaḥ sā ||31||
vāgjanmavaiphalyamasahyaśalyaṃ
guṇādbhute vastuni maunitā cet |
khalatvamalpīyasi jalpite'pi
tadastu bandibhramabhūmitaiva ||32||
kaṃdarpa evedamavindata tvāṃ
puṇyena manye punaranyajanma |
caṇḍīśacaṇḍākṣihutāśakuṇḍe
juhāva yanmandiramindriyāṇām ||33||
śobhāyaśobhirjitaśaivaśailaṃ
karopi lajjāgurumaulimailam |
dasrau haṭhaśrīharaṇādudasrau
kaṃdarpamapyujjhitarūpadarpam ||34||
avaimi haṃsāvalayo valakṣāḥ
tvatkāntikīrteścapalāḥ pulākāḥ |
uḍḍīya yuktaṃ patitāḥ sravantīveśantapūraṃ paritaḥ plavante ||35||
bhavatpadāṅguṣṭhamapi śritā śrīḥ
dhruvaṃ na labdhā kusumāyudhena |
ratīśajetuḥ khalu cihnamasmin
nardhendurāste nakhaveṣadhārī ||36||
rājā dvijānāmanumāsabhinnaḥ
pūrṇāṃ tanūkṛtya tanūṃ tapobhiḥ |
kuhūṣu dṛśyetaratāṃ kimetya
sāyujyamāpnoti bhavanmukhasya ||37||
kṛtvā dṛśau te bahuvarṇacitre
kiṃ kṛṣṇasārasya tayormṛgasya |
adūrajāgradvidarapraṇālīrekhāmayacchadvidhirardhacandram ||38||
mugdhaḥ sa mohātsubhagānna dehāt
dadadbhavadbhrūracanāya cāpam |
bhrūmaṅgajeyastava yanmanobhūḥ
anena rūpeṇa yadātadābhūt ||39||
mṛgasya netradvitayaṃ tavāsye
vidhau vidhutvānumitasya dṛśyam |
tasyaiva cañcatkacapāśaveṣaḥ
puccha sphuraccāmaraguccha eṣaḥ ||40||
āstāmanaṅgīkaraṇādbhavena
dṛśyaḥ smaro neti purāṇavāṇī |
tavaiva dehaṃ śritayā śriyeti
navastu vastu pratibhāti vādaḥ ||41||
tvayā jagatyuccitakāntisāre
yadindunāśīli śiloñchavṛttiḥ |
āropi tanmāṇavako'pi maulau
sa yajvarājye'pi maheśvareṇa ||42||
ādehadāhaṃ kusumāyudhasya
vidhāya saundaryakathādaridram |
tvadaṅgaśilpātpunarīśvareṇa
cireṇa jāne jagadanvakampi ||43||
mahī kṛtārthā yadi mānavo'si
jitaṃ divā yadyamareṣu ko'pi |
kulaṃ tvayālaṃkṛtamauragaṃ cen
nādho'pi kasyopari nāgalokaḥ ||44||
seyaṃ na dhatte'nupapattimuccaiḥ
maccittavṛttistvayi cintyamāne |
mamau sa bhadraṃ culuke samudraḥ
tvayāttagāmbhīryamahattvamudraḥ ||45||
saṃsārasindhāvanubimbamatra
jāgarti jāne tava vairaseniḥ |
bimbānubimbau hi vihāya dhātuḥ
na jātu dṛṣṭātisarūpasṛṣṭiḥ ||46||
iyatkṛtaṃ kena mahījagatyām
aho mahīyaḥ sukṛtaṃ janena |
pādau yamuddiśya tavāpi padyārajaḥsu padmasrajamārabhete ||47||
bravīti me kiṃ kimiyaṃ na jāne
saṃdehadolāmavalambya saṃvit |
kasyāpi dhanyasya gṛhātithistvamalīkasaṃbhāvanayāthavālam ||48||
prāptyaiva tāvattava rūpasṛṣṭiṃ
nipīya dṛṣṭirjanuṣaḥ phalaṃ me |
api śrutī nāmṛtamādriyetāṃ
tayoḥ prasādīkuruṣe giraṃ cet ||49||
itthaṃ madhūtthaṃ rasamudgirantī
tadoṣṭhabandhūkadhanurvisṛṣṭā |
karṇātprasūnāśugapañcabāṇī
vāṇīmiṣeṇāsya mano viveśaḥ ||50||
amajjadākaṇṭhamasau sudhāsu
priyaṃ priyāyā vacanaṃ nipīya |
dviṣanmukhe'pi svadate stutiryā
tanmiṣṭatā neṣṭamukhe tvameyā ||51||
paurastyaśailaṃ janatopanītāṃ
gṛhṇan yathāhnaḥ patirarghyapūjām |
tathātitheyīmatha saṃpratīccha
priyārpitāmāsanamāsasāda ||52||
ayodhi taddhairyamanobhavābhyāṃ
tāmeva bhaimīmavalambya bhūmīm |
āha sma yatra smaracāpamantaḥ
chinnaṃ bhruvo tajjayabhaṅgavārtām ||53||
atha smarajñāmavadhīrya dhairyād
uce sa tadvāgupavīṇito'pi |
vivekadhārāśatadhautamantaḥ
satāṃ na kāmaḥ kaluṣīkaroti ||54||
haritpatīnāṃ sadasaḥ pratīhi
tvadīyamevātithimāgataṃ mām |
vahantamantarguruṇādareṇa
prāṇāniva svaḥprabhuvācikāni ||55||
viramyatāṃ bhūtavatī saparyā
niviśyatāmāsanamujjhitaṃ kim |
yā dūtatā naḥ phalinā vidheyā
saivātitheyī pṛthurudbhavitrī ||56||
kalyāṇi ! kalyāni tavāṅgakāni
kaccittamāṃ cittamanāvilaṃ te |
alaṃ vilambena giraṃ madīyām
ākarṇayākarṇataṭāyatākṣi ! ||57||
kaumāramārabhya gaṇā guṇānāṃ
haranti te dikṣu dhṛtādhipatyān |
surādhirājaṃ salilādhipaṃ ca
hutāśanaṃ cāryamanandanaṃ ca ||58||
caracciraṃ śaiśavayauvanīyad
vairājyabhāji tvayi khedameti |
teṣāṃ rucaścauratareṇa cittaṃ
pañceṣuṇā luṇṭhitadhairyavittam ||59||
teṣāmidānīṃ kila kevalaṃ sā
hṛdi tvadāśā vilasatyajasram |
āśāstu nāsādya tanūrudārāḥ
pūrvādayaḥ pūrvavadātmadārāḥ ||60||
anena sārdhaṃ tava yauvanena
koṭiṃ parāmacchiduro'dhyarohat |
premāpi tanvi ! tvayi vāsavasya
guṇo'pi cāpe sumanaḥ śarasya ||61||
prācīṃ prayāte virahādayaṃ te
tāpācca rūpācca śaśāṅkaśaṅkī |
parāparādhairnidadhāti bhānau
ruṣāruṇaṃ locanavṛndamindraḥ ||62||
trinetramātreṇa ruṣā kṛtaṃ yat
tadeva yo'dyāpi na saṃvṛṇoti |
na veda ruṣṭe'dya sahasranetre
gantā sa kāmaḥ khalu kāmavasthām ||63||
pikasya vāṅmātrakṛtādvyalīkān
narādhanaṃ śīlati śūlino'pi ||64||
tamomayīkṛtya diśaḥ parāgaiḥ
smareṣavaḥ śakradṛśāṃ diśanti |
kuhūgiraścañcupuṭaṃ dvijasya
rākārajanyāmapi satyavācam ||65||
śaraiḥ prasūnaistudataḥ smarasya
smartuṃ sa kiṃ nāśaninā karoti |
abhedyamasyāhaha varma na syāt
anaṅgatā cedgiriśaprasādaḥ ||66||
dhṛtādhṛtestasya bhavadviyogāt
anyānyaśayyāracanāya lūnaiḥ |
apyanyadāridryaharāḥ pravālaiḥ
jātā daridrāstaravo'marāṇām ||67||
ravairguṇāsphālabhavaiḥ smarasya
svarṇāthakarṇau badhirāvabhūtām |
guroḥ śrṛṇoti smaramohanidrāprabodhadakṣāṇi kimakṣarāṇi ||68||
anaṅgatāpapraśamāya tasya
kadarthyamānā muhurāmṛṇālam |
madhau madhau nākamadīnalinyo
varaṃ vahantāṃ śiśire'nurāgam ||69||
damasvasaḥ ! seyamupaiti tṛṣṇā
harerjagatyagrimalekhyalakṣmīm |
dṛśāṃ yadabdhistava nāma dṛṣṭitribhāgalobhārtimasau bibharti ||70||
agyāhitā nityamupāsate yāṃ
dedīpyamānāṃ tanumaṣṭamūrteḥ |
āśāpatiste damayanti ! so'pi
smareṇa dāsībhavituṃ nyadeśi ||71||
tvadgocarastaṃ khalu pañcabāṇaḥ
karoti saṃtāpya tathā vinītam .
svayaṃ yathā svāditataptabhūyaḥ
paraṃ na saṃtāpayitā sa bhūyaḥ ||72||
adāhi yastena daśārdhabāṇaḥ
purā purārernayanālayena |
na nirdahastaṃ bhavadakṣivāsī
na vairaśuddheradhunādhamarṇaḥ ||73||
somāya kupyanniva viprayuktaḥ
sa somamācāmati hūyamānam |
nāmāpi jāgarti hi yatra rātroḥ
tejasvinastaṃ katame sahante ||74||
śarairajasraṃ kusumāyudhasya
kadarthyamānastaruṇi ! tvadarthe |
abhyarcayadbhirvinivedyamānāt
apyeṣa manye kusumādbibheti ||75||
smarendhane vakṣasi tena dattā
saṃvartikā śaivalavallicitrā |
cakāsti cetobhavapāvakasya
dhūmāvilā kīlaparampareva ||76||
putrī suhṛdyena saroruhāṇāṃ
yatpreyasī candanavāsitā dik |
dhairyaṃ vibhuḥ so'pi tavaiva hetoḥ
smarapratāpajvalane juhāva ||77||
taṃ dahyamānairapi manmathaidyaṃ
hastairupāste malayaḥ pravālaiḥ |
kṛcchre'pyasau nojjhati tasya sevāṃ
sadā yadāśāmavalambate yaḥ ||78||
smarasya kīrtyeva sitīkṛtāni
taddoḥpratāpairiva tāpitāni |
aṅgāni dhatte sa bhavadviyogāt
pāṇḍūni caṇḍajvarajarjarāṇi ||79||
yastanvi ! bhartā ghusṛṇena sāyaṃ
diśaḥ samālambhanakautukinyāḥ |
tadā sa cetaḥ prajighāya tubhyaṃ
yadā gato naiti nivṛtya pānthaḥ ||80||
tathā na tāpāya payonidhīnām
aśvāmukhotthaḥ kṣudhitaḥ śikhāvān |
nijaḥ patiḥ saṃprati vāripo'pi
yathā hṛdisthaḥ smaratāpaduḥsthaḥ ||81||
yatpratyuta tvanmṛdubāhuvallīsmṛtisrajaṃ gumphati durvinītā |
tato vidhatte'dhikameva tāpaṃ
tena śritā śaityaguṇā mṛṇālī ||82||
nyastaṃ tatastena mṛṇāladaṇḍakhaṇḍaṃ babhāse hṛdi tāpabhāji |
taccittamagnairmadanasya bāṇaiḥ
kṛtaṃ śatacchidramiva kṣaṇena ||83||
iti trilokītilakeṣu teṣu
manobhuvo vikramakāmacāraḥ |
amoghamastraṃ bhavatīmavāpya
madāndhatānargalacāpalasya ||84||
sārotthadhāreva sudhārasasya
svayaṃvaraḥ śvo bhavitā taveti |
saṃtarpayantī damayanti ! teṣāṃ
śrutiḥ śrutī nākajuṣāmayāsīt ||85||
samaṃ sapatnībhavaduḥkhatīkṣṇaiḥ
svadāranāsāpathikairmarudbhiḥ |
anaṅgaśauryānalatāpaduḥsthaiḥ
atha pratasthe haritāṃ marudbhiḥ ||86||
apāstapāthepasudhopayogaiḥ
tvaccumbinaiva svamanorathena |
kṣudhaṃ ca nirvāpayatā tṛṣaṃ ca
svādīyasā'dhvā gamitaḥ sukhaṃ taiḥ ||87||
priyā manobhūśaradāvadāhe
devīstvadarthena nimajjayadbhiḥ |
sureṣu sāraiḥ kriyate'dhunā taiḥ
pādārpaṇānugrahabhūriyaṃ bhūḥ ||88||
alaṃkṛtāsannamahīvibhāgaiḥ
ayaṃ janastairamarairbhavatyām |
avāpito jaṅgamalaikhalakṣmīṃ
nikṣipya saṃdeśamayākṣarāṇi ||89||
ekaikamete parirabhya pīnastanopapīḍaṃ tvayi saṃdiśanti |
tvaṃ mūrcchatāṃ naḥ smarabhillaśalyaiḥ
mude viśalyauṣadhivalliredhi ||90||
tvatkāntimasmābhirayaṃ pipāsan
manorathāśvāsanayaikayaiva |
nijaḥ kaṭākṣaḥ khalu vipralabhyaḥ
kiyanti yāvadbhaṇa vāsarāṇi ||91||
nije sajāsmāsu bhujai bhajantyāvādityavarge pariveṣaveṣam |
prasīda nirvāpaya tāpamaṅgaiḥ
anaṅgalīlālaharītuṣāraiḥ ||92||
dayasva kiṃ ghātayasi tvamasmān
anaṅgacaṇḍālaśarairadṛśyaiḥ |
bhinnā varaṃ tīkṣṇakaṭākṣabāṇaiḥ
premastava premarasātpavitraiḥ ||93||
tvadarthinaḥ santu parassahasrāḥ
prāṇāstu nastvaccaraṇaprasādaḥ |
viśaṅkase kaitavanartitaṃ cet
antaścaraḥ pañcaśaraḥ pramāṇam ||94||
asmākamadhyāsitametadantaḥ
tāvadbhavatyā hṛdayaṃ cirāya |
bahistvayālaṃkriyatāmidānīm
uro muraṃ vidviṣataḥ śriyeva ||95||
dayodayaścetasi cettavābhūt
alaṃkuru dyāṃ viphalo vilambaḥ |
bhuvaḥ svarādeśamathācarāmo
bhūmau dhṛtiṃ yāsi yadi svabhūmau ||96||
dhinoti nāsmāñjalajena pūjā
tvayānvahaṃ tanvi ! vitanyamānā |
tava prasādopanate tu maulau
pūjāstu nastvatpadapaṅkajābhyām ||97||
svarṇairvitīrṇaiḥ karavāma vāmanetre ! bhavatyā kimupāsanāsu |
aṅga ! tvadaṅgāni nipītapītadarpāṇi pāṇiḥ khalu yācate naḥ ||98||
vayaṃ kalādā iva durvidagdhaṃ
tvadgaurimaspardhi dahema hema |
prasūnanārācaśarāsanena
sahaikavaṃśaprabhavabhru ! babhru ||99||
sudhāsaraḥsu tvadanaṅgatāpaḥ
śānto na naḥ kiṃ purapyasaraḥsu |
nirvāti tu tvanmamatākṣareṇa
sūnāśugeṣormadhusīkareṇa ||100||
khaṇḍaḥ kimu tvadgira eva khaṇḍaḥ
kiṃ śarkarā tatpathaśarkaraiva |
kṛśāṅgi ! tadbhaṅgirasotthakacchatṛṇaṃ nu dikṣu prathitaṃ tadikṣuḥ ||101||
dadāma kiṃ te sudhayā'dhareṇa
tvadāsya eva svayamāsyate hi |
candraṃ vijitya svayameva bhāvi
tvadānanaṃ tanmakhabhāgabhoji ||102||
priye ! vṛṇīṣvāmarabhāvamaśmat
iti trapākṛdvacanaṃ na kiṃ naḥ |
tvatpādapadme śaraṇaṃ praviśya
svayaṃ vayaṃ yena jijīviṣāmaḥ ||103||
nāsmākamasmānmadanāpamṛtyoḥ
strāṇāya pīyūṣarasāyanāni |
prasīda asmādadhikaṃ nijaṃ tu
prayaccha pātuṃ radanacchadaṃ naḥ ||104||
pluṣṭaḥ svaiścāparopaiḥ saha sa hi makareṇātmabhūḥ ketunā'bhūddhattāṃ nastvatprasādādatha manasijatāṃ mānaso nandanaḥ san |
bhrūbhyāṃ te tanvi ! dhanvī bhavatu tava sitairjaitrabhallaḥ smitaiḥ stā
dastu tvannetracañcattaraśapharayugādhīnamīnadhvajāṅkaḥ ||105||
svapnena prāpitāyāḥ pratirajani tava śrīṣu magnaḥ kaṭākṣaḥ
śrotre gītāmṛtābdhau tvagapi nanu tanūmañjarīsaukumārye |
nāsā śvāsādhivāse'dharamadhuni rasajñā caritreṣu cittaṃ
tannastanvaṅgi ! kaiścinna karaṇahariṇairvāgurā laṅghitāsi ||106||
iti dhṛtasurasārthavācikasraṅgi
jarasanātalapatrahārakasya |
saphalaya mama dūtatāṃ vṛṇīṣva
svayamavadhārya digīśamekameṣu ||107||
ānandayendramatha manmathamagnamagniṃ
kelībhiruddhara tanūdari ! nūtanābhiḥ |
āsādayoditadayaṃ śamane mano vā
no vā yadītthamatha tadvaruṇaṃ vṛṇīthāḥ ||108||
śrīharṣaṃ kavirājarājimukuṭālaṃkārahīraḥ sutaṃ
śrīhīraḥ suṣuve jitendriyacayaṃ māmalladevī ca yam |
tasyāgādayamaṣṭamaḥ kavikulādṛṣṭādhvapānthe mahākāvye cāruṇi naiṣadhīyacarite sargo nisargojjvalaḥ ||109||

navamaḥ sargaḥ
itīyamakṣibhruvavibhrameṅgitasphuṭāmanicchāṃ vivarītumutsukā |
taduktimātraśravaṇecchayā'śṛṇod
digīśasaṃdeśagiraṃ na gauravāt ||1||
tadarpitāmaśrutavadvidhāya
tāṃ digīśasaṃdeśamayīṃ sarasvatīm |
idaṃ tamurvītalaśītaladyutiṃ
jagāda vaidarbhanarendranandinī ||2||
mayāṅga ! pṛṣṭaḥ kulanāmanī bhavān
amū vimucyaiva kimanyaduktavān |
na mahyamatrottaradhārayasya kiṃ
hriye'pi seyaṃ bhavato'dhamarṇatā ||3||
adṛśyamānā kvacidīkṣitā kvacin
mamānuyoge bhavataḥ sarasvatī |
kvacitprakāśāṃ kvacidasphuṭārṇasaṃ
sarasvatīṃ jetumanāḥ sarasvatīm ||4||
giraḥ śrutā eva tava śravaḥ sudhāḥ
ślathā bhavannāmni tu na śrutispṛhā |
pipāsutā śāntimupaiti vārijā
na jātu dugdhānmadhuno'dhikādapi ||5||
bibharti vaṃśaḥ katamastamopahaṃ
bhavādṛśaṃ nāyakaratnamīdṛśam |
tamanyasāmānyadhiyāvamānitaṃ
tvayā mahāntaṃ bahu mantumutsahe ||6||
itīrayitvā viratāṃ sa tāṃ punaḥ
girānujagrāhatarāṃ narādhipaḥ |
virutya viśrāntavatīṃ tapātyaye
ghanāghanaścātakamaṇḍalīmiva ||7||
aye ! mamodāsitameva jihvayā
dvaye'pi tasminnanatiprayojane |
garau giraḥ pallavanārthalāghave
mitaṃ ca sāraṃ ca vaco hi vāgmitā ||8||
vṛthā katheyaṃ mayi varṇapaddhatiḥ
kayānupūrvyā samaketi keti ca |
kṣame samakṣavyavahāramāvayoḥ
pade vidhātuṃ khalu yuṣmadasmadī ||9||
yadi svabhāvānmama nojjvalaṃ kulaṃ
tatastadudbhāvanamaucitī kutaḥ |
athāvadātaṃ tadaho viḍambanā
tathā kathā preṣyatayopaseduṣaḥ ||10||
iti pratītyaiva mayāvadhīrite
tavāpi nirbandharaso na śobhate |
haritpatīnāṃ prativācikaṃ prati
śramo girāṃ te ghaṭate hi saṃprati ||11||
tathāpi nirbandhati ! te'thavā spṛhām
ihānurundhe mitayā na kiṃ girā |
himāṃśuvaṃśasya karīrameva māṃ
niśamya kiṃ nāsi phalegrahigrahā ||12||
mahājanāvāraparamparedṛśī
svanāma nāmādadate na sādhavaḥ |
ato'bhidhātuṃ na tadutsahe punaḥ
janaḥ kilācāramucaṃ vigāyati ||13||
ado'yamālapya śikhīva śārado
babhūva tūṣṇīmahitāpakārakaḥ |
athāsyarāgasya dadhā pade pade
vacāṃsi haṃsīva vidarbhajādade ||14||
sudhāṃśuvaṃśābharaṇaṃ bhavāniti
śrute'pi nāpaiti viśeṣasaṃśayaḥ |
kiyatsu maunaṃ vitatā kiyatsu vāṅmahatyaho vañcanacāturī tava ||15||
mayāpi deyaṃ prativācikaṃ na te
svanāma matkarṇasudhāmakurvate |
pareṇa puṃsā hi mamāpi saṃkathā
kulābalācārasahāsanāsahā ||16||
hṛdābhinandya pratibandyanuttaraḥ
priyāgiraḥ sasmitamāha sa sma tām |
vadāmi vāmākṣi ! pareṣu mā kṣipa
svamīdṛśaṃ mākṣikamākṣipadvacaḥ ||17||
karoṣi nemaṃ phalinaṃ mama śramaṃ
diśo'nugṛhṇāsi na kaṃcana prabhum |
tvamitthamarhāsi surānupāsituṃ
rasāmṛtasnānapavitrayā girā ||18||
sureṣu saṃdeśayasīdṛśīṃ bahuṃ
rasasraveṇa stimitāṃ na bhāratīm |
madarpitā darpakatāpiteṣu yā
prayāti dāvārditadāvavṛṣṭitām ||19||
yathā yatheha tvadupekṣayānayā
nimeṣamapyeṣa jano vilambate |
ruṣā śaravyīkaraṇe divaukasāṃ
tathā tathādya tvarate rateḥ patiḥ ||20||
iyaccirasyāvadadhanti matpathe
kimindranetrāṇyaśanirna nirmamau |
dhigastu māṃ satvarakāryamantharaṃ
sthitaḥ parapreṣyaguṇo'pi yatra na ||21||
idaṃ nigadya kṣitibhartari sthite
tayābhyadhāyi svagataṃ vidagdhayā |
adhistri taṃ dūtayatāṃ bhuvaḥ smaraṃ
mano dadhatyā nayanaipuṇavyaye ||22||
jalādhipastvāmadiśanmayi dhruvaṃ
paretarājaḥ prajidhāya sa sphuṭam |
marutvataiva prahito'si niścitaṃ
niyojitaścordhvamukhena tejasā ||23||
atha prakāśaṃ nibhṛtasmitā satī
satīkulasyābharaṇaṃ kimapyasau |
punastadābhāṣaṇavibhramonmukhaṃ
mukhaṃ vidarbhādhipasaṃbhavā dadhau ||24||
vṛthāparīhāsa iti pragalbhatā
na neti ca tvādṛśi vāgvigarhaṇā |
bhavatyavajñā ca bhavatyanuttarāt
ataḥ praditsuḥ prativācamasmi te ||25||
kathaṃ nu teṣāṃ kṛpayāpi vāgasāvasāvi mānuṣyakalāñchane jane |
svabhāvabhaktipravaṇaṃ pratīśvarāḥ
kayā na vācā mudamudgiranti vā ||26||
aho mahendrasya kathaṃ mayaucitī
surāṅganāsaṃgamaśobhitābhṛtaḥ |
hradasya haṃsāvalimāṃsalaśriyo
balākayeva prabalā viḍambanā ||27||
puraḥ surīṇāṃ bhaṇa keva mānavī
na yatra tāstatra tu śobhikāpi sā |
akāñcane'kiṃcana nāyikāṅgake
kimārakūṭābharaṇena na śriyaḥ ||28||
yathā tathā nāma giraḥ kirantu
te śrutī punarme badhire tadakṣare |
pṛṣatkiśārī kurutāmasaṃgatāṃ
kathaṃ manovṛttimapi dvipādhipe ||29||
ado nigadyaiva natāsyayā tayā
śrutau lagitvābhihitālirālapat |
praviśya yanme hṛdayaṃ hriyāha tad
viniryadākarṇaya manmukhādhvanā ||30||
bibhemi cintāmapi kartumīdṛśīṃ
cirāya cittārpitanaiṣadheśvarā |
mṛṇālatantucchidurā satīsthitiḥ
lavādapi truṭyati cāpalātkila ||31||
mamāśayaḥ svapnadaśājñayāpi vā
nalaṃ vilaṅghyetaramaspṛśadyadi |
kutaḥ punastatra samastasākṣiṇī
nijaiva buddhirvibudhairna pṛcchyate ||32||
api svamasvapnamasūṣupannamī
parasya dārānanavaitumeva mām |
svayaṃ duradhvārṇavanāvikāḥ kathaṃ
spṛśantu vijñāya hṛdāpi tādṛśīm ||33||
anugrahaḥ kevalameṣa mādṛśe
manuṣyajanmanyapi yanmano jane |
sa cedvidheyastadamī tameva me
prasadya bhikṣāṃ vitarītumīśatām ||34||
api draḍhīyaḥ śṛṇu matpratiśrutaṃ
sa pīḍayetpāṇimimaṃ na cennṛpaḥ |
hutāśanodbandhanavārikāritāṃ
nijāyuṣastatkaravai svavairitām ||35||
niṣiddhamapyācaraṇīyamāpadi
kriyā satī nāvati yatra sarvathā |
ghanāmbunā rājapathe hi picchile
kvacidbudhairapyapathena gamyate ||36||
striyā mayā vāgmiṣu teṣu śakyate
na jātu samyagvitarītumuttaram |
tadatra madbhāṣitasūtrapaddhatau
prabandhṛtāstu pratibandhṛtā na te ||37||
nirasya dūtaḥ sma tathā visarjitaḥ
priyoktirapyāha kaduṣṇamakṣaram |
kutūhaleneva muhuḥ kuhūravaṃ
viḍambya ḍimbhena pikaḥ prakopitaḥ ||38||
aho manastvāmanu te'pi tanvate
tvamapyamībhyo vimukhīti kautukam |
kva vā nidhirnirdhanameti kiṃca taṃ
sa vākkavāṭaṃ ghaṭayannirasyate ||39||
sahākhilastrīṣu vahe'vahelayā
mahendrarāgādgurumādaraṃ tvayi |
tvamīdṛśi śreyasi saṃmukhe'pi taṃ
parāṅmukhī candramukhi ! nyavīvṛtaḥ ||40||
divaukasaṃ kāmayate na mānavī
navīnamaśrāvi tavānanādidam |
kathaṃ na vā durgrahadoṣa eṣa te
hitena samyagguruṇāpi śāmyate ||41||
anugrahādeva divaukasāṃ naro
nirasya mānuṣyakameti divyatām |
ayovikāre svaritatvamiṣyate
kuto'yasāṃ siddharasaspṛśāmapi ||42||
hariṃ parityajya nalābhilāṣukā
na lajjase vā viduṣibruvā katham ? |
upekṣitekṣoḥ karabhācchamīratāt
uruṃ vade tvāṃ karabhoru ! bhoriti ||43||
vihāya hā sarvasuparvanāyakaṃ
tvayā dhṛtaḥ kiṃ narasādhimabhramaḥ |
mukhaṃ vimucya śvasitasya dhārayā
vṛthaiva nāsāpathadhāvanaśramaḥ ||44||
tapo'nale juhvati sūrayastanuḥ
dive phalāyānyajanurbhaviṣṇave |
kare punaḥ karṣati saiva vihvalā
balādiva tvāṃ balase na bāliśe ! ||45||
yadi svamudbandhumanā vinā nalaṃ
bhaverbhavantīṃ harirantarikṣagām |
divisthitānāṃ prathitaḥ patistato
hariṣyati nyāyyamupekṣate hi kaḥ ||46||
nivekṣyase yadyanale nalojjhitā
sure tadasminmahatī dayā kṛtā |
cirādanenārthanayāpi durlabhaṃ
svayaṃ tvayaivāṅga ! yadaṅgamarpyate ||47||
jitaṃ jitaṃ tatkhalu pāśapāṇinā
vinā nalaṃ vāri yadi pravekṣyasi |
tadā tvadākhyānbahirapyasūnasau
payaḥpatirvakṣasi vakṣyatetarām ||48||
kariṣyase yadyata eva dūṣaṇāt
upāyamanyaṃ viduṣī svamṛtyave |
priyātithiḥ svena gṛhāgatā kathaṃ
na dharmarājaṃ caritārthayiṣyasi ? ||49||
niṣedhaveṣo vidhireṣa te'thavā
tavaiva yuktā khalu vāci vakratā |
vijṛmbhitaṃ yasya kila dhvaneridaṃ
vidagdhanārīvadanaṃ tadākaraḥ ||50||
bhramāmi te bhaimi ! sarasvatīrasapravāhacakreṣu nipatya katyadaḥ |
trapāmapākṛtya manākkuru sphuṭaṃ
kṛtārthanīyaḥ katamaḥ surottamaḥ ||51||
mataḥ kimairāvatakumbhakaitavapragalbhapīnastanadigdhavastava |
sahasranetrānna pṛthagmate mama
tvadaṅgalakṣmīmavagāhituṃ kṣamaḥ ||52||
prasīda tasmai damayanti ! saṃtataṃ
tvadaṅgasaṅgaprabhavairjagatprabhuḥ |
pulomajālocanatīkṣṇakaṇṭakaiḥ
tanuṃ ghanāmātanutāṃ sa kaṇṭakaiḥ ||53||
abodhi tattvaṃ dahane'nurajyase
svayaṃ khalu kṣatriyagotrajanmanaḥ |
vinā tamojasvinamanyataḥ kathaṃ
manorathaste valate vilāsini ! ||54||
tvayaikasatyā tanutāpaśaṅkayā
tato nivartya na manaḥ kathaṃcana |
himopamā tasya parīkṣaṇakṣaṇe
satīṣu vṛttiḥ śataśo nirūpitā ||55||
sa dharmarājaḥ khalu dharmaśīlayā
tvayāsti cittātithitāmavāpitaḥ |
mamāpi sādhuḥ pratibhātyayaṃ kramaḥ
cakāsti yogyena hi yogyasaṃgamaḥ ||56||
ajātavicchedalavaiḥ smarodbhavaiḥ
agastyabhāsā diśi nirmalatviṣi |
dhutāvadhiṃ kālamamṛtyuśaṅkitā
nimeṣavattena nayasva kelibhiḥ ||57||
śirīṣamṛdvī varuṇaṃ kimīhase
payaḥ prakṛtyā mṛduvargavāsavam |
vihāya sarvānvṛṇute sma kiṃ na
sā niśāpi śītāṃśumanena hetunā ||58||
asevi yastyaktadivā divāniśaṃ
śriyaḥ priyeṇānaṇurāmaṇīyakaḥ |
sahāmunā tatra payaḥpayonidhau
kṛśodari ! krīḍa yathāmanoratham ||59||
iti sphuṭaṃ tadvacasastayādarāt
suraspṛhāropaviḍambanādapi |
karāṅkasuptaikakapolakarṇayā
śrutaṃ ca tadbhāṣitamaśrutaṃ ca tat ||60||
cirādanadhyāyamavāṅmukhī mukhe
tataḥ sma sā vāsayate damasvasā |
kṛtāyataśvāsavimokṣaṇātha taṃ
kṣaṇādbabhāṣe karuṇaṃ vicakṣaṇā ||61||
vibhindatā duṣkṛtinīṃ mama śrutiṃ
digindradurvācikasūcisaṃcayaiḥ |
prayātajīvāmiva māṃ prati sphuṭaṃ
kṛtaṃ tvayāpyantakadūtatocitam ||62||
tvadāsyaniryanmadalīkaduryaśom
aṣīmayaṃ sallipirūpabhāgiva |
śrutaṃ mamāviśya bhavaddurakṣaraṃ
sṛjatyadaḥ kīṭavadutkaṭā rujaḥ ||63||
tamālirūce'tha vidarbhajeritā
pragāḍhamaunavratayaikayā sakhī |
trapāṃ samārādhayatīyamanyayā
bhavantamāha svarasajñayā mayā ||64||
tamarcituṃ madvaraṇasrajā nṛpaṃ
svayaṃvaraḥ saṃbhavitā paredyavi |
mamāsubhirgantumanāḥ puraḥ saraiḥ
tadantarāyaḥ punareṣa vāsaraḥ ||65||
tadadya viśramya dayāluredhi me
dinaṃ ninīṣāmi bhavadvilokinī |
nakhaiḥ kilākhyāyi vilikhya pakṣiṇā
tavaiva rūpeṇa samaḥ sa matpriyaḥ ||66||
dṛśordvayī te vidhināsti vañcitā
mukhasya lakṣmīṃ tava yanna vīkṣate |
asāvapi śvastadimāṃ nalānane
vilokya sāphalyamupaitu janmanaḥ ||67||
mamaiva pāṇaukaraṇe'gnisākṣikaṃ
prasaṅgasaṃpāditamaṅga ! saṃgatam |
na hā sahādhītidhṛtaḥ spṛhā kathaṃ
tavāryaputrīyamajaryamarjitum ? ||68||
digīśvarārthe na kathaṃcana tvayā
kadarthanīyāsmi kṛto'yamañjaliḥ |
prasadyatāṃ nādya nigādyamīdṛśaṃ
dṛśau dadhe bāṣparayāspade bhṛśam ||69||
vṛṇe digīśāniti kā kathā tathā
tvayīti nekṣe nalabhāmapīhayā |
satīvrate'gnau tṛṇayāmi jīvitaṃ
smarastu kiṃ vastu tadastu bhasma yaḥ ||70||
nyaveśi ratnatritaye jinena yaḥ
sa dharmacintāmaṇirujjhito yayā |
kapālikopānalabhasmanaḥ kṛte
tadeva bhasma svakule stṛtaṃ tayā ||71||
nipīya pīyūṣarasaurasīrasau
giraḥ svakaṃdarpahutāśanāhutīḥ |
kṛtāntadūtaṃ na tayā yathoditaṃ
kṛtāntameva svamamanyatādayam ||72||
sa bhinnamarmāpi tadartikākubhiḥ
svadūtadharmānna virantumaihata |
śanairaśaṃsannibhṛtaṃ viniśvasan
vicitravākcitraśikhaṇḍinandanaḥ ||73||
divodhavastvāṃ yadi kalpaśākhinaṃ
kadāpi yāceta nijāṅgaṇālayam |
kathaṃ bhaverasya na jīviteśvarī
na moghayācñaḥ sa hi bhīru ! bhūruhaḥ ||74||
śikhī vidhāya tvadavāptikāmanāṃ
svayaṃhutasvāṃśahaviḥ svamūrtiṣu |
kratuṃ vidhatte yadi sārvakāmikaṃ
kathaṃ sa mithyāstu vidhistu vaidikaḥ ||75||
sadā tadāśāmadhitiṣṭhataḥ karaṃ
varaṃ pradātuṃ calitādbalādapi |
muneragastyādvṛṇute sa dharmarāḍ
yadi tvadāptiṃ bhaṇa kā tadā gatiḥ ||76||
kratoḥ kṛte jāgrati vetti kaḥ kati
prabhorapāṃ veśmani kāmadhenavaḥ ? |
tvadarthamekāmapi yācate sa cet
pracetasaḥ pāṇigataiva vartase ||77||
na saṃnidhātrī yadi vighnasiddhaye
pativratā patyuranicchayā śacī |
sa eva rājavrajavaiśasātkutaḥ
parasparaspardhivaraḥ svayaṃvaraḥ ? ||78||
nijasya vṛttāntamajānatāṃ mitho
mukhasya roṣātparuṣāṇi jalpataḥ |
mṛdhaṃ kimacchatrakadaṇḍatāṇḍavaṃ
bhujābhuji kṣoṇibhujāṃ didṛkṣase ||79||
apārthayanyājakaphūtkṛtiśramaṃ
jvaledruṣā cedvapuṣā tu nānalaḥ |
alaṃ nalaḥ kartumanagnisākṣikaṃ
vidhiṃ vivāhe tava sārasākṣi ! kam ? ||80||
patiṃvarāyāḥ kulajaṃ varasya vā
yamaḥ kamapyācaritātithiṃ yadi |
kathaṃ na gantā viphalībhaviṣṇutāṃ
svayaṃvaraḥ sādhvi ! samṛddhimānapi ? ||81||
apaḥ prati svāmitayā'paraḥ suraḥ
sa tā niṣedhedyadi naiṣadhakrudhā |
nalāya lobhāttatapāṇaye'pi te
pitā kathaṃ tvāṃ vada saṃpradāsyate ||82||
idaṃ mahatte'bhihitaṃ hitaṃ mayā
vihāya mohaṃ damayanti ! cintaya |
sureṣu vighnaikapareṣu ko naraḥ
karasthamapyarthamavāptumīśvaraḥ ? ||83||
imā girastasya vicintya cetasā
tatheti saṃpratyayamāsasāda sā |
nivāritāvagrahanīranirjhare
nabhonabhasyatvamalambhayadṛśau ||84||
sphuṭotpalābhyāmalidaṃpatīva tad
vilocanābhyāṃ kucakuṅmalāśayā |
nipatya bindū hṛdi kajjalāvilau
maṇīva nīlau taralau virejatuḥ ||85||
dhutāpatatpuṣpaśilīmukhāśugaiḥ
śucestadāsītsarasī rasasya sā |
rayāya baddhādarayāśrudhārayā
sanālanīlotpalalīlalocanā ||86||
athodbhramantī rudatī gatakṣamā
sasaṃbhramā luptaratiḥ skhalanmatiḥ |
vyadhātpriyaprāptividhātaniścayān
mṛdūni dūnā paridevatāni sā ||87||
tvarasva pañceṣuhutāśanātmanaḥ
tanuṣva madbhasmamayaṃ yaśaścayam |
vidhe ! parehāphalabhakṣaṇavratī
patādya tṛṣyannasubhirmamāphalaiḥ ||88||
bhṛśaṃ viyogānalatapyamāna !
kiṃ vilīyase na tvamayomayaṃ yadi |
smareṣubhirbhedya ! na vajramapyasi
bravīṣi na svānta ! kathaṃ na dīryase ||89||
vilambase jīvita ! kiṃ drava drutaṃ
jvalatyadaste hṛdayaṃ niketanam |
jahāsi nādyāpi mṛṣā sukhāsikām
apūrvamālasyamidaṃ tavedṛśam ||90||
dṛśau ! mṛṣā pātakino manorathāḥ
kathaṃ pṛthū vāmapi vipralebhire |
priyaśriyaḥ prekṣaṇaghāti pātakaṃ
svamaśrubhiḥ kṣālayataṃ śataṃ samāḥ ||91||
priyaṃ na mṛtyuṃ na labhe tvadīpsitaṃ
tadeva na syānmama yattvamicchasi |
viyogameveccha manaḥ ! priyeṇa me
tava prasādānna bhavatvasau mama ||92||
na kākuvākyairativāmamaṅgajaṃ
dviṣatsu yāce pavanaṃ tu dakṣiṇam |
diśāpi madbhasma kiratvayaṃ tayā
priyo yayā vairavidhirvadhāvadhiḥ ||93||
amūni gacchanti yugāni na kṣaṇaḥ
kiyatsahiṣye na hi mṛtyurasti me |
sa māṃ na kāntaḥ sphuṭamantarujjhitā
na taṃ manastacca na kāyavāyavaḥ ||94||
madugratāpavyayaśaktaśīkaraḥ
surāḥ ! sa vaḥ kena pape kṛpārṇavaḥ |
udeti koṭirna mude maduttamā
kimāśu saṃkalpakaṇaśrameṇa vaḥ ||95||
mamaiva vāhardinamaśrudurdinaiḥ
prasahya varṣāsu ṛtau prasañjite |
kathaṃ nu śṛṇvantu suṣupya devatā
bhavatvaraṇyeruditaṃ na me giraḥ ||96||
iyaṃ na te naiṣadha ! dṛkpathātithiḥ
tvadekatānasya janasya yātanā |
hrade hrade hā na kiyadgaveṣitaḥ
sa vedhasā'gopi khago'pi vakti yaḥ ||97||
mamāpi kiṃ no dayase dayāghana !
tvadaṅghrimagnaṃ yadi vettha me manaḥ |
nimajjayansaṃtamase parāśayaṃ
vidhistu vācyaḥ kva tavāgasaḥ kathā ? ||98||
kathāvaśeṣaṃ tava sā kṛte gatetyupaiṣyati śrotrapathaṃ kathaṃ na te ? |
dayāṇunā māṃ samanugrahīpyase
tadāpi tāvadyadi nātha ! nādhunā ||99||
mamādarīdaṃ vidarītumāntaraṃ
tadarthikalpadruma ! kiṃcidarthaye |
bhidāṃ hṛdi dvāramavāpya mā sa me
hatāsubhiḥ prāṇasamaḥ samaṃ gamaḥ ||100||
iti priyākākubhirunmiṣanbhṛśaṃ
digīśadūtyena hṛdi sthirīkṛtaḥ |
nṛpaṃ sa yoge'pi viyogamanmathaḥ
kṣaṇaṃ tamudbhrāntamajījanatpunaḥ ||101||
mahendradūtyādi samastamātmanaḥ
tataḥ sa vismṛtya manorathasthitaiḥ |
kriyāḥ priyāyā lalitaiḥ karambitā
vitarkayannitthamalīkamālapat ||102||
ayi priye ! kasya kṛte vilapyate
vilipyate hā mukhamaśrubindubhiḥ |
purastvayāloki namannayaṃ na kiṃ
tiraścalallocanalīlayā nalaḥ ||103||
cakāsti binducyutakāticāturī
ghanāsrubindusrutikaitavāttava |
masāratārākṣi ! sasāramātmanā
tanoṣi saṃsāramasaṃśayaṃ yataḥ ||104||
apāstapāthoruhi śāyitaṃ kare
karoṣi līlākamalaṃ kimānanam |
tanoṣi hāraṃ kiyadasruṇaḥ sravaiḥ
adoṣanirvāsitabhūṣaṇe hṛdi ||105||
dṛśoramaṅgalyamidaṃ milajjalaṃ
kareṇa tāvatparimārjayāmi te |
athāparādhaṃ bhavadaṅghripaṅkajad
vayīrajobhiḥ samamātmamaulinā ||106||
mama tvadacchāṅghrinakhāmṛtadyuteḥ
kirīmāṇikyamayūkhamañjarī |
upāsanāmasya karotu rohiṇī
tyaja tyajākāraṇaroṣaṇe ! ruṣam ||107||
tanoṣi mānaṃ mayi cenmanāgapi
tvayi śraye tadbahumānamānataḥ |
vinamya vakraṃ yadi vartase kiyan
namāmi te caṇḍi ! tadā padāvadhi ||108||
prabhutvabhūmnānugṛhāṇa vā na vā
praṇāmamātrādhigame'pi kaḥ śramaḥ ? ||109||
smareṣumāthaṃ sahase mṛduḥ kathaṃ
hṛdi draḍhīyaḥ kucasaṃvṛte tava |
nipatya vaisāriṇaketanasya vā
vrajanti bāṇā vimukhotpatiṣṇutām ||110||
smitasya saṃbhāvaya sṛkvaṇā kaṇān
vidhehi līlācalamañcalaṃ bhruvaḥ |
apāṅgarathyāpathikīṃ ca helayā
prasadya saṃdhehi dṛśaṃ mamopari ||111||
samāpaya prāvṛṣamasruvipnuṣāṃ
smitena viśrāṇaya kaumudīmudaḥ |
dṛśāvitaḥ khelatu khañjanadvayī
vikāsi paṅkeruhamastu te mukham ||112||
sudhārasodvelanakelimakṣarasrajā sṛjāntarmama karṇakūpayoḥ |
dṛśau madīye madirākṣi ! kāraya
smitaśriyā pāyasapāraṇāvidhim ||113||
mamāsanārdhe bhava maṇḍanaṃ na na
priye ! madutsaṅgavibhūṣaṇaṃ bhava |
ahaṃ bhramādālapamaṅga ! mṛṣyatāṃ
vinā mamoraḥ katamattavāsanam ||114||
adhītapañcāśugabāṇavañcane !
sthitā madantarbahireṣi cedureḥ |
smarāśugebhyo hṛdayaṃ bibhetu na
praviśya tattvanmayasaṃpuṭe mama ||115||
pariṣvajasvānavakāśabāṇatā
smarasya lagne hṛdayedvaye'stu nau |
dṛḍhā mama tvatkucayoḥ kaṭhorayoḥ
urastaṭīyaṃ paricārikocitā ||116||
tavādharāya spṛhayāmi yanmadhusravaiḥ śravaḥ sākṣikamākṣikā giraḥ |
adhityakāsu stanayostanotu te
mamendulekhābhyudayādbhrutaṃ nakhaḥ ||117||
na vartase manmathanāṭikā kathaṃ
prakāśaromāvalisūtradhāriṇī |
tavāṅgahāre rucimeti nāyakaḥ
śikhāmaṇiśca dvijarāḍvidūṣakaḥ ||118||
śubhāṣṭavargastvadanaṅgajanmanaḥ
tavādhare'likhyata yatra lekhayā |
madīyadantakṣatarājirañjanaiḥ
sa bhūrjatāmarjatu bimbapāṭalaḥ ||119||
girānukampasva dayasva cumbanaiḥ
prasīda śuśrūṣayituṃ mayā kucau |
niśeva cāndrasya karotkarasya
yanmama tvamekāsi nalasya jīvitam ||120||
muniryathātmānamatha prabodhavān
prakāśayantaṃ svamasāvabudhyata |
api prapannāṃ prakṛtiṃ vilokya tām
avāptasaṃskāratayāsṛjadgiraḥ ||121||
aye ! mayātmā kimanihnutīkṛtaḥ
kimatra mantā sa tu māṃ śatakratuḥ |
puraḥ svabhaktyātha namanhriyāvilo
vilokitāhe na tadiṅgitānyapi ||122||
svanāma yannāma mudhābhyadhāmaho
mahendrakārye mahadetadujjhitam |
hanūmadādyairyaśasā mayā punardviṣāṃ
hasairdūtyapathaḥ sitīkṛtaḥ ||123||
dhiyātmanastāvadacāru nācaraṃ
parastu tadveda sa yadvadiṣyati |
janāvanāyodyaminaṃ janārdanaṃ
kṣaye jagajjīvapibaṃ śivaṃ vadan ||124||
sphuṭatpadaḥ kiṃ hṛdayaṃ trapābharād
yadasya śuddhirvibudhairvibudhyatām |
vidantu te tattvamidaṃ tu danturaṃ
janānane kaḥ karamarpayiṣyati ||125||
mama śramaścetanayānayā phalī
balīyasālopi ca saiva vedhasā |
na vastu daivasvarasādvināśvaraṃ
sureśvaro'pi pratikartumīśvaraḥ ||126||
iti svayaṃ mohamahorminirmitaṃ
prakāśanaṃ śocati naiṣadhe nijam |
tathāvyathāmagnataduddidhīrṣayā
dayālurāgāllaghu hemahaṃsarāṭ ||127||
nalaṃ sa tatpakṣaravordhvavīkṣiṇaṃ
sa eṣa pakṣīti bhaṇantamabhyadhāt |
nayādayaināmati mā nirāśatām
asūnvihāteyamataḥ paraṃ param ||128||
sureṣu paśyannijasāparādhatām
iyatprayasyāpi tadarthasiddhaye |
na kūṭasākṣībhavanocito bhavān
satāṃ hi cetaḥ śucitātmasākṣikā ||129||
itīriṇāpṛcchaya nalaṃ vidarbhajām
api prayātena khagena sāntvitaḥ |
mṛdurbabhāṣe bhaginīṃ damasya sa
praṇamya cittena haritpatīnnṛpaḥ ||130||
dade'pi tubhyaṃ kiyatīḥ kadarthanāḥ
sureṣu rāgaprasavāvakeśinīḥ |
adambhadūtyena bhajantu vā dayāṃ
diśantu vā daṇḍamamī mamāgasā ||131||
ayogajāmanvabhavaṃ na vedanāṃ
hitāya me'bhūdiyamunmadiṣṇutā |
udeti doṣādapi doṣalāghavaṃ
kṛśatvamajñānavaśādivainasaḥ ||132||
tavetyayogasmarapāvako'pi me
kadarthanātyarthatayā'gamaddayām |
prakāśamunmādya yadadya kārayan
mayātmanastvāmanukampate sma saḥ ||133||
amī samīhaikaparāstavāmarāḥ
svakiṃkaraṃ māmapi kartumīśiṣe
vicārya kāryaṃ sṛja mā vidhānmudhā
kṛtānutāpastvayi pārṣṇivigraham ||134||
udāsitenaiva mayedamudyase
bhiyā na tebhyaḥ smaratānavānna vā |
hitaṃ yadi syānmadasuvyayena te
tadā tava premaṇi śuddhilabdhaye ||135||
itīritairnaiṣadhasūnṛtāmṛtaiḥ
vidarbhajanmā bhṛśamullalāsa sā |
ṛtoradhiśrīḥ śiśiranujanmanaḥ
pikasvarairdūravikasvarairyathā ||136||
nalaṃ tadāvetya tamāśaye nije
ghṛṇāṃ vigānaṃ ca mumoca bhīmajā |
jugupsamānā hi mano dhṛtaṃ tadā
satīdhiyā daivatadūtadhāvi sā ||137||
manobhuvaste bhavināṃ manaḥ pitā
nimajjayannenasi tanna lajjase |
amudri satputrakathā tvayeti sā
sthitā satī manmathanindinī dhiyā ||138||
prasūnamityeva tadaṅgavarṇanā
na sā viśeṣātkatamattadityabhūt |
tadā kadambaṃ tadavarṇi lomabhiḥ
mudasruṇā prāvṛṣi harṣamāgataiḥ ||139||
mayaiva saṃbodhya nalaṃ vyalāpi yat
svamāha madbuddhamidaṃ vimṛśya tat |
asāviti bhrāntimasāddamasvasuḥ
svabhāṣitasvodbhramavibhramakramaḥ ||140||
vidarbharājaprabhavā tataḥ paraṃ
trapāsakhī vaktumalaṃ na vā nalam |
purastamūce'bhimukhaṃ yadatrapā
mamajja tenaiva mahāhrade hriyaḥ ||141||
yadāpavāryāpi na dātumuttaraṃ
śaśāka sakhyāḥ śravasi priyasva sā |
vihasya sakhyeva tamavravīttadā
hriyā'dhunā maunadhanā bhavatpriyā ||142||
padātitheyām̐llikhitasya te svayaṃ
vitanvatī locananirjharāniyam |
jagāda yāṃ saiva mukhānmama tvayā
prasūnavāṇopaniṣanniśamyatām ||143||
asaṃśayaṃ sa tvayi haṃsa eva māṃ
śaśaṃsa na tvadvirahāptasaṃśayām |
kva candravaṃśasya vataṃsa ! madvadhān
nṛśaṃsatā saṃbhavinī bhavādṛśe ||144||
jitastavayāsyena vidhuḥ smaraḥ śriyā
kṛtapratijñau mama tau vadhe kutaḥ |
taveti kṛtvā yadi tajjitaṃ mayā
na moghasaṅkalpadharāḥ kilāmarāḥ ||145||
nijāṃśunirdagdhamadaṅgabhasmabhiḥ
mudhā vidhurvāñchati lāñchanonmṛjām |
tvadāsyatāṃ yāsyati tāvatāpi kiṃ
vadhūvadhenaiva punaḥ kalaṅkitaḥ ||146||
prasīda yaccha svaśarānmanobhuve
sa hantu māṃ tairdhutakausumāśugaḥ |
tvadekacittāhamasūnvimuñcatī
tvameva bhūtvā tṛṇavajjayāmi tam ||147||
śrutiḥ surāṇāṃ guṇagayanī yadi
tvadaṅghrimagnasya janasya kiṃ tataḥ |
stave raverapsu kṛtāplavaiḥ kṛte
na mudvatī jātu bhavetkumudvatī ||148||
kathāsu śiṣyai varamadya na dhriye
mamāvagantāsi na bhāvamanyathā |
tvadarthamuktāsutayā sunātha ! māṃ
pratīhi jāvābhyadhika ! tvadekikām ||149||
mahendraheterapi rakṣaṇaṃ bhayād
yadarthisādhāraṇamastrabhṛddratam |
prasūnabāṇādapi māmarakṣataḥ
kṣataṃ taduccairavakīrṇinastava ||150||
tavāsmi māṃ dhātukamapyupekṣase
mṛṣāmaraṃ hā'maragauravātsmaram |
avehi caṇḍālamanaṅgamaṅga ! taṃ
svakāṇḍakārasya maghoḥ sakhā hi saḥ ||151||
laghau laghāveva puraḥ pare budhaiḥ
vidheyamuttejanamātmatejasaḥ |
tṛṇe tṛṇeḍhi jvalanaḥ khalu jvalan
kramātkarīṣadrumakāṇḍamaṇḍalam ||152||
surāparādhastava vā kiyānayaṃ
svayaṃvarāyāmanukampratā mayi |
girāpi vakṣyanti makheṣu tarpaṇād
idaṃ na devā mukhalajjayaiva te ||153||
vrajantu te te'pi varaṃ svayaṃvaraṃ
prasādya tāneva mayā variṣyase |
na sarvathā tānapi na spṛśeddayā
na te'pi tāvanmadanastvameva vā ||154||
itīyamālekhyagate'pi vīkṣite
tvayi smaravrīḍasamasyayānayā |
pade pade maunamayāntarīpiṇī
pravartitā sāraghasārasāraṇī ||155||
caṇḍālaste viṣāmaviśikhaḥ spṛśyate dṛśyate na
khyāto'naṅgastvayi jayati yaḥ kiṃnu kṛttāṅgulīkaḥ |
kṛtvā mittraṃ madhumadhivanasthānamantaścaritvā
sakhyāḥ prāṇānharati haritastvadyaśastajjuṣantām ||156||
atha bhīmabhuvaiva raho'bhihitāṃ
natamaulirapatrapayā sa nijām |
amaraiḥ saha rājasamājagatiṃ
jagatīpatirabhyupagatya yayau ||157||
śvastasyāḥ priyamāptusuddhuradhiyo dhārāḥ sṛjantyā rayānnamronnamrakapolapālipulakairvetasvatīrasruṇaḥ |
catvāraḥ praharāḥ smarārtibhirabhūtsāpi kṣapā duḥkṣayā
tattasyāṃ kṛpayākhilaiva vidhinā rātristriyāmā kṛtā ||158||
tadakhilamiha bhūtaṃ bhūtagatyā jagatyāḥ
patirabhilapati sma svātmadūtatvatattvam |
tribhuvanajanayāvaddṛttavṛttāntasākṣātkṛtikṛtiṣu nirastānandamindrādiṣu drāk ||159||
śrīharṣaṃ kavirājarājimukuṭālaṃkārahīraḥ sutaṃ
śrīhīraḥ suṣuve jitendriyacayaṃ māmalladevī ca yam |
saṃdṛbdhārṇavavarṇanasya navamastasya vyaraṃsīnmahākāvye cāruṇi naiṣadhīyacarite sargo nisargojjvalaḥ ||160||

daśamaḥ sargaḥ
rathairathāyuḥ kulajāḥ kumārāḥ
śastreṣu śāstreṣu ca dṛṣṭapārāḥ |
svayaṃvaraṃ śaṃbaravairikāyavyūhaśriyaḥ śrījitayakṣarājāḥ ||1||
nābhūdabhūmiḥ smarasāyakānāṃ
nāsīdagantā kulajaḥ kumāraḥ |
nāsthādapanthā dharaṇeḥ kaṇo'pi
vrajeṣu rājñāṃ yugapadvrajatsu ||2||
yogyairvrajadbhirnṛpajāṃ varītuṃ
vīrairanarhaiḥ prasabhena hartum |
draṣṭuṃ paraistānparikartumanyaiḥ
svamātraśeṣāḥ kakubho babhūvuḥ ||3||
lokairaśeṣairaviniśriyaṃ tām
uddiśya diśyairvihite prayāṇe |
svavartitattajjanayantraṇārtiviśrāntimāyuḥ kakubhāṃ vibhāgāḥ ||4||
talaṃ yatheyurna tilā vikīrṇāḥ
sainyaistathā rājapathā babhūvuḥ |
bhaimīṃ sa labdhāmiva tatra mene
kaḥ prāpa bhūbhṛdbhavituṃ purastāt ||5||
nṛpaḥ purasthaiḥ pratibaddhavartmā
paścāttanaiḥ kaścana nudyamānaḥ |
yantrasthasiddhārthapadābhiṣekaṃ
labdhvāpyasiddhārthamamanyata svam ||6||
rājñāṃ pathi styānatayānupūrvyā
vilaṅghanāśaktivilambabhājām |
āhvānasaṃjñānamivāgrakampairdadurvidarbhendrapurīpatākāḥ ||7||
prāgbhūya karkoṭaka ācakarṣa
sakambalaṃ nāgabalaṃ yaduccaiḥ |
bhuvastale kuṇḍinagāmi rājñāṃ
tadvāsukeścāśvataro'nvagacchat ||8||
āgacchadurvindracamūsamutthairbhūreṇubhiḥ pāṇḍuritā mukhaśrīḥ |
vispaṣṭamācaṣṭa haridvadhūnāṃ
rūpaṃ patityāgadaśānurūpam ||9||
ākhaṇḍalo daṇḍadharaḥ kṛśānuḥ
pāśīti nāthaiḥ kakubhāṃ caturbhiḥ |
bhaimyeva baddhvā svaguṇena kṛṣṭaiḥ
svayaṃvare tatra gataṃ na śeṣaiḥ ||10||
mantraiḥ puraṃ bhīmapurohitasya
tadbaddharakṣaṃ viśati kva rakṣaḥ |
tatrodyamaṃ dikpatirātatāna
yātuṃ tato jātu na yātudhānaḥ ||11||
kartuṃ śaśākābhimukhaṃ na bhaimyā
mṛgaṃ dṛgambhoruhatarjitam yat |
asyā vivāhāya yayau vidarbhāṃstadvāhanastena na gandhavāhaḥ ||12||
jātau na vitte na guṇe na kāmaḥ
saundarya eva pravaṇaḥ sa vāmaḥ |
svacchasvaśailekṣitakutsaberaḥ
tāṃ pratyagānna stritamāṃ kuberaḥ ||13||
bhamīvivāhaṃ sahate sma kasmād
ardhaṃ tanuryā girijātmabhartuḥ |
tenāvrajantyā vidadhe vidarbhānīśānayānāya tayāntarāyaḥ ||14||
svayaṃvaraṃ bhīmanarendrajāyā
diśaḥ patina praviveśa śeṣaḥ |
prayātu bhāraṃ sa niveśya kasminnahirmahīgauravasāsahiryaḥ ||15||
yayau vimṛśyordhvadiśaḥ patirna
svayaṃvaraṃ vīkṣitadharmaśāstraḥ |
vyaloki loke śrutiṣu smṛtau vā
samaṃ vivāhaḥ kva pitāmahena ||16||
bhaimīnirastaṃ svamavetya dūtīmukhātkilendrapramukhā digīśāḥ |
syade mukhendau ca vitatya māndyaṃ
cittasya te rājasamājamīyuḥ ||17||
nalabhrameṇāpi bhajeta bhaimī
kadācidasmāniti śeṣitāśā |
bhūnmahendrādicatuṣṭayī sā
caturnalī kācidalīkarūpā ||18||
prayasyatāṃ tadbhavituṃ surāṇāṃ
dṛṣṭena pṛṣṭena paraspareṇa |
naivānumene nalasāmyasiddhiḥ
svābhāvikātkṛtrimamanyadeva ||19||
pūrṇendumāsyaṃ vidadhuḥ punaste
punarmukhīcakruranidramabjam |
svavaktramādarśatale'tha darśaṃ
darśaṃ babhañjurna tathātimañju ||20||
teṣāṃ tadā labdhumanīśvarāṇāṃ
śriyaṃ nijāsyena nalānanasya |
nālaṃ tarītuṃ punaruktidoṣaṃ
barhirmukhānāmanalānanatvam ||21||
priyāviyogakvathitādivailāccandrācca rāhugrahapīḍitātte |
dhmātādbhavena smarato'pi sāraiḥ
svaṃ kalpayanti sma nalānukalpam ||22||
nalasya paśyatviyadantaraṃ
tairbhaimīti bhūpānvidhirāhṛtāsyai |
spardhāṃ digīśānapi kārayitvā
tasyaiva tebhyaḥ prathimānamākhyat ||23||
sabhā nalaśrīyamakairyamādyaiḥ
nalaṃ vinābhūddhṛtadivyaratnaiḥ |
bhāmāṅgaṇaprādhuṇike caturbhirdevadrumairdyauriva pārijāte ||24||
tatrāgamadvāsukirīśabhūṣā
bhasmopadehasphuṭagauradehaḥ |
phaṇīndravṛndapraṇigadyamānaprasīdajīvādyanujīvivādaḥ ||25||
dvīpāntarebhyaḥ puṭabhedanaṃ tat
kṣaṇādavāpe surabhūmibhūpaiḥ |
tatkālamālambi na kena yūnā
smareṣupakṣānilatūlalīlā ||26||
ramyeṣu harmyeṣu niveśanena
saparyayā kuṇḍinanākanāthaḥ |
priyoktidānādaranamratādyairupācaraccāru sa rājacakram ||27||
catuḥsamudrīparikhe nṛpāṇām
antaḥ pure vāsitakīrtidāre |
dānaṃ dayā sūnṛtamātitheyī
catuṣṭayī rakṣaṇasauvidallā ||28||
abhyāgataiḥ kuṇḍinavāsavasya
parokṣavṛtteṣvapi teṣu teṣu |
jijñāsitasvepsitalābhaliṅgaṃ
svalpo'ti nāvāpi nṛpairviśeṣaḥ ||29||
aṅke vidarbhendrapurasya śaṅke
na saṃmamau naiṣa tathā samājaḥ |
yathā payorāśiragastyahaste
yathā jagadvā jaṭhare murāreḥ ||30||
pure pathi dvāragṛhāṇi tatra
citrīkṛtānyutsavavāñchayeva |
nabho'pi kirmīramakāri teṣāṃ
mahībhujāmābharaṇaprabhābhiḥ ||31||
vilāsavaidagdhyavibhūṣaṇaśrīsteṣāṃ yathāsītparicārake'pi |
ajñāsiṣuḥ strīśiśubāliśāstaṃ
yathāgataṃ nāyakameva kaṃcit ||32||
na svedinaścāmaramārutairna
nimeṣanetrāḥ prativastucitraiḥ |
mlānasrajo nātapavāraṇena
