adabhramabhrājata sāśmaśāṇanāt
prakāśarociḥpratimeva hemajā ||24||
tadā tadaṅgasya bibharti vibhramaṃ
vilepanāmodamucaḥ sphuradrucaḥ |
darasphuratkāñcanaketakīdalāt
suvarṇamabhyasyati saurabhaṃ yadi ||25||
avāpitāyāḥ śucivedikāntaraṃ
kalāsu tasyāḥ sakalāsu paṇḍitāḥ |
kṣaṇena sakhyaściraśikṣaṇaiḥ sphuṭaṃ
pratipratīkaṃ pratikarma nirmamuḥ ||26||
vināpi bhūṣāmavadhiḥ śriyāmiyaṃ
vyabhūṣi vijñābhiradarśi cādhikā |
na bhūṣayaiṣādhicakāsti kiṃ tu sā
nayeti kasyāstu vicāracāturī vidhāya ||27||
vidhāya bandhūkapayojapūjane
kṛtāṃ vidhorgandhaphalībaliśriyam |
nininda labdhādharalocanārcanaṃ
manaḥśilācitrakametya tanmukham ||28||
mahīmadhonāṃ madanāndhatātamītamaḥpaṭārambhaṇatantusaṃtatiḥ |
abandhi tanmūrdhajapāśamañjarī
kayāpi dhūpagrahadhūmakomalā ||29||
punaḥpunaḥ kācana kurvatī kacacchaṭādhiyā dhūpajadhūmasaṃyamam |
sakhī smitaistarkitatannijabhramā
babandha tanmūrdhajacāmaraṃ cirāt ||30||
balasya kṛṣṭeva halena bhāti yā
kalindakanyā ghanabhaṅgabhaṅgurā |
tadārpitaistāṃ karuṇasya kuḍnalaiḥ
jahāsa tasyāḥ kuṭilā kacacchaṭā ||31||
dhṛtaitayā hāṭakapaṭṭikālike
babhūva keśāmbudavidyudeva sā |
mukhendusaṃbandhavaśātsudhājuṣaḥ
sthiratvamūhe niyataṃ tadāyuṣaḥ ||32||
lalāṭikāsīmani cūrṇakuntalā
babhuḥ sphuṭaṃ bhīmanarendrajanmanaḥ |
manaḥśilācitrakadīpasaṃbhavā
bhrāmībhṛtaḥ kajjaladhūmavallayaḥ ||33||
apāṅgamāliṅgya tadīyamuccakairadīpi rekhā janitāñjanena yā |
apāti sūtraṃ tadiva dvitīyayā
vayaḥśriyā vardhayituṃ vilocane ||34||
anaṅgalīlābhirapāṅgadhāvinaḥ
kanīnikānīlamaṇeḥ punaḥ punaḥ |
tamisravaṃśaprabhavena raśminā
svapaddhatiḥ sā kimarañji nāñjanaiḥ ||35||
aseviṣātāṃ suṣamāṃ vidarbhajādṛśāvavāpyāñjanarekhayā'nvayam |
bhujadvayajyākiṇapaddhatispṛśoḥ
smareṇa bāṇīkṛtayoḥ payojayoḥ ||36||
tadakṣitatkālatulāgasā nakhaṃ
nikhāya kṛṣṇasya mṛgasya cakṣuṣī |
vidhiryaduddhartumiyeṣa tattayoradūravartikṣatatā sma śaṃsati ||37||
vilocanābhyāmatimātrapīḍite
vataṃsanīlāmburuhadvayīṃ khalu |
tayoḥ pratidvandvidhiyādhiropayāṃbabhūvaturbhīmasutāśrutī tataḥ ||38||
dhṛtaṃ vataṃsotpalayugmametayā
vyarājadasyāṃ patite dṛśāviva |
manobhuvāndhyaṃ gamitasya paśyataḥ
sthite lagitvā rasikasya kasyacit ||39||
vidarbhaputrīśravaṇāvataṃsikāmaṇīmahaḥkiṃśukakārmukodare |
udītanetrotpalabāṇasaṃbhṛtirnalaṃ paraṃ lakṣyamavaikṣata smaraḥ ||40||
anācarattathyamṛṣāvicāraṇāṃ
tadānanaṃ karṇalatāyugena kim |
babandha jitvā maṇikuṇḍale vidhū
dvicandrabuddhyā kathitāvasūyakau ||41||
avādi bhaimī paridhāpya kuṇḍale
vayasyayābhyāmabhitaḥ samanvayaḥ |
tvadānanendoḥ priyakāmajanmani
śrayatyayaṃ daurudharīṃ dhuraṃ dhruvam ||42||
niveśitaṃ yāvakarāgadīptaye
lagattadīyādharasīmni sikthakam |
rarāja tatraiva nivastumutsukaṃ
madhūni nirdhūya sudhāsadharmaṇi ||43||
svareṇa vīṇetyaviśeṣaṇaṃ purā
sphurattadīyā khalu kaṇṭhakandalī |
avāpya tantrīratha sapta muktikāsarānarājatparivādinī sphuṭam ||44||
upāsyamānāviva śikṣituṃ
tato mṛdutvamaprauḍhamṛṇālanālayā |
virejaturmāṅgalikena saṃgatau
bhujau sudatyā valayena kambunaḥ ||45||
padadvaye'syā navayāvarañjanā
janaistadānīmudanīyatārpitā |
cirāya padmau parirabhya jāgratī
niśīva viśliṣya navā ravidyutiḥ ||46||
kṛtāparādhaḥ sutanoranantaraṃ
vicintya kāntena samaṃ samāgamam |
sphuṭaṃ siṣeva kusumeṣupāvakaḥ
sa rāgacihnaścaraṇau na yāvakaḥ ||47||
svayaṃ tadaṅgeṣu gateṣu cārutāṃ
paraspareṇaiva vibhūṣiteṣu ca |
kimūcire'laṃkaraṇāni tāni tad
dṛthaiva teṣāṃ karaṇaṃ babhūva yat ||48||
kramādhikāmuttaramuttaraṃ śriyaṃ
pupoṣa yāṃ bhūṣaṇacumbanairiyam |
puraḥ purastasthuṣi rāmaṇīyake
tayā babādhe'vadhibuddhidhoraṇiḥ ||49||
maṇīsanābhau mukurasya maṇḍale
babhau nijāsyapratibimbadarśinī |
vidhoradūraṃ svamukhaṃ vidhāya sā
nirūpayantīva viśeṣametayoḥ ||50||
jitastadāsyena kalānidhirdadhe
dvicandradhīsākṣikamāyakāyatām |
tathāpi jigye yugapatsakhīyugapradarśitādarśabahūbhaviṣṇunā ||51||
kimāliyugmārpitadarpaṇadvaye
tadāsyamekaṃ bahu cānyadambujam |
himeṣu nirvāpya niśāsamādhibhiḥ
tadīyasālokyamitaṃ vyalokyata ||52||
palāśadāmetimilacchilīmukhairvṛtā vibhūṣāmaṇiraśmikārmukaiḥ |
alakṣi lakṣairdhanuṣāmasau tadā
ratīśasarvasvatayā'bhirakṣitā ||53||
viśeṣatīrthairiva jahnunandinī
guṇairivājānikarāgabhūmitā |
jagāma bhāgyairiva nītirujjvalairvibhūṣaṇaistatsuṣamā mahārdhatām ||54||
nalātsvavaiśvastyamanāptumānatā
nṛpastriyo bhīmamahotsavāgatāḥ |
tadaṅghrilākṣāmadadhanta maṅgalaṃ
śiraḥsu sindūramiva priyāyuṣe ||55||
amoghabhāvena sanābhitāṃ gatāḥ
prasannagīrvāṇavarākṣarasrajām |
tataḥ praṇamrādhijagāma sā hriyā
gururgurubrahmapativratāśiṣāḥ ||56||
tathaiva tatkālamathānujīvibhiḥ
prasādhanāsañjanaśilpapāragaiḥ |
nijasya pāṇigrahaṇakṣaṇocitā
kṛtā nalasyāpi vibhorvibhūṣaṇā ||57||
nṛpasya tatrādhikṛtāḥ punaḥ punarvicārya tānbandhamavāpipankacān |
kalāpalīlopanidhirgaruttyajaḥ
sa yairapālāpi kalāpisaṃpadaḥ ||58||
patattriṇāṃ drādhimaśālinā dhanurguṇena saṃyogajuṣāṃ manobhuvaḥ |
kacena tasyārjitamārjanaśriyā
sametya saubhāgyamalambhi kuṅnalaiḥ ||59||
anardhyaratnaughamayena maṇḍito
rarāja rājā mukuṭena mūrdhani |
vanīpakānāṃ sa hi kalpabhūruhastato vimuñcanniva mañjumañjarīm ||60||
nalasya bhāle maṇivīrapaṭṭikānibhena lagnaḥ paridhirvidhorbabhau |
tadā śaśāṅkādhikarūpatāṃ gate
tadānane mātumaśaknuvanniva ||61||
babhūva bhaimyāḥ khalu mānasaukasaṃ
jighāṃsato dhairyabharaṃ manobhuvaḥ |
upabhru tadvartulacitrarūpiṇī
dhanuḥsamīpe gulikeva saṃbhṛtā ||62||
acumbi yā candanabindumaṇḍalī
nalīyavaktreṇa sarojatarjinā |
śriyaṃ śritā kācana tārakāsakhī
kṛtā śaśāṅkasya tayāṅkavartinī ||63||
na yāvadagnibhramametyudūḍhatāṃ
nalasya bhaimīti harerdurāśayā |
sa bindurinduḥ prahitaḥ kimasya sā
na veti bhāle paṭhituṃ lipīmiva ||64||
kapolapālījanijānubimbayoḥ
samāgamātkuṇḍalamaṇḍaladvayī |
nalasya tatkālamavāpa cittabhūḥ
atha sphuraccakracatuṣkacārutām ||65||
śritāsya kaṇṭhaṃ guruvipravandanādvinamramoleścibukāgracumbinī |
avāpa muktāvalirāsyacandramaḥ
sravatsudhātundilabinduvṛndatām ||66||
yato'jani śrīrbalavānbalaṃ dviṣan
babhūva yasyājiṣu vāraṇena saḥ |
apūpurattānkamalārthino ghanān
samudrabhāvaṃ sa babhāra tadbhujaḥ ||67||
kṛtārthayannarthijanānanārataṃ
babhūva tasyāmarabhūruhaḥ karaḥ |
tadīyamūle nihitaṃ dvitīyavad
ddhruvaṃ dadhe kaṅkaṇamālavālatām ||68||
rarāja dormaṇḍanamaṇḍalījuṣoḥ
sa vajramāṇikyasitāruṇatviṣoḥ |
miṣeṇa varṣandaśadiṅmukhonmukhau
yaśaḥpratāpāvavanījayārjitau ||69||
ghane samastāpaghanāvalambināṃ
vibhūṣaṇānāṃ maṇimaṇḍale nalaḥ |
svarūparekhāmavalokya niṣphalīcakāra sevācaṇadarpaṇārpaṇām ||70||
vyaloki lokena na kevalaṃ calan
mudā tadīyābharaṇāṛpaṇādyutiḥ |
adarśi visphāritaratnalocanaiḥ
paraspareṇeva vibhūṣaṇairapi ||71||
tato'nu vārṣṇeyaniyantṛkaṃ rathaṃ
yudhi kṣitārikṣitibhṛjjayadrathaḥ |
nṛpaḥ pṛthāsūnurivādhirūḍhavānsa
janyayātrāmuditaḥ kirīṭavān ||72||
vidarbhanāmnastridivasya vīkṣituṃ
rasodayādapsarasastamujjvalam |
gṛhādgṛhādetya dhṛtaprasādhanā
vyarājayanrājapathānathādhikam ||73||
ajānatī kāpi vilokanotsukā
samīradhūtārdhamapi stanāṃśukam |
kucena tasmai calate'karotpuraḥ
purāṅganā maṅgalakumbhasaṃbhṛtim ||74||
sakhīṃ nalaṃ darśayamānayāṅkato
javādudastasya karasya kaṅkaṇe |
viṣajya hāraistruṭitairatarkitaiḥ
kṛtaṃ kayāpi kṣaṇalājamokṣaṇam ||75||
lasannakhādarśamukhāmbujasmitaprasūnavāṇīmadhupāṇipallavam |
yiyāsatastasya nṛpasya jajñire
praśastavastūni tadevayauvatam ||76||
karasthatāmbūlajighatsurekikā
vilokanaikāgravilocanotpalā |
mukhe nicikṣepa mukhadvirājatāruṣeva līlākamalaṃ vilāsinī ||77||
kayāpi vīkṣāvimanaskalocane
samāja evopapateḥ samīyuṣaḥ |
ghanaṃ savighnaṃ parirambhasāhasaiḥ
tadā tadālokanamanvabhūyata ||78||
didṛkṣuranyā vinimeṣāvīkṣaṇāṃ
nṛṇāmayogyāṃ dadhatī tanuśriyam |
padāgramātreṇa yadaspṛśanmahīṃ
na tāvatā kevalamapsaro'bhavat ||79||
vibhūṣaṇasraṃsanaśaṃsanārpitaiḥ
karaprahārairapi dhūnanairapi |
amāntamantaḥ prasabhaṃ purā'parā
sakhīṣu saṃmāpayatīva saṃmadam ||80||
vataṃsanīlāmburuheṇa kiṃ dṛśā
vilokamāne vimanībabhūvatuḥ |
api śrutī darśanasaktacetasāṃ
na tena te śuśruvaturmṛgīdṛśām ||81||
kāścinnirmāya cakṣuḥprasṛticulukitaṃ tāsvaśaṅkanta kāntā
maugdhyādācūḍamoghairniculitamiva taṃ bhūṣaṇānāṃ maṇīnām |
sāhasrībhirnimeṣākṛtamatibhirayaṃ dṛgbhirāliṅgitaḥ kiṃ
jyotiṣṭomādiyajñaśrutiphalajagatīsārvabhaumabhrameṇa ||82||
bhavansudyumnaḥ strī narapatirabhūdyasya jananī
tamurvaśyāḥ prāṇānapi vijayamānastanurucā |
harārabdhakrodhendhanamadanasiṃhāsanamasāvalaṃkarmīṇaśrīrudabhavadalaṃkartumadhunā ||83||
arthī sarvasuparvaṇāṃ patirasāvetasya yūnaḥ kṛte
paryatyāji vidarbharājasutayā yuktaṃ viśeṣajñayā |
asminnāma tayā vṛte sumanasaḥ santo'pi yannirjarā
jātā durmanaso na soḍhumucitā teṣāṃ tu sā'naucitī ||84||
asyotkaṇṭhitakaṇṭhaloṭhivaraṇasraksākṣibhirdigbhaṭaiḥ
svaṃ vakṣaḥ svayamasphuṭanna kimadaḥ śastrādapi sphoṭitam |
vyāvṛtyopanatena hā śatamakhenādya prasādyā kathaṃ
bhaimyāṃ vyarthamanorathena ca śacī sācīkṛtāsyāmbujā ||85||
mā jānīta vidarbhajāmaviduṣīṃ kīrtiṃ mudaḥ śroyasīṃ
seyaṃ bhadramacīkaranmaghavatā na svaṃ dvitīyāṃ śacīm |
kaḥ śacyā racayāṃcakāra carite kāvyaṃ sa naḥ kathyatāmetasyāstu kariṣyate rasadhunīpātre caritre na kaiḥ ||86||
vaidarbhībahujanmanirmitatapaḥśilpena dehaśriyā
netrābhyāṃ svadate yuvāyamavanīvāsaḥ prasūnāyudhaḥ |
gīrvāṇālayasārvabhaumasukṛtaprāgbhāraduṣprāpayā
yogaṃ bhīmajayānubhūya bhajatāmadvaitamadya tviṣām ||87||
strīpuṃsavyatiṣañjanaṃ janayataḥ patyuḥ prajānāmabhūdabhyāsaḥ paripākimaḥ kimanayordāmpatyasaṃpattaye |
āsaṃsārapurandhripūruṣamithaḥpremārpaṇakrīḍayāpyetajjampatigāḍharāgaracanā prākarṣi cetobhuvaḥ ||88||
tābhirdṛśyata eṣa yānpathi mahājyaiṣṭhīmahe manmahe
yadṛgbhiḥ puruṣottamaḥ paricitaḥ prāgmañcamañcankṛtaḥ |
sā strīrāṭ patayālubhiḥ śitisitaiḥ syādasya dṛkcāmaraiḥ
sasne māghamaghātighātiyamunāgaṅgaughayoge yayā ||89||
vaidarbhīvipulānurāgakalanātsaubhāgyamatrākhilakṣoṇīcakraśatakratau nijagade tadbṛttavṛttakramaiḥ |
kiṃcāsmākanarendrabhūsubhagatāsaṃbhūtaye lagnakaṃ
devendrāvaraṇaprasāditaśacīviśrāṇitāśīrvacaḥ ||90||
āsutrāmamapāsanānmakhabhujāṃ bhaimyaiva rājavraje
tādarthyāgamanānurodhaparayā yuktārji lajjāmṛjā |
ātmānaṃ tridaśaprasādaphalatām patye vidhāyānayā
hrīroṣāpayaśaḥkathānavasaraḥ sṛṣṭaṃ surāṇāmapi ||91||
ityālepuranupratīkanilayālaṃkārasāraśriyāhaṃkurvattanurāmaṇīyakamamūrālokya paurapriyāḥ |
sānandāḥ kuruvindasundarakarasyānandanaṃ syandanaṃ
tasyādhyāsya yataḥ śatakratuharitkrīḍādrimindoriva ||92||
śrīharṣaṃ kavirājarājimukuṭālaṃkārahīraḥ sutaṃ
śrīhīraḥ suṣuve jitendriyacayaṃ māmalladevī ca yam |
yātaḥ pañcadaśaḥ kṛśetararasasvādāvihāyaṃ mahākāvye tasya kṛtau nalīyacarite sargo nisargojjvalaḥ ||93||
ṣoḍaśa sargaḥ
vṛtaḥ pratasthe sa rathairatho rathī gṛhānvidarbhādhipaterdharādhipaḥ |
purodhasaṃ gautamamātmavittamaṃ dvidhā puraskṛtya gṛhītamaṅgalaḥ ||1||
vibhūṣaṇāṃśupratibimbitaiḥ sphuṭaṃ bhṛśāvadātaiḥ svanivāsibhirguṇaiḥ |
mṛgekṣaṇānāṃ samupāsi cāmarairvidhūyamānaiḥ sa vidhuprabhaiḥ prabhuḥ ||2||
parārdhyaveṣābharaṇaiḥ puraḥsaraiḥ samaṃ jihāne niṣadhāvanībhuji |
dadhe sunāsīrapadābhidheyatāṃ sa rūḍhimātrādyadi vṛtraśātravaḥ ||3||
nalasya nāsīrasṛjāṃ mahībhujāṃ kirīṭaratnaiḥ punaruktadīpayā |
adīpi rātrau varayātrayā tayā camūrajomiśratamisrasaṃpadā ||4||
vidarbharājaḥ kṣitipānanukṣaṇaṃ śubhakṣaṇāsannataratvasatvaraḥ |
dideśa dūtānpathi yānyathottaraṃ camūmamuṣyopacikāya taccayaḥ ||5||
haridvipadvīpibhirāṃśukairnabhonabhasvadādhmāpanapīnitairabhūt |
tarasvadaśvadhvajinīdhvajairvanaṃ vicitracīnāmbaravallivellivellitam ||6||
bhuvāhvayantīṃ nijatoraṇasrajā gajālikarṇānilakhelayā tataḥ |
dadarśa dūtīmiva bhīmajanmanah sa tatpratīhāramahīṃ mahīpatiḥ ||7||
ślathairdalaiḥ stambhayugasya rambhayoścakāsti caṇḍātakamaṇḍitā sma sā |
priyāsakhīvāsya manaḥsthitisphuratsukhāgatapraśnitatūryaniḥsvanā ||8||
vinetṛbhartṛdvayabhītidāntayoḥ parasparasmādanavāptavaiśasaḥ |
ajāyata dvāri narendrasenayoḥ samāgamaḥ sphāramukhāravodgamaḥ ||9||
nirdiśya bandhūnita ityudīritaṃ damena gatvārdhapathe kṛtārhaṇam |
vinītamā dvārata eva padgatāṃ gataṃ tamaikṣiṣṭa mudā vidarbharāṭ ||10||
athāyamutthāya visārya doryugaṃ mudā pratīyeṣa tamātmajanmanaḥ |
surasravantyā iva pātramāgataṃ bhṛtābhitovīcigatiḥ saritpatiḥ ||11||
yathāvadasmai puruṣottamāya tāṃ sa sādhulakṣmīṃ bahuvāhinīśvaraḥ |
śivāmatha svasya śivāya nandanāṃ dade patiḥ sarvavide mahībhṛtām ||12||
asisvadadyanmadhuparkamarpitaṃ sa tadvyadhāttarkamudarkadarśine |
yadeṣa pāsyanmadhu bhīmajādharaṃ miṣeṇa puṇyāhavidhiṃ tadākṛta ||13||
varasya pāṇiḥ paradhātakautukī vadhūkaraḥ paṅkajakāntitaskaraḥ |
surājñi tau tatra vidarbhamaṇḍale tato nibaddhau kimu karkaśaiḥ kuśaiḥ ||14||
vidarbhajāyāḥ karavārijena yannalasya pāṇerupari sthitaṃ kila |
viśaṅkya sūtraṃ puruṣāyitasya tadbhaviṣyato'smāyi tadā tadālibhiḥ ||15||
sakhā yadasmai kila bhīmasaṃjñayā sa yakṣasakhyādhigataṃ dadau bhavaḥ |
dadau tadeṣa śvaśuraḥ surocitaṃ nalāya cintāmaṇidāma kāmadam ||16||
bahordurāpasya varāya vastunaścitasya dātuṃ pratibimbakaitavāt |
babhautarāmantaravasthitaṃ dadhadyadarthamabhyarthitadeyamarthine ||17||
asiṃ bhavānyāḥ kṣatakāsarāsuraṃ varāya bhīmaḥ sma dadāti bhāsuram |
dade hi tasmai dhavanāmadhāriṇe sa śaṃbhusaṃbhoganimagnayānayā ||18||
adhāri yaḥ prāṅnahiṣāsuradviṣā kṛpāṇamasmai tamadatta kūkudaḥ |
ahāyi tasyā hi dhavārdhamajjinā sa dakṣiṇārdhena parāṅgadāraṇaḥ ||19||
uvāha yaḥ sāndratarāṅgakānanaḥ svaśauryasūryodayaparvatavratam|
sanirjharaḥ śāṇanadhautadhārayā samūḍhasaṃdhyaḥ kṣataśatrujāsṛjā ||20||
yamena jihvā prahiteva yā nijā tamātmajāṃ yācitumarthinā bhṛśam |
sa tāṃ dade'smai parivāraśobhinīṃ karagrahārhāmasiputrikāmapi ||21||
yadaṅgabhūmī babhatuḥ svayoṣitāmurojapattrāvalinetrakajjale |
raṇasthalasthaṇḍilaśāyitāvratairgṛhītadīkṣairiva dakṣiṇīkṛte ||22||
puraiva tasminsamadeśi tatsutābhikena yaḥ sauhṛdanāṭināgninā |
nalāya viśrāṇayati sma taṃ rathaṃ nṛpaḥ sulaṅghyādrisamudrakāpatham ||23||
prasūtavattā nalakūbarānvayaprakāśitāsyāpi mahārathasya yat |
kuberadṛṣṭāntabalena puṣpakaprakṛṣṭataitasya tato'numīyate ||24||
mahendramuccaiḥśravasā pratārya yannijena patyā'kṛta sindhuranvitam |
sa taddade'smai hayaratnamarpitaṃ purā'nubandhuṃ varuṇena bandhutām ||25||
javādavārīkṛtadūradṛkpathastathākṣiyugmāya dade mudaṃ na yaḥ |
dadaddidṛkṣādaradāsatāṃ yathā tayaiva tatpāṃsulakaṇṭhanālatām ||26||
divaspaterādaradarśinādarādaḍhauki yastaṃ prati viśvakarmaṇā |
tamekamāṇikyamayaṃ mahonnataṃ patadgrahaṃ grāhitavānnalena saḥ ||27||
nalena tāmbūlavilāsitojjhitairmukhasya yaḥ pūgakaṇairbhṛto na vā |
iti vyaveci svamayūkhamaṇḍalādudañcaduccāruṇacāruṇaścirāt ||28||
mayena bhīmaṃ bhagavantamarcatā nṛpeti pūjā prabhunāmni yā kṛtā |
adatta bhīmo'pi sa naiṣadhāya tāṃ harinmaṇerbhojanabhājanam mahat ||29||
chade sadaiva cchavimasya bibhratāṃ na kekināṃ sarpaviṣaṃ visarpati |
na nīlakaṇṭhatvamadhāsyadatra cet sa kālakūṭaṃ bhagavānabhokṣyata ||30||
virādhya durvāsasamaskhaladdivaḥ srajaṃ tyajannasya kimindrasindhuraḥ |
adatta tasmai sa madacchalātsadā yamabhramātaṅgatayaiva varṣukam ||31||
madānmadagre bhavatāthavā bhiyā paraṃ digantādapi yāta jīvata |
iti sma yo dikkariṇaḥ svakarṇayorvinaiva varṇasrajamāgatairgataiḥ ||32||
babhāra bījaṃ nijakīrtaye radau dviṣāmakīrtyai khalu dānavipluṣaḥ |
śravaḥśramaiḥ kumbhakucāṃ śiraḥśriyaṃ mude madasvedavatīmupāsta yaḥ ||33||
na tena vāheṣu vivāhadakṣiṇīkṛteṣu saṃkhyānubhave'bhavatkṣamaḥ |
na śātakumbheṣu na mattakumbhiṣu prayatnavānko'pi na ratnarāśiṣu ||34||
karagrahe vāmyamadhatta yastayoḥ prasādya bhaimyānu ca dakṣiṇīkṛtaḥ |
kṛtaḥ puraskṛtya tato nalena sa pradakṣiṇastatkṣaṇamāśuśukṣaṇiḥ ||35||
sthirā tvamaśmeva bhaveti mantravāganeśadāśāsya kimāśu tāṃ hriyā |
śilā caletpreraṇayā nṛṇāmapi sthitestu nācāli biḍaujasāpi sā ||36||
priyāṃśukagranthinibaddhavāsasaṃ tadā purodhā vidadhe vidarbhajām |
jagāda vicchidya paṭaṃ prayāsyato nalādaviśvāsamivaiṣa viśvavit ||37||
dhruvāvalokāya tadunmukhabhuvā nirdiśya patyābhidadhe vidarbhajā |
kimasya na syādaṇimākṣisākṣikastathāpi tathyo mahimāgamoditaḥ ||38||
dhavena sādarśi vadhūrarundhatīṃ satīmimāṃ paśya gatāmivāṇutām |
kṛtasya pūrvaṃ hṛdi bhūpateḥ kṛte tṛṇīkṛtasvargapaterjanāditi ||39||
prasūnatā tatkarapallavasthitairuḍucchavirvyomavihāribhiḥ pathi |
mukhe'marāṇāmanale radāvalerabhāji lājairanayojjhitairdyutiḥ ||40||
tayā pratīṣṭāhutidhūmapaddhatirgatā kapole mṛganābhiśobhitām |
yayau dṛśorañjanatāṃ śrutau śritā tamālalīlāmalike'lakāyitā ||41||
apahnutaḥ svedabharaḥ kare tayostrapājuṣordānajalairmilanmuhuḥ |
dṛśorapi prasrutamasru sāttvikaṃ ghanaiḥ samādhīyata dhūmalaṅghanaiḥ ||42||
bahūni bhīmasya vasūni dakṣiṇāṃ prayacchataḥ sattvamavekṣya tatkṣaṇam |
janeṣu romāñcamiteṣu miśratāṃ yayustayoḥ kaṇṭakakuḍmalaśriyaḥ ||43||
babhūva na stambhavijitvarī tayoḥ śrutikriyārambhaparamparātvarā |
na kampasaṃpattimalumpadagrataḥ sthito'pi vahniḥ samidhā samedhitaḥ ||44||
damasvasuḥ pāṇimamuṣya gṛhṇataḥ purodhasā saṃvidadhetarāṃ vidheḥ |
maharṣiṇevāṅgirasena sāṅgatā pulomajāmudvahataḥ śatakratoḥ ||45||
sakautukāgāramagātpuraṃdhribhiḥ sahasrarandhrīkṛtamīkṣituṃ tataḥ |
adhātsahasrākṣatanutramitratāmadhiṣṭhitaṃ yatkhalu jiṣṇunāmunā ||46||
tathāśanāya niraśeṣi no hriyā na samyagāloki parasparakriyā |
vimuktasaṃbhogamaśāyi saspṛhaṃ vareṇa vadhvā ca yathāvidhi tryaham ||47||
kaṭākṣaṇājjanyajanairnijaprajāḥ kvacitparīhārasamacīkarttarām |
dharāpsarobhirvarayātrayāgatānabhojayadbhojakulāṅkuraḥ kvacit ||48||
sa kaṃcidūce racayantu temanopahāramatrāṅgaruceryathocitam |
pipāsataḥ kāścana sarvatomukhaṃ tavārpayantāmapi kāmamodanam ||49||
mukhena tetropaviśatvasāviti prayācya sṛṣṭānumatiṃ khalāhasat |
varāṅgabhāgaḥ svamukhaṃ mato'dhunā sa hi sphuṭaṃ yena kilopaviśyate ||50||
yuvāmime me stritame itīriṇau gale tathoktā nijagucchamekikā |
na bhāsyadastucchagalo vadanniti nyadhatta janyasya tataḥ parākṛṣat ||51||
nalāya vālavyajanaṃ vidhunvatī damasya dāsyā nibhṛtaṃ pade'rpitāt |
ahāsi lokaiḥ saraṭātpaṭojjhinī bhayena jaṅghāyatilaṅghiraṃhasaḥ ||52||
puraḥsthalāṅgūlamadātkhalā vṛsīmupāviśattatra ṛjurvaradvijaḥ |
punastadutthāpya nijāmatervadā'hasacca paścātkṛtapucchatatpradā ||53||
svayaṃ kathābhirvarapakṣasubhruvaḥ sthirīkṛtāyāḥ padayugmamantarā |
pareṇa paścānnibhṛtaṃ nyadhāpayaddadarśa cādarśatalaṃ hasankhalu ||54||
athopacāroddhuracārulocanā vilāsanirvāsitadhairyasaṃpadaḥ |
smarasya śilpaṃ varavargavikriyā vilokakaṃ lokamahāsayanmuhuḥ ||55||
tirovaladvaktrasarojanālayā smite smitaṃ yatkhalu yūni bālayā |
tayā tadīye hṛdaye nikhāya tadvyadhīyatāsaṃmukhalakṣyavedhitā ||56||
kṛtaṃ yadanyatkaraṇocitatyajā didṛkṣu cakṣuryadavāri bālayā |
hṛdastadīyasya tadeva kāmuke jagāda vārtāmakhilāṃ khaluṃ khalu ||57||
jalaṃ dadatyāḥ kalitānatermukhaṃ vyavasyatā sāhasikena cumbitam |
pade patadvāriṇi mandapāṇinā pratīkṣito'nyekṣaṇavañcanakṣaṇaḥ ||58||
yuvānamālokya vidagdhaśīlayā svapāṇipāthoruhanālanirmitaḥ |
ślatho'pi sakhyāṃ paridhiḥ kalānidhau dadhāvaho taṃ prati gāḍhabandhatām ||59||
natabhruvaḥ svacchanakhānubimbanacchalena ko'pi sphuṭakampakaṇṭakaḥ |
payo dadatyāścaraṇe bhṛśaṃ kṣataḥ smarasya bāṇaiḥ śaraṇe nyavikṣata ||60||
mukhaṃ yadasmāyi vibhujya subhruvā hriyaṃ yadālambya natāsyamāsitam |
avādi vā yanmṛdu gadgadaṃ yuvā tadeva jagrāha tadāptilagnakam ||61||
vilokya yūnā vyajanaṃ vidhunvatīmavāptasattvena bhṛśaṃ prasiṣvade |
udastakaṇṭhena mṛṣoṣmanāṭinā vijitya lajjāṃ dadṛśe tadānanam ||62||
sa tatkucaspṛṣṭakaceṣṭidorlatācaladdalābhavyajanānilākulaḥ |
avāpa nānānalajālaśṛṅkhalānibaddhanīḍodbhavavibhramaṃ yuvā ||63||
avacchaṭā kāpi kaṭākṣaṇasya sā tathaiva bhaṅgī vacanasya kācana |
yathā yuvabhyāmanunāthane mithaḥ kṛśo'pi dūtasya na śeṣitaḥ śramaḥ ||64||
papau na ko'pi kṣamamāsyamelitaṃ jalasya gaṇḍūṣamudītasaṃmadaḥ |
cucumba tatra pratibimbitaṃ mukhaṃ puraḥ sphuratyāḥ smarakāṛmukabhruvaḥ ||65||
harinmaṇerbhojanabhājane'rpite gatāḥ prakopaṃ kila vārayātrikāḥ |
bhṛtaṃ na śākaiḥ pravitīrṇamasti vastviṣedamevaṃ hariteti bodhitāḥ ||66||
dhruvaṃ vinītaḥ smitapūrvavāgyuvā kimapyapṛcchanna vilokayanmukham |
sthitāṃ puraḥ sphāṭikakuṭṭime vadhūṃ tadaṅghriyugmāvanimadhyabaddhadṛk ||67||
amī lasadbāṣpamakhaṇḍitākhilaṃ viyuktamanyonyamamuktamāṛdavam |
rasottaraṃ gauramapīvaraṃ rasādabhuñjatāmodanamodanaṃ janāḥ ||68||
vayovaśastokavikasvarastanīṃ tirastiraścumbati sundare dṛśā |
svayaṃ kila srastamuraḥsthamambaraṃ gurustanī hrīṇatarā parādade ||69||
yadādihetuḥ surabhiḥ samudbhave bhavedyadājyaṃ surabhirdhruvaṃ tataḥ |
vadhūbhirebhyaḥ pravitīrya pāyasaṃ tadoghakulyātaṭasaikataṃ kṛtam ||70||
yadaṣyapītā vasudhālayaiḥ sudhā tadapyadaḥ svādu tato'numīyate |
api kratūṣarbudhadagdhagandhine spṛhāṃ yadasmai dadhate sudhāndhasaḥ ||71||
abodhi no hrīnibhṛtaṃ madiṅgitaṃ pratītya vā nādṛtavatyasāviti |
lunāti yūnaḥ sma dhiyaṃ kiyadgatā nivṛtya bālādaradarśaneṣuṇā ||72||
na rājikārāddhamabhoji tatra kairmukhena sītkārakṛtā dadhaddadhi |
dhutottamāṅgaiḥ kaṭubhāvapāṭavādakāṇḍakaṇḍūyitamūrdhatālubhiḥ ||73||
viyogidāhāya kaṭūbhavattviṣastuṣārabhānoriva khaṇḍamāhṛtam |
sitaṃ mṛdu prāgatha dāhadāyi tatkhalaḥ suhṛtpūrvamivāhitastataḥ ||74||
navau yuvānau nijabhāvagopināvabhūmiṣu prāgvihitabhramikramaḥ |
dṛśorvidhattaḥ sma yadṛcchayā kila tribhāgamanyonyamukhe punaḥ punaḥ ||75||
vyadhustamāṃ te mṛgamāṃsasādhitaṃ rasādaśitvā mṛdu temanaṃ manaḥ |
niśādhavotsaṅgakuraṅgajairadaḥ palaiḥ sapīyūṣajalaiḥ kimaśrapi ||76||
parasparākūtajadūtakṛtyayoranaṅgamārāddhumapi kṣaṇaṃ prati |
nimeṣaṇenaiva kiyaccirāyuṣā janeṣu yūnorudapādi nirṇayaḥ ||77||
aharniśā veti ratāya pṛcchati kramoṣṇaśītānnakarārpaṇādviṭe |
hriyā vidagdhā kila tanniṣedhinī nyadhatta saṃdhyāmadhure'dhare'ṅgulim ||78||
krameṇa kūraṃ spṛśatoṣmaṇaḥ padaṃ sitāṃ ca śītāṃ catureṇa vīkṣitā |
dadhau vidagdhāruṇite'dhare'ṅgulīmanaucitīcintanavismitā kila ||79||
kiyattyajannodanamānayankiyatkarasya papraccha gatāgatena yām |
ahaṃ kimeṣyāmi kimeṣyasīti sā vyadhatta namraṃ kila lajjayānanam ||80||
yathāmiṣe jagmuranāmiṣabhramaṃ nirāmiṣe cāmiṣamohamūhire |
tathā vidagdhaiḥ parikarmanirmitaṃ vicitramete parihasya bhojitāḥ ||81||
nakhena kṛtvādharasannibhāṃ nibhādyuvā mṛduvyañjanamāṃsaphālikām |
dadaṃśa dantaiḥ praśaśaṃsa tadrasaṃ vihasya paśyanpariveṣikādharam ||82||
anekasaṃyojanayā tathākṛternikṛtya niṣpiṣya ca tādṛgarjanāt |
amī kṛtākālikavastuvismayaṃ janā bahu vyañjanamabhyavāharan ||83||
pipāsurasmīti vibodhitā mukhaṃ nirīkṣya bālā suhitena vāriṇaḥ |
punaḥ kar kartumanā galantikāṃ hasātsakhīnāṃ sahasā nyavartata ||84||
yuvā samāditsuramatragaṃ ghṛtaṃ vilokya tatraiṇadṛśo'nubimbanam |
cakāra tannīviniveśinaṃ karaṃ babhūva tacca sphuṭakaṇṭakotkaram ||85||
pralehajasnehadhṛtānubimbanāṃ cucumba ko'pi śritabhojanacchalaḥ |
muhuḥ parispṛśya karāṅgulīmukhaistato nu raktaiḥ svamavāpitairmukham ||86||
arādhi yanmīnamṛgājapattrijaiḥ palairmṛdu svādu sugandhi temanam |
aśāki lokaiḥ kuta eva jemituṃ na tattu saṃkhyātumapi sma śakyate ||87||
kṛtārthanaścāṭubhiriṅgitaiḥ purā parāsi yaḥ kiṃcanakuñcitabhuvā |
kṣipanmukhe bhojanalīlayāṅgulīḥ punaḥ prasannānanayānvakampi saḥ ||88||
akāri nīhāranibhaṃ prabhañjanādadhūpi yaccāgurusāradārubhiḥ |
nipīya bhṛṅgārakasaṅgi tatra tairavarṇi vāri prativāramīdṛśam ||89||
tvayā vidhātaryadakāri cāmṛtaṃ kṛtaṃ ca yajjīvanamambu sādhu tat |
vṛthedamārambhi tu sarvatomukhastathocitaḥ kartuṃidaṃpibastava ||90||
sarojakośābhinayena pāṇinā sthite'pi kūre muhureva yācate |
sakhi tvamasmai vitara tvamityubhe mitho na vādāddadatuḥ kilaudanam ||91||
iyaṃ kiyaccārukuceti paśyate payaḥpradāyā hṛdayaṃ samāvṛtam |
dhruvaṃ manojñā vyataradyuduttaraṃ bhiṣeṇa bhṛṅgāradhṛte karadvayī ||92||
amībhirākaṇṭhamabhoji tadgṛhe tuṣāradhārāmṛditeva śarkarā |
hayadviṣadbaṣkayaṇīpayaḥsutaṃ sudhāhradātpaṅkamivoddhṛtaṃ dadhi ||93||
tadantarantaḥ suṣirasya bindubhiḥ karambitaṃ kalpayatā jagatkṛtā |
itastataḥ spaṣṭamacori māyinā nirīkṣya tṛṣṇācalajihvatābhṛtā ||94||
dadāsi me tanna ruceryadāspadaṃ na yatra rāgaḥ sitayāpi kiṃ tayā |
itīriṇe bimbaphalaṃ phalecchalādadāyi bimbādharayārucecca tat ||95||
samaṃ yayoriṅgitavānvayasyayostayorvihāyopahṛtapratīṅgitām |
akāri nākūtamavāri sā yayā vidagdhayā'rañji tayaiva bhāvavit ||96||
sakhīṃ prati smāha yuveṅgitekṣiṇī krameṇa te'yaṃ kṣamate na ditsutām |
viloma tadvyañjanamarpyate tvayā varaṃ kimasmai na nitāntamarthine ||97||
samāptilipyeva bhujikriyāvidherdalodaraṃ vartulayālayīkṛtam |
alaṃkṛtaṃ kṣīravaṭaistadaśnatāṃ rarāja pākārpitagairikaśriyā ||98||
cucumba norvīvalayorvaśīṃ paraṃ puro'dhipāri pratibimbitāṃ viṭaḥ |
punaḥpunaḥ pānakapānakaitavāccakāra taccumbanacuṃkṛtānyapi ||99||
ghanairamīṣāṃ pariveṣakairjanairavarṣi varṣopalagolakāvalī |
caladbhujābhūṣaṇaratnarociṣā dhṛtendracāpaiḥ śritacāndrasaurabhā ||100||
kiyadbahu vyañjanametadarpyate mameti tṛptervadatāṃ punaḥpunaḥ |
amūni saṃkhyātumasāvaḍhauki taiśchalena teṣāṃ kaṭhinīva bhūyasī ||101||
vidagdhabāleṅgitagupticāturī pravahlikotpāṭanapāṭave hṛdaḥ |
nijasya ṭīkāṃ prababandha kāmukaḥ spṛśadbhirākūtaśataistadaucitīm ||102||
ghṛtaplute bhojanabhājane puraḥ sphuratpuraṃdhripratibimbitākṛteḥ |
yuvā nidhāyorasi laḍḍukadvayaṃ nakhairlilekhātha mamarda nirdayam ||103||
vilokite rāgitareṇa sasmitaṃ hriyātha vaimukhyamite sakhījane |
tadālirānīya kuto'pi śārkarīṃ kare dadau tasya vihasya putrikām ||104||
nirīkṣya ramyāḥ pariveṣikā dhruvaṃ na bhuktamevaibhiravāptatṛptibhiḥ |
aśaknuvidbhirbahubhuktavattayā yadujjhitā vyañjanapuñjarāśayaḥ ||105||
pṛthakprakāreṅgitaśaṃsitāśayo yuvā yayodāsi tayāpi tāpitaḥ |
tato nirāśaḥ paribhāvayanparāmaye tayātoṣi saroṣāyaiva saḥ ||106||
payaḥ smitā maṇḍakamaṇḍanāmbarā vaṭānanenduḥ pṛthulaḍḍukastanī |
padaṃ rucerbhojyabhujāṃ bhujikriyā priyā babhūvojjvalakūrahāriṇī ||107||
ciraṃ yuvākūtaśataiḥ kṛtāṛthanaściraṃ saroṣeṅgitayā ca nirdhutaḥ |
sṛjankarakṣālanalīlayāñjalīnaseci kiṃcidvidhutāmbudhārayā ||108||
na ṣaḍvidhaḥ ṣiṅgajanasya bhojane tathā yathā yauvatavibhramodbhavaḥ |
apāraśṛṅgāramayaḥ samunmiṣanbhṛśaṃ rasastoṣamadhatta saptamaḥ ||109||
mukhe nidhāya kramukaṃ nalānugairathaujjhi parṇāliravekṣya vṛścikam |
damārpitāntarmukhavāsanirmitaṃ bhayāvilaiḥ svabhramahāsitākhilaiḥ ||110||
amīṣu tathyānṛtaratnajātayorvarāṭarāṭcārunitāntacāruṇoḥ |
svayaṃ gṛhāṇaikamihetyudīrya taddvayaṃ dadau śeṣajighṛkṣave hasan ||111||
iti dvikṛtvaḥ śucimṛṣṭabhojināṃ dināni teṣāṃ katicinmudā yayuḥ |
dviraṣṭasaṃvatsaravārasundarīparīṣṭibhistuṣṭimupeyuṣāṃ niśi ||112||
uvāsa vaidarbhagṛheṣu pañcaṣā niśāḥ kṛśāṅgīṃ pariṇīya tāṃ nalaḥ |
atha pratasthe niṣadhānsahānayā rathena vārṣṇeyagṛhītaraśminā ||113||
parasya na spraṣṭumimāmadhikriyā priyā śiśuḥ prāṃśurasāviti bruvan |
rathe sa bhaimīṃ svayamadhyarūruhanna tatkilāślikṣadimāṃ janekṣitaḥ ||114||
iti smaraḥ śīghramatiścakāra taṃ vadhūṃ ca romāñcabhareṇa karkaśau |
skhaliṣyati snigdhatanuḥ priyādiyaṃ mradīyasī pīḍanabhīrudoryugāt ||115||
tathā kimājanmanijāṅkavardhitāṃ prahitya putrīṃ pitarau viṣedatuḥ |
visṛjya tau taṃ duhituḥ patiṃ yathā vinītatālakṣaguṇībhavadguṇam ||116||
nijādanuvrajya sa maṇḍalāvadhernalaṃ nivṛttau caṭulāpatāṃ gataḥ |
taḍāgakallola ivānilaṃ taṭāddhṛtānatirvyāvavṛte varāṭarāṭ ||117||
pitātmanaḥ puṇyamanāpadaḥ kṣamā dhanaṃ manastuṣṭirathākhilaṃ nalaḥ |
ataḥ paraṃ putri na ko'pi te'hamityudasrureṣa vyasṛjannijaurasīm ||118||
priyaḥ priyaikācaraṇāccireṇa tāṃ pituḥ smarantīmacikitsadādhiṣu |
tathāsta tanmātṛviyogavāḍavaḥ sa tu priyapremamahāmbudhāvapi ||119||
asau mahībhṛdbahudhātumaṇḍitastayā nijopatyakayeva kāmapi |
bhuvā kuraṅgakṣaṇadanticārayorbabhāra śobhāṃ kṛtapādasevayā ||120||
tadekatānasya nṛpasya rakṣituṃ ciroḍhayā bhāvamivātmani śriyā |
vihāya sāpatnyamarañji bhīmajā samagratadvāñchitapūrtivṛttibhiḥ ||121||
masāramālāvalitoraṇāṃ puraṃ nijādviyogādiva lambitālakām |
dadarśa paśyāmiva naiṣadhaḥ priyāmathāśritodgrīvikamunnatairgṛhaiḥ ||122||
purīnirīkṣānyamanā manāgiti priyāya bhaimyā nibhṛtaṃ visarjitaḥ |
yayau kaṭākṣaḥ sahasā nivartinā tadīkṣaṇenārdhapathe samāgamam ||123||
atha nagaradhṛtairamātyaratnaiḥpathi samiyāya sa jāyayābhirāmaḥ |
madhuriva kusumaśriyā sanāthaḥ kramamilitairalibhiḥ kutūhalotkaiḥ ||124||
kiyadapi kathayansvavṛttajātaṃ śravaṇakutūhalacañcaleṣu teṣu |
kiyadapi nijadeśavṛttamebhyaḥ śravaṇapathaṃ sa nayanpurīṃ viveśa ||125||
atha pathi pathi lājairātmano bāhuvallīmukulakulasakulyaiḥ pūjayantyo jayeti |
kṣitipatimupanemustaṃ dadhānā janānāmamṛtajalamṛṇālīsaukumāṛyaṃ kumāryaḥ ||126||
abhinavadamayantīkāntijālāvalokapravaṇapurapurandhrīvaktracandrānvayena |
nikhilanagarasaudhāṭṭāvalīcandraśālāḥ
kṣāṇamiva nijasaṃjñāṃsānvayāmanvabhūvan ||127||
niṣadhanṛpamukhenduśrīsudhāṃ saudhavātāyanavivaragaraśmiśreṇinālopanītām |
papurasamapipāsāpāṃsulatvotparāgāṇyakhilapurapurandhrīnetranīlotpalāni ||128||
avanipatipathāṭṭastraiṇapāṇipravālaskhalitasurabhilājavyājabhājaḥpratīcchan |
upari kusumavṛṣṭīreṣa vaimānikānāmabhinavakṛtabhaimīsaudhabhūmiṃ viveśa ||129||
iti pariṇayamitthaṃ yānamekatra yāne
daracakitakaṭākṣaprekṣaṇaṃ cānayostat |
divi diviṣadadhīśāḥkautukenāvalokya
praṇidadhurivagantuṃ nākamānandasāndrāḥ ||130||
śrīharṣaṃ kavirājarājimukuṭālaṃkārahīraḥ sutaṃ
śrīhīraḥ suṣuve jitendriyacayaṃ māmalladevī ca yam |
kāśmīrairmahite caturdaśatayīṃ vidyāṃ vidadbhirmahākāvye tadbhuvi naiṣadhīyacarite sargo'gamatṣoḍaśaḥ ||131||

 

saptadaśa sargaḥ
athārabhya vṛthāprāyaṃ dharitrīdhāvanaśramam |
surāḥ sarasvadullolalīlā jagmuryathāgatam ||1||
bhaimīṃ patye bhuvastasmai ciraṃ citte dhṛtāmapi |
vidyāmiva vinītāya na viṣeduḥ pradāya te ||2||
kāntimanti vimānāni bhejire bhāsurāḥ surāḥ |
sphaṭikādrestaṭānīva pratibimbā vivasvataḥ ||3||
javājjātena vātena balākṛṣṭabalāhakaiḥ |
śVasanātsvasya śīghratvaṃ rathaireṣāmivākathi ||4||
kamāddavīyasāṃ teṣāṃ tadānīṃ samadṛśyata |
spaṣṭamaṣṭaguṇaiśvaryātparyavasyannivāṇimā ||5||
tatāna vidyutā teṣāṃ rathe pītapatākatām |
labdhaketuśikhollekhā lekhā jalamucaḥ kvacit ||6||
punaḥpunarmilantīṣu pathi pāthodapaṅktiṣu |
nākanātharathālambi babhūvābharaṇaṃ dhanuḥ ||7||
jale jaladajālānāṃ vajritajrānubimbanaiḥ |
jāne tatkālajaisteṣāṃ jātāśanisanāthatā ||8||
sphuṭaṃ sāvarṇivaṃśyānāṃ kulacchatraṃ mahībhujām |
cakre daṇḍabhṛtaścumbandaṇḍaścaṇḍaruciṃ kvacit ||9||
nalabhīmabhuvoḥ premṇi vismitāyā dadhau divaḥ |
pāśipāśaḥ śiraḥkampasrastabhūṣaśravaḥśriyam ||10||
pavanaskandhamāruhya nṛtyattarakaraḥ śikhī |
anena prāpi bhaimīti bhramaṃ cakre nabhaḥsadām ||11||
tatkarṇau bhāratī dūnau virahādbhīmajāgirām |
adhvani dhvanibhirvaiṇiranukalpairvyanodayat ||12||
athāyāntamavaikṣanta te janaughamasitviṣam |
teṣāṃ pratyudgamaprītyā miladvyomeva mūrtimat ||13||
adrākṣurājihānaṃ te smaramagresaraṃ surāḥ |
akṣāvinayaśikṣārthaṃ kalineva puraskṛtam ||14||
agamyārthaṃ tṛṇaprāṇāḥ pṛṣṭhasthīkṛtabhīhriyaḥ |
śambhalībhuktasarvasvā janā yatpāripārśvikāḥ ||15||
bibharti lokajidbhāvaṃ buddhasya spardhayeva yaḥ |
yasyeśatulayevātra kartṛtvamaśarīriṇaḥ ||16||
īśvarasya jagatkṛtsnaṃ sṛṣṭimākulayannimām |
asti yo'strīkṛtastrīkastasya vairaṃ smaranniva ||17||
cakre śakrādinetrāṇāṃ smaraḥ pītanalaśriyām |
api daivatavaidyābhyāmacikitsyamarocakam ||18||
yattatkṣipantamutkampamutthāyukamathāruṇam |
bubudhurvibudhāḥ krodhamākrośākrośaghoṣaṇam ||19||
yamupāsanta dantauṣṭhakṣatāsṛkśiṣyacakṣuṣaḥ |
bhrukuṭīphaṇinīnādanibhaniśvāsaphūtkṛtaḥ ||20||
durgaṃ kāmāśugenāpi durlaṅghyamavalambya yaḥ |
durvāsohṛdayaṃ lokānsendrānapi didhakṣati ||21||
vairāgyaṃ yaḥ karotyuccai rañjanaṃ janayannapi |
sūte sarvendriyācchādi prajvalannapi yastamaḥ ||22||
pañceṣuvijayāśaktau bhavasya krudhyato jayāt |
yenānyavigṛhītārijayakālanayaḥ śritaḥ ||23||
hastau vistārayannibhye bibhyadardhapathasthavāk |
sūcayankākumākūtairlobhastatra vyaloki taiḥ ||24||
dainyastainyamayā nityamatyāhārāmayāvinaḥ |
bhuñjānajanasākūtapaśyā yasyānujīvinaḥ ||25||
dhanidānāmbuvṛṣṭeryaḥ pātrapāṇāvavagrahaḥ |
svāndāsāniva hā niḥsvādvikrīṇīte'rthavatsu yaḥ ||26||
ekadvikaraṇe hetū mahāpātakapañcake |
na tṛṇe manyate kopakāmau yaḥ pañca kārayan ||27||
yaḥ sarvendriyasadmāpi jihvāṃ bahvavalambate |
tasyāmācāryakaṃ yāñjābaṭave pāṭave'rjitum ||28||
pathyāṃ tathyāmagṛhṇantamandhaṃ bandhuprabodhanām |
śūnyamāśliṣya nojjhantaṃ mohamaikṣanta hanta te ||29||
śvaḥśvaḥ prāṇaprayāṇe'pi na smaranti smaradviṣāḥ |
magnāḥ kuṭumbajambāle bāliśā yadupāsinaḥ ||30||
puṃsāmalabdhanirvāṇajñānadīpamayātmanām |
antarmlāpayati vyaktaṃ yaḥ kajjalavadujjvalam ||31||
brahmacārivanasthāyiyatayo gṛhiṇaṃ yathā |
trayo yamupajīvanti krodhalobhamanobhavāḥ ||32||
jāgratāmapi nidrā yaḥ paśyatāmapi yo'ndhatā |
śṛute satyapi jāḍyaṃ yaḥ prakāśe'pi ca yastamaḥ ||33||
kurusainyaṃ hareṇeva prāgalajjata nāṛjunaḥ |
hataṃ yena jayankāmastamoguṇajuṣā jagat ||34||
cihnitāḥ katiciddevaiḥ prācaḥ paricayādamī |
anye na kecanācūḍamenaḥ kañcukamecakāḥ ||35||
tatrodgūrṇa ivārṇodhau sainye'bhyarṇamupeyuṣi |
kasyāpyākarṇayāmāsuste varṇānkarṇakarkaśān ||36||
grāvonmajjanavadyajñaphaleti śrutisatyatā |
kā śraddhā tatra dhīvṛddhāḥ kāmādhvā yatkhilīkṛtaḥ ||37||
kenāpi bodhisattvena jātaṃ sattvena hetunā |
yadvedamarmabhedāya jagade jagadasthiram ||38||
agnihotraṃ trayītantraṃ tridaṇḍaṃ bhasmapuṇḍrakam |
prajñāpauruṣāniḥsvānāṃ jīvo jalpati jīvikā ||39||
śuddhirvaṃśadvayīśuddhau pitroḥ pitroryadekaśaḥ |
tadānantakulādoṣādadoṣā jātirasti kā ||40||
kāminīvargasaṃsargairna kaḥ saṃkrāntapātakaḥ |
nāśnāti snāti hā mohātkāmakṣāmavrataṃ jagat ||41||
īrṣyayā rakṣato nārīrdhikkulasthitidāmbhikān |
smarāndhatvāviśeṣe'pi tathā naramarakṣataḥ ||42||
paradāranivṛttiryā so'yaṃ svayamanādṛtaḥ |
ahalyākelilolena dambho dambholipāṇinā ||43||
gurutalpagatau pāpakalpanāṃ tyajata dvijāḥ |
yeṣāṃ vaḥ patyuratyuccairgurudāragrahe grahaḥ ||44||
pāpāttāpā mudaḥ puṇyātparāsoḥ syuriti śrutiḥ |
vaiparītyaṃ drutaṃ sākṣāttadākhyāta balābale ||45||
saṃdehe'pyanyadehāptervivarjyaṃ vṛjinaṃ yadi |
tyajata śrotriyāḥ sattraṃ hiṃsādūṣaṇasaṃśayāt ||46||
yastrivedīvidāṃ vandyaḥ sa vyāso'pi jajalpa vaḥ |
rāmāyā jātakāmāyāḥ praśastā hastadhāraṇā ||47||
sukṛte vaḥ kathaṃ śraddhā surate ca kathaṃ na sā |
tatkarma puruṣaḥ kuryādyenānte sukhamedhate ||48||
bālātkuruta pāpāni santu tānyakṛtāni vaḥ |
sarvānbalakṛtāndoṣānakṛtānmanurabravīt ||49||
svāgamārthe'pi mā sthāsmiṃstīrthikā vicikitsavaḥ |
taṃ tamācaratānandaṃ svacchandaṃ yaṃ yamicchatha ||50||
śrutismṛtyarthabodheṣu kvaikamatyaṃ mahādhiyām |
vyākhyā buddhibalāpekṣā sā nopekṣya sukhonmukhī ||51||
yasminnasmīti dhīrdehe taddāhe vaḥ kimenasā |
kvāpi tatkiṃ phalaṃ na syādātmeti parasākṣike ||52||
mṛtaḥ smarati janmāni mṛte karmaphalormayaḥ |
anyabhuktairmṛte tṛptirityalaṃ dhūrtavārtayā ||53||
janena janatāsmīti kāyaṃ nāyaṃ tvamityasau |
tyājyate grāhyate cānyadaho śrutyātidhūrtayā ||54||
ekaṃ saṃdigdhayostāvadbhāvi tatreṣṭajanmani |
hetumāhuḥ svamantrādīnasaṅgānanyathā viṭāḥ ||55||
ekasya viśvapāpena tāpe'nante nimajjataḥ |
kaḥ śrautasyātmano bhīro bhāraḥ syādduritena te ||56||
kiṃ te vṛntahṛtātpuṣpāttanmātre hi phalatyadaḥ |
nyasya tanmūrdhnyananyasya nyāsyamevāśmano yadi ||57||
tṛṇānīva ghṛṇāvādānvidhūnaya vadhūranu |
tavāpi tādṛśasyaiva kā ciraṃ janavañcanā ||58||
kurudhvaṃ kāmadevājñāṃ brahmādyairapyalaṅghitām |
vedo'pi devakīyājñā tatrājñāḥ kādhikārhaṇā ||59||
pralāpamapi vedasya bhāgaṃ manyadhva eva cet |
kenābhagyena duḥkhānna vidhīnapi tathecchatha ||60||
śrutiṃ śraddhattha vikṣiptāḥ prakṣiptāṃ brūtha ca svayam |
mīmāṃsāmāṃsalaprajñāstāṃ yūpadvipadāpinīm ||61||
ko hi vedāstyamuṣminvā loka ityāha yā śrutiḥ |
tatprāmāṇyādamuṃ lokaṃ lokaḥ pratyetu vā katham ||62||
dharmādharmau manurjalpannaśakyārjanavarjanau |
vyājānmaṇḍaladaṇḍārthī śṛadadhāyi mudhā budhaiḥ ||63||
vyāsasyaiva girā tasmiñśraddhetyaddhā stha tāntrikāḥ |
matsyasyāpyupadeśyānvaḥ ko matsyānapi bhāṣatām ||64||
paṇḍitaḥ pāṇḍavānāṃ sa vyāsaścāṭupaṭuḥ kaviḥ |
nininda teṣu nindatsu stuvatsu stutavānna kim ||65||
na bhrātuḥ kila devyāṃ sa vyāsaḥ kāmātsamāsajat |
dāsīratastadāsīdyanmātrā tatrāpyadeśi kim ||66||
devairdvijaiḥ kṛtā granthāḥ panthā yeṣāṃ tadādṛtau |
gāṃ nataiḥ kiṃ na tairvyaktaṃ tato'pyātmādharīkṛtaḥ ||67||
sādhukāmukatāmuktā śāntasvāntairmakhonmukhaiḥ |
sāraṅgalocanāsārāṃ divaṃ pretyāpi lipsubhiḥ ||68||
kaḥ śamaḥ kriyatāṃ prājñāḥ priyāprītau pariśramaḥ |
bhasmībhūtasya bhūtasya punarāgamanaṃ kutaḥ ||69||
ubhayī prakṛtiḥ kāme sajjediti munermanaḥ |
apavarge tṛtīyeti bhaṇataḥ pāṇinerapi ||70||
bibhratyupariyānāya janā janitamajjanāḥ |
vigrahāyāgrataḥ paścādgatvarorabhravibhramam ||71||
enasānena tiryaksyādityādiḥ kā bibhīṣikā |
rājilo'pi hi rājeva svaiḥ sukhī sukhahetubhiḥ ||72||
hatāśceddivi dīvyanti daityā daityāriṇā raṇe |
tatrāpi tena yudhyantāṃ hatā api tathaiva te ||73||
svaṃ ca brahma ca saṃsāre muktau tu brahma kevalam |
iti svocchittimuktyuktivaidagdhī vedavādinām ||74||
muktaye yaḥ śilātvāya śāstramūce sacetasām |
gotamaṃ tamavekṣyaiva yathā vittha tathaiva saḥ ||75||
dārā hariharādīnāṃ tanmagnamanaso bhṛśam |
kiṃ na muktāḥkutaḥ santi kārāgāre manobhuvaḥ ||76||
devaścedasti sarvajñaḥ karuṇābhāgavandhyavāk |
tatkiṃ vāgvyayamātrānnaḥ kṛtārthayati nāṛthinaḥ ||77||
bhavināṃ bhāvayanduḥkhaṃ svakarmajamapīśvaraḥ |
syādakāraṇavairī naḥ kāraṇādapare pare ||78||
tarkāpratiṣṭhayā sāmyādanyonyasya vyatighnatām |
nāprāmāṇyaṃ matānāṃ syātkeṣāṃ satpratipakṣavat ||79||
akrodhaṃ śikṣayantyanyaiḥ krodhanā ye tapodhanāḥ |
nirdhanāste dhanāyaiva dhātuvādopadeśinaḥ ||80||
kiṃ vittaṃ datta tuṣṭeyamadātari haripriyā |
dattvā sarvaṃ dhanaṃ mugdho bandhanaṃ labdhavānbaliḥ ||81||
dogdhā drogdhā ca sarvo'yaṃ dhaninaścetasā janaḥ |
visṛjya lobhasaṃkṣobhamekadvā yadyudāsate ||82||
dainyasyāyuṣyamastainyamabhakṣyaṃ kukṣivañcanā |
svācchandyamṛcchatānandakandalīkandamekakam ||83||
itthamākarṇya durvarṇaṃ śakraḥ sakrodhatāṃ dadhe |
avocaduccaiḥ kaskoyaṃ dharmamarmāṇi kṛntati ||84||
lokatrayīṃ trayīnetrāṃ vajravīryasphuratkare |
ka itthaṃ bhāṣate pākaśāsane mayi śāsati ||85||
varṇāsaṃkīrṇatāyāṃ vā jātyalopo'nyathāpi vā |
brahmahādeḥ parīkṣāsu bhaṅgamaṅga pramāṇaya ||86||
brāhmaṇyādiprasiddhāyā gantā yannekṣate jayam |
tadviśuddhimaśeṣasya varṇavaṃśasya śaṃsati ||87||
jalānalaparīkṣādau saṃvādo vedavedite |
galahastitanāstikyāṃ dhigdhiyaṃ kurute'nate ||88||
satyeva patiyogādau garbhāderadhruvodayāt |
ākṣiptaṃ nāstikāḥ karma na kiṃ marma bhinatti vaḥ ||89||
yācataḥ svagayāśrāddhaṃ pretasyāviśya kaṃcana |
nānādeśajanopajñāḥ pratyeṣi na kathāḥ katham ||90||
nītānāṃ yamadūtena nāmabhrānterupāgatau |
śraddhatse saṃvadantīṃ na paralokakathāṃ katham ||91||
jajvāla jvalanaḥ krodhādācakhyau cākṣipannamum |
kimāttha re kimātthedamasmadagre nirargalam ||92||
mahāparākiṇaḥ śrautadharmaikabalajīvinaḥ |
kṣaṇābhakṣaṇamūrcchāla smaranvismayase na kim ||93||
putreṣṭiśyenakārīrīmukhā dṛṣṭaphalā makhāḥ |
na vaḥ kiṃ dharmasaṃdehamandehajayabhānavaḥ ||94||
daṇḍatāṇḍavanaiḥ kurvansphuliṅgāliṅgitaṃ nabhaḥ |
nirmame'tha girāmūrmīrbhinnamarmeva dharmarāṭ ||95||
tiṣṭha bhostiṣṭha kaṇṭhoṣṭhaṃ kuṇṭhayāmi haṭhādayam |
apaṣṭhu paṭhataḥ pāṭhyamadhigoṣṭhi śaṭhasya te ||96||
vedaistadveṣibhistadvatsthiraṃ mataśataiḥ kṛtam |
paraṃ kaste paraṃ vācā lokaṃ lokāyata tyajet ||97||
samajñānālpabhūyiṣṭhapānthavaimatyametya yam |
loke prayāsi panthānaṃ paraloke na taṃ kutaḥ ||98||
svakanyāmanyasātkartuṃ viśvānumatidṛśvanaḥ |
loke paratra lokasya kasya na syādṛḍhaṃ manaḥ ||99||
kasminnapi mate satye hatāḥ sarvamatatyajaḥ |
taddṛṣṭyā vyarthatāmātramanarthastu na dharmajaḥ ||100||
kvāpi sarvairavaimatyātpātityādanyathā kvacit |
sthātavyaṃ śrauta eva syāddharme śeṣe'pi tatkṛteḥ ||101||
babhāṇa varuṇaḥ krodhādaruṇaḥ karuṇojjhitam |
kiṃ na pracaṇḍātpākhaṇḍapāśa pāśādbibheṣi naḥ ||102||
mānavāśakyanirmāṇā kūrmādyaṅkabilā śilā |
na śraddhāpayate mugdhāstīrthikādhvani vaḥ katham ||103||
śatakratūrujādyākhyāvikhyātirnāstikāḥ katham |
śrutivṛttāntasaṃvādairna vaścamadacīkarat ||104||
tattajjanakṛtāveśāngayāśrāddhādiyācinaḥ |
bhūtānanubhavanto'pi kathaṃ śraddhattha na śrutīḥ ||105||
nāmabhramādyamaṃ nītānatha svatanumāgatān |
saṃvādavādino jīvānvīkṣya mā tyajata śrutīḥ ||106||
saṃrambhairjambhajaitrādestabhyamānādbalādvalan |
mūrdhabaddhāñjalirdevānathaivaṃ kaścidūcivān ||107||
nāparādhī parādhīno jano'yaṃ nākanāyakāḥ |
kālasyāhaṃ kalerbandī taccāṭucaṭulānanaḥ ||108||
iti tasminvadatyeva devāḥ syandanamandiram |
kalimākalayāṃcakrurdvāparaṃ cāparaṃ puraḥ ||109||
saṃdadarśonnamadgrīvaḥ śrībahutvakṛtādbhutān |
tattatpāpaparītastānnākīyānnārakīva saḥ ||110||
gururīḍhāvalīḍhaḥ prāgabhūnnamitamastakaḥ |
sa triśaṅkurivākrāntatejaseva biḍaujasaḥ ||111||
vimukhāndraṣṭumapyenaṃ janaṃgama iva dvijān |
eṣa mattaḥ sahelaṃ tānupetya samabhāṣata ||112||
svasti vāstoṣpate tubhyaṃ śikhinnasti na khinnatā |
sakhe kāla sukhenāsi pāśahasta mudastava ||113||
svayaṃvaramahe bhaimīvaraṇāya tvarāmahe |
tadasmānanumanyadhvamadhvane tatra dhāvine ||114||
te'vajñāya tamasyoccairahaṃkāramakāraṇam |
ūcire'ticireṇainaṃ smitvā dṛṣṭamukhā mithaḥ ||115||
punarvakṣyasi mā maivaṃ kathamudvakṣyase tu saḥ |
sṛṣṭavānparameṣṭhī yaṃ naiṣṭhikabrahmacāriṇam ||116||
drohiṇaṃ druhiṇo vettu tvāmākarṇyāvakīrṇinam |
tvajjanairapi vā dhātuḥ seturlaṅghyastvayā na kim ||117||
ativṛttaḥ sa vṛttāntastrijagadyuvagarvanut |
āgacchatāmapādānaṃ sa svayaṃvara eva naḥ ||118||
nāgeṣu sānurāgeṣu paśyatsu diviṣatsu ca |
bhūmipālaṃ naraṃ bhaimī varaṃ sā'vavaradvaram ||119||
bhujageśānasadveśānvānarānitarānnarān |
amarānpāmarānbhaimī nalaṃ veda guṇojjvalam ||120||
iti śrutvā sa roṣāndhaḥ paramaścaramaṃ yugam |
jagannāśaniśārudramudrastānuktavānadaḥ ||121||
kayāpi krīḍatu brahmā divyāḥ strīrdīvyata svayam |
kalistu caratu brahma praitu vātipriyāya vaḥ ||122||
caryeva katameyaṃ vaḥ parasmai dharmadeśinām |
svayaṃ tatkurvatāṃ sarvaṃ śrotuṃ yadbibhitaḥ śrutī ||123||
tatra svayaṃvare'lambhi bhuvaḥ śrīrnaiṣādhena sā |
jagato hrīstu yuṣmābhirlābhastulyābha eva vaḥ ||124||
dūrānnaḥ prekṣya yauṣmākī yukteyaṃ vaktravakraṇā |
lajjayaivāsamarthānāṃ mukhamāsmākamīkṣitum ||125||
sthitaṃ bhavadbhiḥ paśyadbhiḥ kathaṃ bhostadasāṃpratam |
nirdagdhā durvidagdhā kiṃ sā dṛśā na jvalatkrudhā ||126||
mahāvaṃśānanādṛtya mahāntamabhilāṣukā |
svīcakāra kathaṃkāramaho sā taralaṃ nalam ||127||
bhavādṛśairdiśāmīśairmṛgyamāṇāṃ mṛgekṣaṇām |
svīkurvāṇaḥ kathaṃ soḍhaḥ kṛtarīḍhastṛṇaṃ nalaḥ ||128||
dāruṇaḥ kūṭamāśritya śikhī sākṣībhavannapi |
nāvahatkiṃ tadudvāhe kūṭasākṣikriyāmayam ||129||
aho mahaḥsahāyānāṃ saṃbhūtā bhavatāmapi |
kṣamaivāsmai kalaṅkāya devasyevāmṛtadyuteḥ ||130||
sā vavre yaṃ tamutsṛjya mahyamīrṣyājuṣāḥ stha kim |
brūtāgaḥ sadmanastasmācchadmanādyācchinadmi tān ||131||
yatadhvaṃ sahakartuṃ māṃ pāñcālī pāṇḍavairiva |
sāpi pañcabhirasmābhiḥ saṃvibhajyaiva bhujyatām ||132||
athāparivṛḍhā soḍhuṃ mūrkhatāṃ mukharasya tām |
cakre girā śarāghātaṃ bhāratī sāratīvrayā ||133||
kīrtiṃ bhaimīṃ varaṃ cāsmai dātumevāgamannamī |
na līḍhe dhīravaidagdhīṃ dhīragambhīragāhinī ||134||
vāggminīṃ jaḍajihvastāṃ prativaktumaśaktimān |
līlāvahelitāṃ kṛtvā devānevāvadatkaliḥ ||135||
prauñchi vāñchitamasmābhirapi tāṃ prati saṃprati |
tasminnale na leśo'pi kāruṇyasyāsti naḥ punaḥ ||136||
vṛtte karmaṇi kurmaḥ kiṃ tadā nābhūma tatra yat |
kālocitamidānīṃ yaḥ śṛṇutālocitaṃ punaḥ ||137||
pratijñeyaṃ nale vijñāḥ kalervijñāyatāṃ mama |
tena bhaimīṃ ca bhūmiṃ ca tyājayāmi jayāmi tam ||138||
naiṣadhena virodhaṃ me caṇḍatāmaṇḍitaijasaḥ |
jaganti hanta gāyantu raveḥ kairavavairavat ||139||
dvāparaḥ sādhukāreṇa tadvikāramadīdipat |
praṇīya śravaṇe pāṇimavocannamuce ripuḥ ||140||
vismeyamatirasmāsu sādhu vailakṣyamīkṣase |
yaddatte'lpamanalpāya taddatte hriyamātmanaḥ ||141||
phalasīmāṃ caturvargaṃ yacchatāṃśo'pi yacchati |
nalasyāsmadupaghnā sā bhaktirbhūtāvakeśinī ||142||
bhavyo na vyavasāyaste nale sādhumatau kale |
lokapālaviśālo'yaṃ niṣādhānāṃ sudhākaraḥ ||143||
na paśyāmaḥ kalestasminnavakāśaṃ kṣāmābhṛti |
nicitākhiladharme ca dvāparasyodayaṃ vayam ||144||
sā vinītaatamā bhaimī vyarthānarthagrahairaho |
kathaṃ bhavadvidhairbādhyā pramitirvibhramairiva ||145||
taṃ nāsatyayugaṃ tāṃ vā tretā spardhitumarhati |
ekaprakāśadharmāṇaṃ na kalidvāparau yuvām ||146||
kariṣye'vaśyamityuktiḥ kariṣyannapi duṣyasi |
dṛṣṭādṛṣṭā hi nāyattāḥ kāryīyā hetavastava ||147||
drohaṃ mohena yastasminnācaredacireṇa saḥ |
tatpāpasaṃbhavaṃ tāpamāpnuyādanayāttataḥ ||148||
yugaśeṣa tava dveṣastasminneṣa na sāṃpratam |
bhavitā na hitāyaitadvairaṃ te vairaseninā ||149||
tatra yāmītyasajjñānaṃ rājasaṃ sadihāsyatām |
iti tara gato mā gā rājasaṃsadi hāsyatām ||150||
gatvāntarā nalaṃ bhaimīṃ nākasmāttvaṃ pravekṣyasi |
ṣaṇṇāṃ cakramasaṃyuktaṃ paṭhyamānaṃ ḍakāravat ||151||
apare'pi diśāmīśā vācametāṃ śacīpateḥ |
anvamanyanta kiṃtvenāṃ nādatta yugayoryugam ||152||
kaliṃ prati kaliṃ devā devānpratyekaśaḥ kaliḥ |
sopahāsaṃ samairvarṇairitthaṃ vyararacanmithaḥ ||153||
tavā'gamanamevārhaṃ vairasenau tayā vṛte |
udvegena vimānena kimanenāpi dhāvatā ||154||
purā yāsi varītuṃ yāmagra eva tayā vṛte |
anyasminbhavato hāsyaṃ vṛttametattrapākaram ||155||
patyau tayā vṛte'nyasminyadarthaṃ gatavānasi |
bhavataḥ koparodhastādakṣamasya vṛthāruṣaḥ ||156||
yāsi smarañjayankāntyā yojanaughaṃ mahārvatā |
samūḍhastaṃ vṛte'nyasminkiṃ na hīste'tra pāmara ||157||
nalaṃ pratyanapetārti tārtīyīkaturīyayoḥ |
yugayoryugalaṃ buddhvā divi devā dhiyaṃ dadhuḥ ||158||
dvāparaikaparīvāraḥ kalirmatsaramūrcchitaḥ |
nalanigrāhiṇīṃ yātrāṃ jagrāha grahilaḥ kila ||159||
naleṣṭāpūrtasaṃpūrterdūraṃ durgānamuṃ prati |
niṣedhanniṣadhāngantuṃ vighnaḥ saṃjaghaṭe ghanaḥ ||160||
maṇḍalaṃ niṣādhendrasya candrasyevāmalaṃ kaliḥ |
prāpa mlāpayituṃ pāpaḥ svarbhānuriva saṃgrahāt ||161||
kiyatāpi ca kālena kālaḥ kalirupeyivān |
bhaimībharturahaṃmānī rājadhānīṃ mahībhujaḥ ||162||
vedānuddharatāṃ tatra mukhādākarṇayanpadam |
na prasārayituṃ kālaḥ kaliḥ padamapārayat ||163||
śrutipāṭhakavaktrebhyastatrākarṇayataḥ kramam |
kramaḥ saṃkucitastasya pure dūramavartata ||164||
tāvadgatirdhṛtāṭopā pādayostena saṃhitā |
na vedapāṭhikaṇṭhebhyo yāvadaaśrāvi saṃhitā ||165||
tasya homājyagandhena nāsā nāśamivāgamat |
tathātata dṛśau nāsau kratudhūmakadarthitaḥ ||166||
atithīnāṃ padāmbhobhirimaṃ pratyatipicchile |
aṅgaṇe gṛhiṇāṃ tatra khalenānena caskhale ||167||
puṭapākamasau prāpa kratuśuṣmamahoṣmabhiḥ |
tatpratyaṅgamivākarti pūrtormivyajanānilaiḥ ||168||
pitṝṇāṃ tarpaṇe varṇaiḥ kīrṇādveśmani veśmani |
kālādiva tilātkālāddūramatrasadatra saḥ ||169||
snātṝṇāṃ tilakairmene svamantardīrṇameva saḥ |
kṛpāṇībhūya hṛdayaṃ praviṣṭairiva tatra taiḥ ||170||
pumāṃsaṃ mumude tatra vidanmithyāvadāvadam |
striyaṃ prati tathā vīkṣya tamatha mlānavānayam ||171||
yajñayūpaghanāṃ jajñau sa puraṃ śaṅkusaṃkulām |
janairdharmadhanaiḥ kīrṇāṃ vyālakroḍīkṛtāṃ ca tām ||172||
sa pārśvamaśakadgantuṃ na varākaḥ parākiṇām |
māsopavāsināṃ chāyālaṅghane ghanamaskhalat ||173||
āvāhitāṃ dvijaistatra gāyatrīmarkamaṇḍalāt |
sa saṃnidadhatīṃ paśyandṛṣṭanaṣṭo'bhavadbhiyā ||174||
sa gṛhe gṛhibhiḥ pūrṇe vane vaikhānasairghane |
yatyādhāre'marāgāre kvāpi na sthānamānaśe ||175||
kvāpi nāpaśyadanviṣyanhiṃsāmātmapriyāmasau |
svamittraṃ tatra na prāpnodapi mūrkhamukhe kalim ||176||
hiṃsāgavīṃ makhe vīkṣya riraṃsurdhāvati sma saḥ |
sā tu saumyavṛṣāsaktā kharaṃ dūrānnirāsa tam ||177||
maunena vrataniṣṭhānāṃ svākrośaṃ manyate sma saḥ |
vandyavandārubhirjajñau sva śiraśca padāhatam ||178||
ṛṣīṇāṃ sa bṛsīḥ pāṇau paśyannācāmatāmapaḥ |
mene ghanairamī hantuṃ śaptuṃ māmadbhirudyatāḥ ||179||
mauñjīdhṛto dhṛtāṣāḍhānāśaśaṅke sa varṇinaḥ |
rajjvāmī banddhumāyānti hantuṃ daṇḍena māṃ tataḥ ||180||
dṛṣṭvā puraḥ puroḍāśamāsīduttrāsadurmanāḥ |
manvānaḥ phaṇinīstatra sa mumocāsru ca srucaḥ ||181||
mumude madirādānaṃ vidanneṣa dvijanmanaḥ |
dṛṣṭvā sautrāmaṇīmiṣṭiṃ taṃ kurvantamadūyata ||182||
apaśyadyāvato vedavidāṃ brahmāñjalīnasau |
udaḍīyanta tāvantastasyāsrāñjalayo hṛdaḥ ||183||
snātakaṃ ghātakaṃ jajñe jajñau dāntaṃ kṛtāntavat |
vācaṃyamasya dṛṣṭyeva yamasyeva bibhāya saḥ ||184||
sa pākhaṇḍajanānveṣī prāpnuvanvedapaṇḍitān |
jalārthīvānalaṃ prāpya pāpastāpādapāsarat ||185||
tatra brahmahaṇaṃ paśyannatisaṃtoṣāmānaśe |
nirvarṇya sarvamedhasya yajvānaṃ jvarati sma saḥ ||186||
yatihastasthitaistasya rāmbhairārambhi tarjanā |
durjanasyājani kliṣṭirgṛhiṇāṃ vedayaṣṭibhiḥ ||187||
maṇḍalatyāgamevaicchadvīkṣya sthaṇḍilaśāyinaḥ |
pavitrālokanādeṣa pavitrāsamavindata ||188||
apaśyañjinamanviṣyannajinaṃ brahmacāriṇā |
kṣapaṇārthī sadīkṣasya sa cākṣapaṇamaikṣata ||189||
japatāmakṣamālāsu bījākarṣaṇadarśanāt |
sa jīvākṛṣṭikaṣṭāni viparītadṛganvabhūt ||190||
trisaṃdhyaṃ tatra viprāṇāṃ sa paśyannaghamarṣaṇam |
varamaicchaddṛśoreva nijayorapakarṣaṇam ||191||
adrākṣīttatra kiṃcinna kaliḥ paricitaṃ kvacit |
bhaimīnalavyalīkāṇupraśnakāmaḥ paribhraman ||192||
tapaḥ svādhyāyayajñānāmakāṇḍadviṣṭatāpasaḥ |
svavidviṣāṃ śriyaṃ tasminpaśyannupatatāpa saḥ ||193||
kamraṃ tatropanamrāyā viśvasyā vīkṣya tuṣṭavān |
sa mamlau taṃ vibhāvyātha vāmadevyābhyupāsakam ||194||
vairiṇī śucitā tasmai na praveśaṃ dadau bhuvi |
na vedadhvanirālambamambare vitatāra vā ||195||
darśasya darśanātkaṣṭamagniṣṭomasya cānaśe |
jughūrṇe paurṇamāsekṣī somaṃ so'manatāntakam ||196||
tenādṛśyanta vīraghnā na tu vīrahaṇo janāḥ |
nāpaśyatso'bhinirmuktāñjīvanmuktānavaikṣata ||197||
sa tutoṣāśnato viprāndṛṣṭvā spṛṣṭaparasparān |
homaśeṣībhavatsomabhujastānvīkṣya dūnavān ||198||
śrutvā janaṃ rajojuṣṭaṃ tuṣṭiṃ prāpnojjhaṭityasau |
taṃ paśyanpāvanasnānāvasthaṃ duḥsthastato'bhavat ||199||
adhāvatkvāpi gāṃ vīkṣya hanyamānāmayaṃ mudā |
atithibhyastathā buddhvā mando mandaṃ nyavartata ||200||
hṛṣṭavānsa dvijaṃ dṛṣṭvā nityanaimittikatyajam |
yajamānaṃ nirūpyainaṃ dūraṃ dīnamukho'dravat ||201||
ānananda nirīkṣyāyaṃ pure tarātmaghātinam |
sarvasvārasya yajvānamenaṃ dṛṣṭvātha vivyathe ||202||
kratau mahāvrate paśyanbrahmacārītvarīratam |
jajñe yajñakriyāmajñaḥ sa bhaṇḍākāṇḍatāṇḍavam ||203||
yajvabhāryāśvamedhāśvaliṅgāliṅgivarāṅgatām |
dṛṣṭvācaṣṭa sa kartāraṃ śṛuterbhaṇḍamapaṇḍitaḥ ||204||
atha bhīmajayā juṣṭaṃ vyalokata kalirnalam |
duṣṭadṛgbhirdurālokaṃ prabhayeva prabhāprabhum ||205||
tayoḥ sauhārdasāndratvaṃ paśyañśalyamivānaśe |
marmacchedamivānarccha sa tannarmormibhirmithaḥ ||206||
amarṣādātmano doṣāttayostejasvitāguṇāt |
spraṣṭuṃ dṛśāpyanīśastau tasmādapyacalatkaliḥ ||207||
agacchadāśrayānveṣī naladveṣī sa niḥśvasan |
abhirāmaṃ gṛhārāmaṃ tasya rāmasamaśriyaḥ ||208||
rakṣilakṣavṛtatvena bādhanaṃ na tapodhanaiḥ |
mene mānī manāktatra svānukūlaṃ kaliḥ kila ||209||
dalapuṣpaphalairdevadvijapūjābhisaṃdhinā |
sa nalenārjitānprāpa tatra nākramituṃ drumān ||210||
atha sarvodbhidāsattipūraṇāya sa ropitam |
bibhītakaṃ dadarśaikaṃ kuṭaṃ dharme'pyakarmaṭham ||211||
sa taṃ naiṣadhasaudhasya nikaṭaṃ niṣkuṭadhvajam |
bahu mene nijaṃ tasminkalirālambanaṃ vane ||212||
niṣpadasya kalestatra sthānadānādbibhītakam |
kalidrumaḥ paraṃ nāsīdāsītkalpadrumo'pi saḥ ||213||
dadau padena dharmasya sthātumekena yatkaliḥ |
ekaḥ so'pi tadā tasya padaṃ manye'milattataḥ ||214||
udbhidviracitāvāsaḥ kapotādiva tatra saḥ |
rājñaḥ sāgrerdvijādasmātsaṃtāpaṃ prāpa dīkṣitāt ||215||
bibhītakamadhiṣṭhāya tathābhūtena tiṣṭhatā |
tena bhīmabhuvo'bhīkaḥ sa rājarṣiradharṣi na ||216||
tamālambanamāsādya vaidarbhīniṣādheśayoḥ |
kaluṣaṃ kaliranviṣyannavātsīdvatsarānbahūn ||217||
yathāsītkānane tatra vinidrakalikā latā |
tathā nalacchalāsaktivinidrakalikālatā ||218||
doṣaṃ nalasya jijñāsurbabhrāma dvāparaḥ kṣitau |
adoṣaḥ ko'pi lokasya mukhe'stīti durāśayā ||219||
amuṣminnārāme satatanipataddohadatayā
prasūnairunnidrairaniśamamṛtāṃśupratibhaṭe |
asau baddhālambaḥ kalirajani kādambavihagacchadacchāyābhyaṅgocitarucitayā lāñchanamṛgaḥ ||220||
sphāre tādṛśi vairaseninagare puṇyaiḥ prajānāṃ ghanaṃ
vighnaṃ labdhavaaścirādupanatistasminkilābhūtkaleḥ |
etasminpunarantare'ntaramitānandaḥ sa bhaimīnalāvārāddhuṃ vyadhita smaraḥ śrutiśikhāvandārucūḍaṃ dhanuḥ ||221||
śrīharṣaṃ kavirājarājimukuṭālaṃkārahīraḥ sutaṃ
śrīhīraḥ suṣuve jitendriyacayaṃ māmalladevī ca yam |
yātaḥ saptadaśaḥ svasuḥ susadṛśi chindapraśastermahākāvye tadbhuvi naiṣadhīyacarite sargo nisargojjvalaḥ ||222||

 

aṣṭādaśa sargaḥ
so'yamitthamatha bhīmanandinīṃ dārasāramadhigamya naiṣadhaḥ |
tāṃ tṛtīyapuruṣārthavāridheḥ pāralambhanatarīmarīramat ||1||
ātmavitsaha tayā divāniśaṃ bhogabhāgapi na pāpamāpa saḥ |
āhṛtā hi viṣayaikatānatā jñānadhautamanasaṃ na limpati ||2||
nyasya mantriṣu sa rājyamādarādārarādha madanaṃ priyāsakhaḥ |
naikavarṇamaṇikoṭikuṭṭime hemabhūmibhṛti saudhabhūdhare ||3||
vīrasenasutakaṇṭhabhūṣaṇībhūtadivyamaṇipaṅktiśaktibhiḥ |
kāmanopanamadarthatāguṇādyastṛṇīkṛtasuparvaparvataḥ ||4||
dhūpitaṃ yadudarāntaraṃ ciraṃ mecakairagarusāradārubhiḥ |
jālajāladhṛtacandracandanakṣodamedurasamīraśītalam ||5||
kvāpi kāmaśaravṛttavartayo yaṃ mahāsurabhitailadīpikāḥ |
tenire vitimiraṃ smarasphuraddoḥpratāpanikarāṅkuraśriyaḥ ||6||
kuṅkumaiṇamadapaṅkalepitāḥ kṣalitāśca himavālukāmbubhiḥ |
rejuradhvatataśailajasrajo yasya mugdhamaṇikuṭṭimā bhuvaḥ ||7||
naiṣadhāṅgaparimardamedurāmodamāṛdavamanojñavarṇayā |
yadbhuvaḥ kvacana sūnaśayyayābhaji bhālatilakapragalbhatā ||8||
kvāpi yannikaṭaniṣkuṭasphuratkorakaprakarasaurabhormibhiḥ |
sāndramādriyata bhīmanandanānāsikāpuṭakuṭīkuṭumbitā ||9||
ṛddhasarvaṛtuvṛkṣavāṭikākīrakṛttasahakāraśīkaraiḥ |
yajjuṣaḥ sma kulamukhyamāśugaḥ prāṇavātamupadābhirañcati ||10||
kutracitkanakanirmitākhilaḥ kvāpi yo vimalaratnajaḥ kila |
kutracidracitacitraśālikaḥ kvāpi cāsthiravidhaindrajālikaḥ ||11||
citratattadanukāryavibhramādhāyyanekavidharūparūpakam |
vīkṣya yaṃ bahu dhuvañśiro jarāvātakī vidhirakalpi śilpirāṭ ||12||
bhittigarbhagṛhagopitairjanairyaḥ kṛtādbhutakathādikautukaḥ |
sūtrayantrajaviśiṣṭaceṣṭayāścaryasañjibahuśālabhañjikaḥ ||13||
tāmasīṣvapi tamīṣu bhittigai ratnaraśmibhiramandracandrikaḥ |
yastapepi jalayantrapātukāsāradūradhutatāpatandrikaḥ ||14||
yatra puṣpaśaraśāstrakārikāsārikādhyuṣitanāgadantikā |
bhīmajāniṣadhasārvabhaumayoḥ pratyavaikṣata rate kṛtākṛte ||15||
yatra mattakalaviṅkaśīlitāślīlakelipunaruktavattayoḥ |
kvāpi dṛṣṭibhiravāpi vāpikottaṃsahaṃsamithunasmarotsavaḥ ||16||
yatra vaiṇaravavaiṇavasvarairhuṃkṛtairupavanīpikālinām |
kaṅkaṇālikalahaiśca nṛtyatāṃ kubjitaṃ suratakūjitaṃ tayoḥ ||17||
sītkṛtānyaśṛṇutāṃ viśaṅkayoryatpratiṣṭhitaratismarārcayoḥ |
jālakairapavarāntare'pi tau tyājitaiḥ kapaṭakuḍyatāṃ niśi ||18||
kṛṣṇasāramṛgaśṛṅgabhaṅgurā svādurujjvalarasaikasāriṇī |
nāniśaṃ truṭati yanmukhe purā kinnarīvikaṭagītijhaṃkṛtiḥ ||19||
bhitticitralikhitākhilakramā yatra tasthuritihāsasaṃkathāḥ |
padmanandanasutāriraṃsutāmandasāhasahasanmanobhuvaḥ ||20||
puṣpakāṇḍajayaḍiṇḍimāyitaṃ yatragautamakalatrakāminaḥ |
pāradārikavilāsasāhasaṃ devabharturudaṭaṅki bhittiṣu ||21||
uccalatkalaravālikaitavādvaijayantavijayārjitā jagat |
yasya kīrtiravadāyati sma sā kārtikītithiniśīthinīsvasā ||22||
gaurabhānugurugehinīsmarodvṛttabhāvamitivṛttamāśritāḥ |
rejire yadajire'bhinītibhirnāṭikā bharatabhāratīsudhā ||23||
śaṃbhudāruvanasaṃbhujikriyāmādhavavrajavadhūvilāsayoḥ |
gumphitairuśanasā subhāṣitairyasya hāṭakaviṭaṅkamaṅkitam ||24||
ahni bhānubhuvi dāśadārikāṃ yaccaraḥ paricarantamujjagau |
kāladeśaviṣāyāsahātsmarādutsukaṃ śukapitāmahaṃ śukaḥ ||25||
nītameva karalabhyapāratāmapratīrya munayastaporṇavam |
apsaraḥkucaghaṭāvalambanātsthāyinā kvacana yatra citragāḥ ||26||
svāminā ca vahatā ca taṃ mayā sa smaraḥ suratavarjanājjitaḥ |
yo'yamīdṛgiti nṛtyate sma yatkekinā murajanisvanairghanaiḥ ||27||
yatra vīkṣya nalabhīmasaṃ bhave mahyato ratiratīśayorapi |
spardhayeva jayatorjayāya te kāmakāmaramaṇībabhūvatuḥ ||28||
tatra saudhasurabhūdhare tayorāvirāsuratha kāmakelayaḥ |
ye mahākavibhirapyavīkṣitāḥ pāṃsulābhirapi ye na śikṣitāḥ ||29||
pauruṣaṃ dadhati yoṣitā nale svāmini śritatadīyabhāvayā |
yūni śaiśavamatīrṇayā kiyatprāpi bhīmasutayā na sādhvasam ||30||
dūtyasaṃgatigataṃ yadātmanaḥ prāgaśiśravadiyaṃ priyaṃ giraḥ |
taṃ vicintya vinayavyayaṃ hriyā na sma veda karavāṇi kīdṛśam ||31||
yattayā sadasi naiṣādhaḥ svayaṃ prāgvṛtaḥ sapadi vītalajjayā |
tannijaṃ manasikṛtya cāpalaṃ sā śaśāka na vilokituṃ nalam ||32||
āsane maṇimarīcimāṃsale yāṃ diśaṃ sa parirabhya tasthivān |
tāmasūyitavatīva māninī na vyalokayadiyaṃ manāgapi ||33||
hrīsarannijanimajjanocitaṃ maulidūranamanaṃ dadhānayā |
dvāri citrayuvatiśriyā tayā bhartṛhūtiśatamaśrutīkṛtam ||34||
veśma patyuraviśanna sādhvasādveśitāpi śayanaṃ na sā'bhajat |
bhājitāpi savidhaṃ na sāsvapatsvāpitāpi na ca saṃmukhābhavat ||35||
kevalaṃ na khalu bhīmanandinī dūramatrapata naiṣadhaṃ prati |
bhīmajāhṛdi jitaḥ striyā hriyā manmatho'pi niyataṃ sa lajjitaḥ ||36||
ātmanāpi haradārasundarī yatkimapyabhilalāṣa ceṣṭitum |
svāminā yadi tadarthamarthitā mudritastadanayā tadudyamaḥ ||37||
hrībharādvimukhayā tayā bhiyaṃ sañjitāmananurāgaśaṅkini |
sa svacetasi lulopa saṃsmarandūtyakālakalitaṃ tadāśayam ||38||
pārśvamāgami nijaṃ sahālibhistena pūrvamatha sā tayaikayā |
kvāpi tāmapi niyujya māyinā svātmamātrasacivāvaśeṣitā ||39||
saṃnidhāvapi nije niveśitāmālibhiḥ kusumaśastraśāstravit |
ānayadvyavadhimāniva priyāmaṅkapālivalayena saṃnidhim ||40||
prāgacumbadalike hriyānatāṃ tāṃ kramāddaranatāṃ kapolayoḥ |
tena viśvasitamānasāṃ jhaṭityānane sa paricumbya siṣmiye ||41||
lajjayā prathamametya huṃkṛtaḥ sādhvasena balinātha tarjitaḥ |
kiṃciducchvasita eva taddhṛdi nyagbabhūva punararbhakaḥ smaraḥ ||42||
vallabhasya bhujayoḥ smarotsave ditsatoḥ prasabhamaṅkapālikām |
ekakaściramarodhi bālayā talpayantraṇanirantarālayā ||43||
hāracārimavilokane mṛṣākautukaṃ kimapi nāṭayannayam |
kaṇṭhamūlamadasīyamaspṛśatpāṇinopakucadhāvinā dhavaḥ ||44||
yattvayāsmi sadasi srajāñcitastanmayāpi bhavadarhaṇārhati |
ityudīrya nijahāramarpayannaspṛśatsa tadurojakorakau ||45||
nīvisīmni nihitaṃ sa nidrayā subhruvo niśi niṣiddhasaṃvidaḥ |
kampitaṃ śayamapāsa yannayaṃ dolanairjanitabodhayā'nayā ||46||
sa priyoruyugakañcukāṃśuke nyasya dṛṣṭimatha siṣmiye nṛpaḥ |
āvavāra tadathāmbarāñcalaiḥ sā nirāvṛtiriva trapāvṛtā ||47||
buddhimānvyadhita tāṃ kramādayaṃ kiṃciditthamapanītasādhvasām |
kiṃca tanmanasi cittajanmanā hrīranāmi dhanuṣā samaṃ manāk ||48||
siṣmiye hasati na sma tena sā prīṇitāpi parihāsabhāṣaṇaiḥ |
sve hi darśayati te pareṇa kānardhyadantakuruvindamālike ||49||
vīkṣya bhīmatanayāstanadvayaṃ magnahāramaṇimudrayāṅkitam |
soḍhakāntaparirambhagāḍhatā sānvamāyi sumukhī sakhījanaiḥ ||50||
yācate sma paridhāpikāḥ sakhīḥ sā svanīvinibiḍakriyāṃ yadā |
anvaminvata tadā vihasya tā vṛttamatra patipāṇicāpalam ||51||
kurvatī niculitaṃ hriyā kiyatsauhṛdādvivṛtasaurabhaṃ kiyat |
kuḍnalonmiṣitasūnasevinīṃ padminīṃ jayati sā sma padminī ||52||
nāvilokya nalamāsituṃ smaro hrīrna vīkṣitumadatta subhruvaḥ |
taddṛśaḥ patidiśācalannatha vrīḍitāḥ samakucanmuhuḥ pathaḥ ||53||
nā'nayā patiranāyi netrayorlakṣyatāmapi parokṣatāmapi |
vīkṣyate sa khalu yadvilokane tatra tatra nayate dadānayā ||54||
vāsare virahaniḥsahā niśāṃ kāntasaṅgasamayaṃ samaihata |
sā hriyā niśi punardinodayaṃ vāñchatisma patikelilajjitā ||55||
tatkaromi paramabhyupaiṣi yanmā hriyaṃ vraja bhiyaṃ parityaja |
ālivarga iva te'hamityamūṃ śaśvadāśvasanamūcivānnalaḥ ||56||
yena tanmadanavahninā sthitaṃ hrīmahauṣadhiniruddhaśaktinā |
siddhimadbhirudateji taiḥ punaḥ sa priyapriyavacobhimantraṇaiḥ ||57||
yadvidhūya dayitārpitaṃ karaṃ dordvayena pidadhe kucau dṛḍham |
pārśvagaṃ priyamapāsya sā hriyā taṃ hṛdisthitamivālilaṅga tat ||58||
anyadasmi bhavatīṃ na yācitā vāramekamadharaṃ dhayāmi te |
ityasisvadadupāṃśukākuvāksopamardahaṭhavṛttireva tam ||59||
pītatāvakamukhāsavo'dhunā bhṛtya eṣa nijakṛtyamarhati |
tatkaromi bhavadūrumityasau tatra saṃnyadhita pāṇipallavam ||60||
cumbanādiṣu babhūva nāma kiṃ tadvṛthā bhiyamihāpi mā kṛthāḥ |
ityudīrya rasanāvalivyayaṃ nirmame mṛgadṛśo'yamādimam ||61||
astivāmyabharamastikautukaṃ sāstigharmajalamastivepathu |
astibhīti ratamastivāñchitaṃ prāpadastisukhamastipīḍanam ||62||
hrīstaveyamucitaiva yannavastāvake manasi matsamāgamaḥ |
tattu nistrapamajasrasaṃgamādvīḍamāvahati māmakaṃ manaḥ ||63||
ityupālabhata saṃbhujikriyārambhavighnaghanalajjitairjitām |
tāṃ tathā sa caturo'tha sā yathā traptumeva tamanu trapāmayāt ||64||
bāhuvaktrajaghanastanāṅghritadbandhagandharatasaṃgatānatīḥ |
icchurutsukajane dinesmite vīkṣite ti samaketi tena sā ||65||
prātarātmaśayanādviniryatīṃ saṃnirudhya yadasādhyamanyadā |
tanmukhārpaṇamukhaṃ sukhaṃ bhuvo jambhajitkṣitiśacīmacīkarat ||66||
nāyakasya śayanādaharmukhe nirgatā mudamudīkṣya subhruvām |
ātmanā nijanavasmarotsavasmāriṇīyamahṛṇīyata svayam ||67||
tāṃ mitho'bhidadhatīṃ sakhīṃ priyasyātmanaśca sa niśāviceṣṭitam |
pārśvagaḥ suravarātpidhāṃ dadhaddṛśyatāṃ śṛutakatho hasangataḥ ||68||
cakradāravirahekṣaṇakṣaṇe bibhyatī dhavahasāya sābhavat |
kvāpi vastuni vadatyanāgataṃ cittamudyadanimittavaikṛtam ||69||
cumbitaṃ na mukhamācakarṣa yatpatyurantaramṛtaṃ vavarṣa tat |
sā nunoda na bhujaṃ tadarpitaṃ tena tasya kimabhūnna tarpitam ||70||
nītayoḥ stanapidhānatāṃ tayā dātumāpa bhujayoḥ karaṃ paraṃ |
vītabāhuni tato hṛdaṃśuke kevale'pyatha sa tatkucadvaye ||71||
yācanānna dadatīṃ nakhakṣataṃ tāṃ vidhāya kathayā'nyacetasam |
vakṣasi nyasitumāttatatkaraḥ svaṃ vibhidya mumude sa tannakhaiḥ ||72||
sa prasahya hṛdayāpavārakaṃ hartumakṣamata subhruvo bahiḥ |
hrīmayaṃ tu na tadīyamāntaraṃ tadvinetumabhavatprabhuḥ prabhuḥ ||73||
sā smareṇa balinā'pyahāpitā hrīkṣame bhṛśamaśobhatābalā |
bhāti cāpi vasanaṃ vinā natu vrīḍadhairyaparivarjanairjanaḥ ||74||
āttha neti ratayācinaṃ na yanmāmato'numatavatyasi sphuṭam |
ityamuṃ tadabhilāpanotsukaṃ dhūnitena śirasā nirāsa sā ||75||
yā śirovidhutirāha neti te sā mayā na kimiyaṃ samākali |
tanniṣedhasamasaṃkhyatā vidhiṃ vyaktameva tava vakti vāñchitam ||76||
nāttha nāttha śṛṇvāni te na kiṃ tena vācamiti tāṃ nigadya saḥ |
sā sma dūtyagatamāha taṃ yathā tajjagāda mṛdubhistaduktibhiḥ ||77||
nīvisīmni nibiḍaṃ purā'ruṇatpāṇinā'tha śithilena tatkaram |
sā krameṇa nananeti vādinī vighnamācaradamuṣya kevalam ||78||
rūpaveṣavasanāṅgavāsanābhūṣaṇādiṣu pṛthagvidagdhatām |
sānyadivyayuvatibhramakṣamāṃ nityametya tamagānnavā navā ||79||
iṅgitena nijarāganīradhiṃ saṃvibhāvya caṭubhirguṇajñatām |
bhaktatāṃ ca paricaryayā'niśaṃ sādhikādhikavaśaṃ vyadhatta tam ||80||
svāṅgamarpayitumetya vāmatāṃ roṣitaṃ priyamathānunīya sā |
ātadīyahaṭhasaṃbubhukṣutāṃ nānvamanyata punastamarthinam ||81||
ādyasaṃgamasamādarāṇyadhādvallabhāya dadatī kathaṃcana |
aṅgakāni ghanamānavāmatāvrīḍalambhitadurāpatāni sā ||82||
patyurāgiriśamātaru kramātsvasya cāgirijamālataṃ vapuḥ |
tasya cārhamakhilaṃ pativratā krīḍati sma tapsā vidhāya sā ||83||
na sthalī na jaladhirna kānanaṃ nādribhūrna viṣāyo na viṣṭapam |
krīḍitā na saha yatra tena sā sā vidhaiva na yayā yayā na vā ||84||
namrayāṃśukavikarṣiṇi priye vaktravātahatadīptadīpayā |
bhartṛmaulimaṇidīpitāstayā vismayena kakubho nibhālitāḥ ||85||
kāntamūrdhni dadatī pidhitsayā tanmaṇeḥ śravaṇapūramutpalam |
rantumarcanamivācaratpuraḥ sā svavallabhatanormanobhuvaḥ ||86||
taṃ pidhāya muditātha pārśvayorvīkṣya dīpamubhayatra sā svayoḥ |
cittamāpa kutukādbhutatrapātaṅkasaṃkaṭaniveśitasmaram ||87||
ekakasya śamane paraṃ punarjāgrataṃ śamitamapyavekṣya tam |
jātavahnivarasaṃsmṛtiḥ śiraḥ sā vidhūya nimimīla kevalam ||88||
paśya bhīru na mayāpi dṛśyase yannimīlitavatī dṛśāvasi |
ityanena parihasya sā tamaḥ saṃvidhāya samabhoji lajjitā ||89||
cumbyase'yamayamaṅkyase nakhaiḥ śliṣyaseyamayamarpyase hṛdi |
no punarna karavāṇi te giraṃ huṃ tyaja tyaja tavāsmi kiṃkarā ||90||
ityalīkaratakātarā priyaṃ vipralabhya surate hriyaṃ ca sā |
cumbanādi vitatāra māyinī kiṃ vidagdhamanasāmagocaraḥ ||91||
svepsitodgamitamātraluptayā dīpikācapalayā tamoghane |
nirviśaṅkaratajanmatanmukhākūtadarśanasukhānyabhuṅkta saḥ ||92||
yadbhruvau kuṭilite tayā rate manmathena tadanāmi kārmukam |
yattu huṃhumiti sā tadā vyadhāttatsmarasya śaramuktihuṃkṛtam ||93||
īkṣitopadiśatīva nartituṃ tatkṣaṇoditamudaṃ manobhuvam |
kāntadantaparipīḍitādharā pāṇidhūnanamiyaṃ vitanvatī ||94||
sā śaśāka parirambhadāyinī gāhituṃ bṛhaduraḥ priyasya na |
cakṣame ca sa na bhaṅgurabhruvastuṅgapīnakucadūratāṃ gatam ||95||
bāhuvalliparirambhamaṇḍalī yā parasparamapīḍayattayoḥ |
āsta hemanalinīmṛṇālajaḥ pāśa eva hṛdayeśayasya saḥ ||96||
vallabhena parirambhapīḍitau preyasīhṛdi kucāvavāpatuḥ |
kelatīmadanayorupāśraye tatra vṛttamilitopadhānatām ||97||
bhīmajoruyugalaṃ nalāṛpitaiḥ pāṇijasya mṛdubhiḥ padairbabhau |
tatpraśasti ratikāmayorjayastambhayugmamiva śātakumbhajam ||98||
bahvamāni vidhināpi tāvakaṃ nābhimūruyugamantarāṅgakam |
sa vyadhādadhikavarṇakairidaṃ kāñcanairyaditi tāṃ purāha saḥ ||99||
pīḍanaya mṛdunī vigāhya tau kāntapāṇinaline spṛhāvatī |
tatkucau kalaśapīnaniṣthurau hārahāsavihate vitenatuḥ ||100||
yau kuraṅgamadakuṅkumāñcitau nīlalohitarucau badhūkucau |
sa priyorasi tayoḥ svayaṃbhuvorācacāra nakhakiṃśukārcanam ||101||
ambudheḥ kiyadanutthitaṃ vidhuṃ svānubimbamilitaṃ vyaḍambayat |
cumbadambujamukhīmukhaṃ tadā naiṣadhasya vadanendumaṇḍalam ||102||
pūgabhāgabahutākaṣāyitairvāsitairudayabhāskareṇa tau |
cakraturnidhuvane'dharāmṛtaistatra sādhumadhupānavibhramam ||103||
āha nāthavadanasya cumbataḥ sā sma śītakaratāmanakṣaram |
sītkṛtāni sudatī vitanvatī sattvadattapṛthuvepathustadā ||104||
cumbanāya kalitapriyākucaṃ vīrasenasutavaktramaṇḍalam |
prāpa bhartumamṛtaiḥ sudhāṃśunā saktahāṭakaghaṭena mittratām ||105||
vīkṣya vīkṣya punaraikṣi sā mudā paryarambhi parirabhya cāsakṛt |
cumbitā punaracumbi cādarāttṛptirāpi na kathaṃcanāpi ca ||106||
chinnamapyatanu hāramaṇḍalaṃ mugdhayā suratalāsyakelibhiḥ |
na vyatarki sudṛśā cirādapi svedabindukitavakṣasā hṛdi ||107||
yattadīyahṛdi hāramauktikairāsi tatra guṇa eva kāraṇam |
anyathā kathamamutra vartituṃ tairaśāki na tadā guṇacyutaiḥ ||108||
ekavṛttirapi mauktikāvaliśchinnahāravitatau tadā tayoḥ |
chāyayā'nyahṛdaye vibhūṣaṇaṃ śrāntivāribharabhāvite'bhavat ||109||
vāmapādatalaluptamanmathaśrīmadena mukhavīkṣiṇāniśam |
bhujyamānanavayauvanāmunā pārasīmani cacāra sā mudām ||110||
āntarānapi tadaṅgasaṃgamaistarpitānavayavānamanyata |
netrayoramṛtasārapāraṇāṃ tadvilokanamacintayannalaḥ ||111||
bhūṣaṇairatuṣadāśritaiḥ priyāṃ prāgatha vyaṣadadeṣa bhāvayan |
tairabhāvi kiyadaṅgadarśane yatpidhānamayavighnakāribhiḥ ||112||
yojanāni parirambhaṇe'ntaraṃ romaharṣajamapi sma bodhataḥ |
tau nimeṣamapi vīkṣaṇe mitho vatsaravyavadhimadhyagacchatām ||113||
vīkṣya bhāvamadhigantumutsukāṃ pūrvamacchamaṇikuṭṭime mṛdum |
ko'yamityuditasaṃbhramīkṛtāṃ svānubimbamadadarśataiṣa tām ||114||
tatkṣaṇāvahitabhāvabhāvitadvādaśātmasitadīdhitisthitiḥ |
svāṃ priyāmabhimatakṣaṇodayāṃ bhāvalābhalaghutāṃ nunoda saḥ ||115||
svena bhāvajanane sa tu priyāṃ bāhumūlakucanābhicumbanaiḥ |
nirmame ratarahaḥsamāpanāśarmasārasamasaṃvibhāginīm ||116||
viślathairavayavairnimīlayā lomabhirdrutamitairvinidratām |
sūcitaṃ śvasitasītkṛtaiśca tau bhāvamakramakamadhyagacchatām ||117||
āsta bhāvamadhigacchatostayoḥ saṃmadeṣu karajakṣatārpaṇā |
phāṇiteṣu maricāvacūrṇanā sā sphuṭaṃ kaṭurapi spṛhāvahā ||118||
ardhamīlitavilolatārake sā dṛśau nidhuvanaklamālasā |
yanmuhūrtamavahanna tatpunastṛptirāsta dayitasya paśyataḥ ||119||
tatklamastamadidīkṣata kṣaṇaṃ tālavṛntacalanāya nāyakam |
tadvidhā hi bhavadaivataṃ priyā vedhaso'pi vidadhāi cāpalam ||120||
svedabindukitanāsikāśikhaṃ tanmukhaṃ sukhayati sma naiṣadham |
proṣitādharaśayāluyāvakaṃ sāmiluptapulakaṃ kapolayoḥ ||121||
hrīṇameva pṛthu sasmaraṃ kiyatklāntameva bahunirvṛtaṃ manāk |
kāntacetasi tadīyamānanaṃ tattadālabhata lakṣamādarāt ||122||
svedavāriparipūritaṃ priyāromakūpanivahaṃ yathāyathā |
naiṣadhasya dṛgapāttathātathā citramāpadapatṛṣṇatāṃ na sā ||123||
vītamālyakacahastasaṃyamavyastahastayugayā sphuṭīkṛtam |
bāhumūlamanayā tadujjvalaṃ vīkṣña saukhyajaladhau mamajja saḥ ||124||
vīkṣya patyuradharaṃ kṛśodarī bandhujīvamiva bhṛṅgasaṃgatam |
mañjulaṃ nayanakajjalairnijaiḥ saṃvarītumaśakatsmitaṃ na sā ||125||
tāṃ vilokya vimukhaśritasmitāṃ pṛcchato hasitahetumīśituḥ |
hrīmatī vyataraduttaraṃ vadhūḥ pāṇipaṅkaruhi darpaṇārpaṇām ||126||
lākṣayātmacaraṇasya cumbanāccārubhālamavalokya tanmukham |
sā hriyā natanatānanā'smaraccheṣarāgamuditaṃ patiṃ niśaḥ ||127||
svedabhāji hṛdaye'nubimbitaṃ vīkṣya mūrtamiva hṛdgataṃ priyam |
nirmame dhutarataśramaṃ nijairhrīnatātimṛdunāsikānilaiḥ ||128||
sūnanāyakanideśavibhramairapratītacaravedanodayam |
dandataṃśamadhare'dhigāmukā sāspṛśanmṛdu camaccakāra ca ||129||
vīkṣya vīkṣya karajasya vibhramaṃ preyasārjitamurojayoriyam |
kāntamaikṣata hasaspṛśaṃ kiyatkopakuñcitavilocanāñcalā ||130||
roṣarūṣitamukhīmiva priyāṃ vīkṣya bhītidarakampitākṣarām |
tāṃ jagāda sa na vedmi tanvi taṃ kaścakāra tava koparopaṇām ||131||
roṣakuṅkumavilepanānmanāṅganvavāci kṛśatanvavāci te |
bhūdayuktasamayaiva rañjanāmānane vidhubidheyamānane ||132||
kṣipramasya tu rujā nakhādijāstāvakīramṛtasīkaraṃ kirat |
etadarthamidamarthitaṃ mayā kaṇṭhacumbi maṇidāma kāmadam ||133||
svāparādhamalupatpayodhare matkaraḥ suradhanuṣkarastava |
sevayā vyajanacālanābhuvā bhūya eva caraṇau karotu vā ||134||
ānanasya mama cedanaucitī nirdayaṃ daśanadaṃśadāyinaḥ |
śodhyate sudati vairamasya tatkiṃ tvayā vada vidaśya nādharam ||135||
dīpalopamaphalaṃ vyadhatta yastvatpaṭāhṛtiṣu macchikhāmaṇiḥ |
no tadāgasi paraṃ samarthanā so'yamastu padapātukastava ||136||
itthamuktimupahṛtya komalāṃ talpacumbicikuraścakāra saḥ |
ātmamaulimaṇikāntibhaṅginīṃ tatpadāruṇasarojasaṅginīm ||137||
tatpadākhilanakhānubimbanaiḥ svaiḥ sametya samatāmiyāya saḥ |
rudrabhūmavijigīṣayā ratisvāminopadaśamūrtitābhṛtā ||138||
ākhyataiṣa kuru kopalopanaṃ paśya naśyati kṛśā madharniśā |
etameva tu niśāntare varaṃ roṣaśeṣamanurotsyasi kṣaṇam ||139||
sātha nāthamanayatkṛtārthatāṃ pāṇigopitanijāṅghripaṅkajā |
tatpraṇāmadhutamānamānanaṃ smerameva sudatī vitanvatī ||140||
tau mitho ratirasāyanātpunaḥ saṃbubhukṣumanasau babhūvatuḥ |
cakṣame natu tayormanorathaṃ durjanī rajaniralpajīvanā ||141||
svapnumāptaśayanīyayostayoḥ svairamākhyata vacaḥ priyāṃ priyaḥ |
utsavairadharadānapānajaiḥ sāntarāyapadamantarāntarā ||142||
devadūtyamupagamya nirdayaṃ dharmabhītikṛtatādṛśāgasaḥ |
astu seyamaparādhamārjanā jīvitāvadhi nalasya vaśyatā ||143||
sa kṣāṇaḥ sumukhi yattvadīkṣaṇaṃ taccarājyamuru yena rajyasi |
tannalasya sudhayābhiṣecanaṃ yattvadaṅgaparirambhavibhramaḥ ||144||
śarma kiṃ hṛdi hareḥ priyārpaṇaṃ kiṃ śivārdhaghaṭane śivasya vā |
kāmaye tava maheṣu tanvi taṃ nanvayaṃ saridudanvadanvayam ||145||
dhīyatāṃ mayi dṛḍhā mameti dhīrvaktumevamavakāśa eva kaḥ |
yadvidhūya tṛṇavaddivaspatiṃ krītavatyasi dayāpaṇena mām ||146||
śṛṇvatā nibhṛtamālibhirbhavadvāgvilāsamasakṛnmayā kila |
mogharāghavavivarjyajānakīśrāviṇī bhayacalāsi vīkṣitā ||147||
chuptapatravinimīlitātkṣupātkacchapasya dhṛtacāpalātpalāt |
tvatsakhīṣu saraṭācchirodhutaḥ svaṃ bhiyo'bhidadhatīṣu vaibhavam ||148||
tvaṃ madīyavirahānmayā nijāṃ bhītimīritavatī rahaḥśrutā |
nojjhitāsmi bhavatīṃ tadityayaṃ vyāharadvaramasatyakātaraḥ ||149||
saṃgamayya virahe'smi jīvikā yaiva vāmatha ratāya tatkṣaṇam |
hanta dattha iti ruṣṭayāvayornidrayā'dya kimu nopasadyate ||150||
īdṛśaṃ nigadati priye dṛśaṃ saṃmadātkiyadiyaṃ nyamīlayat |
prātarālapati kokile kalaṃ jāgarādiva niśaḥ kumudvatī ||151||
miśritoru militādharaṃ mithaḥ svapnavīkṣitaparasparakriyam |
tau tato'nu parirambhasaṃpuṭaiḥ pīḍanāṃ vidadhatau nidadratuḥ ||152||
tadyātāyātaraṃhacchalakalitarataśrāntiniśvāsadhārājasravyāmiśrabhāvasphuṭakathitamithaḥprāṇabhedavyudāsam |
bālāvakṣojapatrāṅkurakarimakarīṃ mudritorvīndravakṣaścihnākhyātaikabhāvobhayahṛdayamayāddvandvamānandanidrām ||153||
śrīharṣaṃ kavirājarājimukuṭālaṃkārahīraḥ sutaṃ
śrīhīraḥ suṣuve jitendriyacayaṃ māmalladevī ca yam |
yāto'smiñśivaśaktisiddhibhaginīsaubhrātrabhavye mahākāvye tasya kṛtau nalīyacarite sargo'yamaṣṭādaśaḥ ||154||

 

ekonaviṃśatiḥ sargaḥ
niśi daśamitāmāliṅgantyāṃ vibodhavidhitsubhirniṣadhavasudhāmīnāṅkasya priyāṅkamupeduṣaḥ |
śrutimadhupadasragvaidagdhīvibhāvitabhāvikasphuṭarasabhṛśābhyaktā vaitālikairjagire giraḥ ||1||
jaya jaya mahārāja prābhātikīṃ suṣamāmimāṃ saphalayata māṃ dānāḍakṣṇordarālasapakṣmaṇī |
prathamaśakunaṃ śayyotthāyaṃ tavāstu vidarbhajā priyajanamukhāmbhojāttuṅgaṃ yadaṅga na maṅgalam ||2||
varuṇagṛhiṇīmāśāmāsādayantamamuṃ rucīnicayasicayāṃśāṃśabhraṃśakrameṇa niraṃśukam |
tuhinamahasaṃ paśyantīva prasādamiṣādasau nijamukhamitaḥ smeraṃ dhatte harermahiṣīharit ||3||
amahatitarāstādṛktārā na locanagocarāstaraṇikiraṇā dyāmañcanti kramādaparasparāḥ |
kathayati pariśrātiṃ rātrī tamassahayudhvanāmayamapi daridrāṇaprāṇastamīdayitastviṣām ||4||
sphurati timirastomaḥ paṅkaprapañca ivoccakaiḥ purusitagaruccañcaccañcaccañcūpuṭasphuṭacumbitaḥ |
api madhukarī kāliṃmanyā virājati dhūmalacchaviriva raverlākṣālakṣmīṃ karairatipātukaiḥ ||5||
rajanivamathuprāleyāmbhaḥkaṇakramasaṃbhṛtaiḥ kuśakisalayasyācchairagreśayairudabindubhiḥ |
suṣirakuśalenāyaḥsūcīśikhāṅkurasaṃkaraṃ kimapi gamitānyantarmuktāphalānyavamenire ||6||
raviruciṛcāmokāreṣu sphuṭāmalabindutāṃ gamayitumamūruccīyante vihāyasi tārakāḥ |
svaraviracanāyāsāmuccairudāttatayā hṛtāḥ śiśiramahaso bimbādasmādasaṃśayamaṃśavaḥ ||7||
vrajati kumude dṛṣṭvā mohaṃ dṛśorapidhāyake bhavati ca nale dūraṃ tārāpatau ca hataujasi |
laghu raghupaterjāyāṃ māyāmayīmiva rāvaṇistimiracikuragrāhaṃ rātriṃ hinasti gabhastirāṭ ||8||
tridaśamithunakrīḍātalpe vihāyasi gāhate nidhuvanadhutasragbhāgaśrībharaṃ grahasaṃgrahaḥ |
mṛdutarakarākāraistūlotkarairudaraṃbhariḥ pariharati nākhaṇḍo gaṇḍopadhānavidhāṃ vidhuḥ ||9||
daśaśatacaturvedīśākhāvivartanamūrtayaḥ savidhamadhunā'laṃkurvanti dhruvaṃ raviraśmayaḥ |
vadanakuhare'pyadhyetṝṇāmayaṃ tadudañcati śrutipadabhayasteṣāmeva pratidhvaniradhvani ||10||
nayati bhagavānambhojasyā'nibandhanabāndhavaḥ kimapi maghavaprāsādasya praghāṇamupaghnatām |
apasaradaridhvāntapratyagviyatpathamaṇḍalīlaganaphaladaśrāntasvarṇācalabhramavibhramaḥ ||11||
nabhasi mahasāṃ dhvāntadhvāṅkṣapramāpaṇapattriṇāmiha viharaṇaiḥ śyainaṃpātāṃ raveravadhārayan |
śaśaviśasanatrāsādāśāmayāccaramāṃ śaśī tadadhigamanāttārāpārāpatairudaḍīyata ||12||
bhṛśamabibharustārā hārāccyutā iva mauktikāḥ surasuratajakrīḍālūnāddyusadviyadaṅgaṇam |
bahukarakṛtātprātaḥsaṃmārjanādadhunā punarnirupadhinijāvasthālakṣmīvilakṣaṇamīkṣyate ||13||
prathamamupahṛtyārghaṃ tārairakhanḍitataṇḍulaistimirapariṣaddūrvāparvāvalīśabalīkṛtaiḥ |
atha ravirucāṃ grāsātithyaṃ nabhaḥ svavihāribhiḥ sṛjati śiśirakṣodaśreṇīmayairudasaktubhiḥ ||14||
asurahitamapyādityotthāṃ vipattimupāgataṃ ditisutaguruḥ prāṇairyoktuṃ na kiṃ kacavattamaḥ |
paṭhati luṭhatīṃ kaṇṭhe vidyāmayaṃ mṛtajīvanīṃ yadi na vahate saṃdhyāmaunavratavyayabhīrutām ||15||
udayaśikhariprasthānyahnā raṇe'tra niśaḥ kṣaṇe dadhati viharatpūṣāṇyūṣmadrutāśmajatusravān |
udayadaruṇaprahvībhāvādarādaruṇānuje milati kimu tatsaṅgācchaṅkyā naveṣṭakaveṣṭanā ||16||
ravirathahayānaśvasyanti dhruvaṃ vaḍavā balapratibalabalāvasthāyinyaḥ samīkṣya samīpagān |
nijaparivṛḍhaṃ gāḍhapremā rathāṅgavihaṃgamī smaraśaraparādhīnasvāntā vṛṣasyati saṃprati ||17||
niśi niraśanāḥ kṣīrasyantaḥ kṣudhā|śvakiśorakā madhuramadhuraṃ heṣante te vilolitavāladhi |
turagasamajaḥ sthānotthāyaṃ kvaṇanmaṇimanthabhūdharabhavaśilālehāyehācaṇo lavaṇasyati ||18||
uḍupariṣadaḥ kiṃ nārhatvaṃ niśaḥ kimu naucitī patiriha na yaddṛṣṭastābhyāṃ gaṇeyarucīgaṇaḥ |
sphuṭamuḍupaterāśmaṃ vakṣaḥ sphuranmalināśmanaśchavi yadanayorvicchede'pi drutaṃ bata na drutam ||19||
aruṇakiraṇe vahnau lājānudūni juhoti yā pariṇayati tāṃ saṃdhyāmetāmavaimi maṇirdivaḥ |
iyamiva sa evāgnibhrāntiṃ karoti purā yataḥ karamapi na kastasyaivotkaḥ sakautukamīkṣitum ||20||
ratiratipatidvaitaśrīkau dhuraṃ bibhṛmastarāṃ priyavacasi yannagnācāryā vadāmatamāṃ tataḥ |
api viracito vidmaḥ puṇyadruhaḥ khalu narmaṇaḥ paruṣamaruṣe naikasyai vāmudeti mude'pi tat ||21||
bhava laghuyutākāntaḥ saṃdhyāmupāssva tapomala tvarayati kathaṃ saṃdhyeyaṃ tvāṃ na nāma niśānujā |
dyutipatirathāvaśyaṃkārī dinodayamāsitā haripatiharitpūrṇabhrūṇāyitā kiyataḥ kṣanān ||22||
muṣitamanasaścitraṃ bhaimi tvayādya kalāgṛhairniṣādhavasudhānāthasyāpi ślathaślathatā vidhau |
ajagaṇadayaṃ saṃdhyāṃ vandhyāṃ vidhāya na dūṣaṇaṃ namasitumanā yannāma syānna saṃprati pūṣaṇam ||23||
na viduṣitarā kāpi tvatastato niyatakriyāpatanadurite heturbharturmanasvini mā sma bhūḥ |
aniśabhavadatyāgādenaṃ janaḥ khalu kāmukīsubhagamabhidhāsyatyuddāmā parāṅkavadāvadaḥ ||24||
raha sahacarīmetāṃ rājannapi stritamāṃ kṣāṇaṃ taraṇikiraṇaiḥ stokānmuktaiḥ samālabhate nabhaḥ |
udadhinirayadbhāsvatsvarṇodakumbhadidṛkṣutāṃ dadhati nalinaṃ prasthāyinyaḥ śriyaḥ kumudānmudā ||25||
prathamakakubhaḥ pānthatvena sphuṭekṣitavṛtrahāṇyanupadamiha drakṣyanti tvāṃ mahāṃsi mahaspateḥ |
paṭimavahanādūhāpohakṣamāṇi vitanvatāmahaha yuvayostāvallakṣmīvivecanacāturīm ||26||
anatiśithile puṃbhāvena pragalbhabalāḥ khalu prasabhamalayaḥ pāthojāsye niviśya niritvarāḥ |
kimapi mukhataḥkṛtvānītaṃ vitīrya sarojnīmadhurasamuṣoyoge jāyāṃ navānnamacīkaran ||27||
mihirakiraṇābhogaṃ bhoktuṃ pravṛttatayā puraḥ kalitaculukāpośānasya grahārthamiyaṃ kimu |
iti vikasitenaikena prāgdalena sarojinī janayati matiṃ sākṣātkarturjanasya dinodaye ||28||
taṭatarukhagaśreṇīsāṃrāviṇairiva sāmprataṃ sarasi vigalannidrāmudrājaniṣṭa sarojinī |
adharasudhayā madhye madhye vadhūmukhalabdhayā dhayati madhupaḥ svāduṃkāraṃ madhūni saroruhām ||29||
gatacaradinasyāyurbhraṃśe dayodayasaṃkucatkamalamukulakroḍānnīḍapraveśamupeyuṣām |
iha madhulihāṃ bhinneṣvambhoruheṣu samāyatāṃ saha sahacarairālokyante'dhunā madhupāraṇāḥ ||30||
timiravirahātpāṇḍūyante diśaḥ kṛśatārakāḥ kamalahasitaiḥ śyenīvonnīyate sarasī na kā |
śaraṇamilitadhvāntadhvaṃsiprabhādaradhāraṇādgaganaśikharaṃ nīlatyekaṃ nijairayaśobharaiḥ ||31||
sarasijavanānyudyatpakṣāryamāṇi hasantu na kṣatarucisuhṛccandraṃ tandrāmupaitu na kairavam |
himagiridṛṣaddāyādaśri pratītamadaḥ smitaṃ kumudavipinasyātho pāthoruhairnijanidrayā ||32||
dhayatu naline mādhvīkaṃ vā na vābhinavāgataḥ kumudamakarandaughaiḥ kukṣiṃbharirbhramarotkaraḥ |
iha tu lihate rātrītarṣaṃ rathāṅgavihaṃgamā madhu nijavadhūvaktrāmbhoje'dhunādharanāmakam ||33||
jagati mithune cakrāveva smarāgamapāragau navamiva mithaḥ saṃbhuñjāte viyujya viyujya yau |
satatamamṛtādevāhārādyudāpadarocakaṃ tadamṛtabhujāṃ bhartā śaṃbhurviṣāṃ bubhuje vibhuḥ ||34||
viśati yuvatityāge rātrīmucaṃ mihikārucaṃ dinamaṇimaṇiṃ tāpe cittānnijācca yiyāsati |
virahataralajjihvā bahvāhvayantyativihvalāmiha sahacarīṃ nāmagrāhaṃ rathāṅgavihaṃgamāḥ ||35||
svamukulamayairnetrairandhaṃbhaviṣṇutayā janaḥ kimu kumudinīṃ durvyācaṣṭe raveranavekṣikām |
likhitapaṭhitā rājño dārāḥ kavipratibhāsu ye śṛṇuta śṛṇutāsūryaṃpaśyā na sā kila bhāvinī ||36||
culukitatamaḥsindhorbhṛṅgaiḥ karādiva śubhyate nabhasi bisinībandhorandhracyutairudavindubhiḥ |
śatadalamadhusrotaḥkacchadvayīparirambhaṇādanupadamadaḥpaṅkāśaṅkāmamī mama tanvate ||37||
ghusṛṇasumanaḥśṛeṇīśrīṇāmanādaribhiḥ saraḥparisaracarairbhāsāṃ bhartuḥ kumārataraiḥ karaiḥ |
ajani jalajāmodānandotpatiṣṇumadhuvratā baliśabalanādguñjāpuñjaśriyaṃ hṛgayālubhiḥ ||38||
racayati ruciḥ śoṇīmetāṃ kumāritarā raveryadalipaṭalī nīlīkartuṃ vyavasyati pātukā |
ajani sarasī kalmāṣī taddhruvaṃ dhavalasphuṭatkavalakalikāṣaṇḍaiḥ pāṇḍūkṛtodaramaṇḍalā ||39||
kamalakuśalādhāne bhānoraho puruṣāvrataṃ yadupakurute netrāṇi śrīgṛhatvavivakṣubhiḥ |
kavibhirupamānādapyambhojatāṃ gamitānyasāvapi yadatathābhāvānmuñcatyulūkavilocane ||40||
yadatimahatī bhaktirbhānau tadenamuditvaraṃ tvaritamupatiṣṭhasvādhvanya tvamadhvarapaddhateḥ |
iha hi samaye mandeheṣu vrajantyudavajratāmabhi ravimupasthānotkṣiptā jalāñjalayaḥ kila ||41||
udayaśikhariprasthāvasthāyinī khanirakṣāyā śiśutaramahomāṇikyānāmaharmaṇimaṇḍalī |
rajanidṛṣadaṃ dhvāntaśyāmāṃ vidhūya pidhāyikāṃ na khalu katameneyaṃ jāne janena vimudritā ||42||
suraparivṛḍhaḥ karṇātpratyagrahītkila kuṇḍaladvayamatha khalu prācyai prādānmudā sa hi tatpatiḥ |
vidhurudayabhāgekaṃ tatra vyaloki vilokyate navatarakarasvarṇasrāvi dvitīyamaharmaṇiḥ ||43||
dahanamaviśaddīptiryāstaṃ gate gatavāsarapraśamasamayaprāpte patyau vivasvati rāgiṇi |
adharabhuvanātsoddhṛtyaiṣā haṭhāttaraṇeḥ kṛtāmarapatipuraprāptirdhatte satīvratamūrtitām ||44||
badhujanakathā tathyaiveyaṃ tanau tanujanmanaḥ pitṛśitiharidvarṇādyāhārajaḥ kila kālimā |
śamanayamunākroḍaiḥ kālairitastamasāṃ pibādapi yadamalacchāyātkāyādabhūyata bhāsvataḥ ||45||
abhajata cirābhyāsaṃ devaḥ pratikṣaṇadātyaye dinamayamayaṃ kālaṃ bhūyaḥ prasūya tathā raviḥ |
na khalu śakitā śīlaṃ kālaprasūtirasau purā yamayamunayorjanmādhāneṣyanena yathojjhitum ||46||
ruciracaraṇaḥ sūtoruśrīsanātharathaḥ śaniṃ śamanamapi sa trātuṃ lokānasūta sutāviti |
rathapadakṛpāsindhurbandhurdṛśāmapi durjanairyadupahasito bhāsvānnāsmānhasiṣyati kaḥ khalaḥ ||47||
śiśirajarujāṃ gharmaṃ śarmodayāya tanūbhṛtāmatha kharakaraśyānāsyānāṃ prayacchati yaḥ payaḥ |
